# LP P&L and Where LVR Goes Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Measure who makes money providing liquidity to the Uniswap V3 USDC/WETH 0.05% pool in the
window: exact LP fees vs measured and theoretical LVR, per-position P&L (window and lifetime), and
where the LVR goes (searcher / gas / builder / proposer), behind `uv run ulpe lp`.

**Architecture:**
- **Swap engine:** `v3swap.py` gains V3's exact-output path, per-step records and a three-mode
  bit-exact reproduction of every logged swap.
- **Accumulators:** `lp/state.py` adds the contract's fee-growth accounting (exact Q128 integers)
  and parallel float accumulators (USD fees, LVR at 0 s and 12 s) on top of the existing `TickBook`.
- **Positions:** `lp/positions.py` rebuilds every position from the pool's full history (one paid
  BigQuery query, `data/history.py`). `lp/ledger.py` keeps a contract mirror (checked against the
  chain) and a separate attribution layer used for the analysis.
- **Analysis:** `lp/engine.py` replays the window. `lp/pnl.py`, `lp/theory.py`, `lp/lifetime.py`
  and `lp/destination.py` compute results; `lp/checks.py` gates them (L1–L7);
  `lp/analyze.py` writes `results/`.
- **Milestones:** each ends with a real-data run (controller tasks), so the engine is proven against
  the chain before the paid query runs.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13, uv, polars, numpy, matplotlib (Agg), httpx, google-cloud-bigquery,
pytest, ruff, mypy --strict. No new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-lp-pnl-lvr-design.md`. Read it before starting. The
spec is the binding authority; the rulings below amend it where the plan had to decide.

## Global Constraints

- Python 3.13 via uv. Run everything as `uv run <cmd>` from the repo root.
- **No new runtime or dev dependencies.**
- Pool `0x88e6a0c2ddd26feeb64f039a2c41296fcb3f5640`: token0 USDC (6 decimals), token1 WETH
  (18 decimals), fee 500 pips, tick spacing 10. NonfungiblePositionManager (NPM)
  `0xc36442b4a4522e871399cd717abdd847ab11fe88`. Pool created 2021-05-04.
- Window `[2026-07-01, 2026-10-01)` UTC, blocks 25,433,939–26,093,737; snapshot block 25,433,938.
- **Exact integers:** pool state, liquidity and token amounts are Python `int` / `pl.Int128`
  (stored as `Decimal(38, 0)` via `io.write_exact`/`io.read_exact`). **Fee-growth values and
  `feeGrowthInside*Last` are uint256 and are stored as 64-char lowercase hex strings** (they do not fit
  Int128). Floats only for USD, prices, bp and LVR.
- uint256 arithmetic wraps: every fee-growth subtraction/addition is `% 2**256`; tokens owed
  accrue as `uint128(...)`, i.e. `% 2**128`.
- LVR horizons `(0, 12)` seconds; 0 s is primary. `γ = ln(1 + 0.0005)`, `λ = 1/12 s⁻¹`.
- Bootstrap: 1,000 resamples, seed 42 (positions for group tables, UTC days for pool-level daily).
- Range-width buckets (bp): `<50`, `50-200`, `200-1000`, `1000-5000`, `>=5000`. Holding buckets in
  window: `same_block`, `<1h`, `1h-1d`, `1d-7d`, `>=7d`, `whole_window`. Lifetime holding buckets:
  `<1d`, `1-7d`, `7-30d`, `30-180d`, `>=180d`. LP types: `jit`, `lp_vault`, `nft_wallet`,
  `nft_contract`, `direct_other`.
- **Never print, log or put `ETH_RPC_URL` into an exception message** (it holds the API key). Never
  read `.env`.
- **The paid history query (`ulpe fetch-history` stage 1) runs only after the user confirms in chat**
  (Task 17), always behind `--confirm-cost`, with `maximum_bytes_billed` = 3.1 TB.
- Tests that touch the network are marked `@pytest.mark.live` (skipped by default).
- Before every commit run `uv run ruff format .`; then `uv run ruff check .`, `uv run ruff format
  --check .`, `uv run mypy src`, `uv run pytest -q` must all pass. Code blocks here are the content to
  write; ruff may re-wrap them.
- **Commit messages carry no `Co-Authored-By` or other attribution trailer** (user instruction).
  Push after every commit with `git push` (pre-approved for this private repo).
- Matplotlib uses the Agg backend.
- **Real data** lives in the main checkout's `data/`. In a worktree the controller sets `DATA_DIR` in
  the worktree's `.env` to that folder before Task 3. Steps that need real data say so; only the
  controller tasks (3, 7, 12, 17, 22, 25) touch it.
- The user dislikes the word "toxicity": never use it in names, docs or output.
- The destination limitation text (spec section 7) is used verbatim; it lives in
  `lp.destination.LIMITATION_TEXT` (Task 24).

## Rulings (plan decisions that amend or pin the spec)

1. **P2 (exact fees) moves from M1 to M2 (Task 6):** exact per-step fees need the three-mode swap
   reproduction (Tasks 4–5). M1 keeps P1, P3, P4.
2. **uint256 values on disk are hex strings** (Global Constraints). `snapshot_fees`, `sample_fees`,
   `start_positions`, `end_positions` store them as 64-char hex.
3. **Float accumulators start at zero.** At the snapshot, `fee_usd`/LVR globals and every tick's
   outside values are 0. Only differences between checkpoints are used, and "zero accrued on either
   side since the snapshot" is a valid starting state for the outside convention.
4. **D5 is aggregated in BigQuery.** The window has ~110 M transactions, too many to download.
   `fetch-builders` writes three aggregated tables: `block_revenue.parquet` (per block: priority
   fees excluding the fee recipient's own transactions, successful trace payments to the fee
   recipient, and the block's last transaction), `pool_tx_direct_payments.parquet` (per transaction
   touching our pool: trace payments to the fee recipient) and `pool_tx_other_logs.parquet` (per
   transaction touching our pool: logs from addresses other than pool/USDC/WETH/the tx target). The
   last replaces the spec's `searcher_tx_other_logs.parquet` and covers every pool transaction.
5. **Time-weighted position value is sampled hourly** (UTC hour starts in the window). A position
   with no grid point while open uses its value at its first in-window deposit.
6. **Owner code** comes from one `eth_getCode` call per address (Multicall3 cannot read code),
   cached in `data/raw/chain_state/owner_code.parquet`.
7. **The engine has its own merged row stream** (`lp.engine.EngineRow`) for pool and NPM events,
   because `replay.ReplayRow` has no owner or token fields. `replay.py` is unchanged.
8. **`ulpe lp` reuses sub-project 2 outputs:** it requires `data/processed/aligned.parquet`,
   `blocks_state.parquet` and `swap_fees.parquet` (from `ulpe analyze`) and takes `P_cex`, flow
   labels and block state from them.
9. **Per-day attribution:** at the first event of each new UTC day the ledger checkpoints the float
   attribution of every open position, giving `position_days.parquet` (regime × LP group tables).
   Raw-token attribution checkpoints only at contract events and the window boundaries, so its
   floors match the contract.
10. **`summary.json`:** `ulpe lp` reads the file `ulpe analyze` wrote and replaces only its `"lp"`
    key.
11. **`labels/lp_addresses.csv`** starts with its header only; Task 22 adds web-verified rows.
12. **`ulpe all`** gains `--confirm-cost` and runs the whole pipeline; without the flag it stops at
    `fetch-history` with a message when the history is missing.
13. **L6 fee tolerance.** Position raw fees floor at every checkpoint, not only per step, so Σ
    position fees may fall short of the pool's LP fees by at most one raw unit per accrued step,
    plus one per window flow, plus two per account; they can never exceed them (spec L6 said "per
    step").
14. **NPM events read the pool key's stored fee growth**, never the ticks (the pool `Burn` just
    before an NPM `DecreaseLiquidity` may have cleared them), exactly as the position manager reads
    `pool.positions(key)`.

## Review Focus

1. **A swap step with zero active liquidity.** The contract takes the fee (and the protocol cut) but
   updates no fee growth; the engine must do the same and count such steps. Test: Task 10
   `test_add_with_zero_liquidity_accrues_nothing`.
2. **An NPM `Collect` on a token with zero liquidity.** NPM does not poke the pool then, so no fees
   accrue; owed only decreases. Test: Task 16 `test_npm_collect_without_liquidity_does_not_accrue`.
3. **A direct position key reused after its liquidity returned to zero.** It must become a new
   position, with later collects of the old owed going to the old one until the next mint. Test:
   Task 14 `test_direct_key_reuse_starts_a_new_position`.
4. **A tick cleared by a burn and re-initialized later in the window.** Its outside values must be
   re-initialized from the current tick, not reused. Test: Task 10
   `test_reinitialized_tick_gets_fresh_outside_values`.
5. **A position whose only window event is a collect** (liquidity 0 throughout the window) is not
   window-active and must not appear in P&L tables. Test: Task 18
   `test_collect_only_position_is_not_window_active`.

Among Binance trades sharing a microsecond, the reference must be the latest one (highest aggregated
trade ID), not an arbitrary one.

`align.py`:

```python
def reference_table(trades: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """ETHUSDC trades as a (ts_us, price) reference. Among trades sharing a microsecond, the one
    with the highest Binance aggregated-trade ID (the latest) is the price in force."""
    return (
        trades.sort("ts_us", "agg_trade_id")
        .unique(subset="ts_us", keep="last", maintain_order=True)
        .select("ts_us", "price")
    )
```

## Milestone M2 — swap engine and exact fees

### Task 4: Exact-output path and per-step records (`v3swap.py`)

Port V3's exact-output branch of `computeSwapStep` and `getNextSqrtPriceFromOutput`, and make the
swap loop handle both signs of `amountSpecified` while recording every step.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/v3swap.py`
- Test: `tests/test_v3swap.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `next_sqrt_price_from_output(sqrt_price: int, liquidity: int, amount_out: int, zero_for_one: bool) -> int`
  - `compute_swap_step(sqrt_current, sqrt_target, liquidity, amount_remaining, fee_pips) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]`
    — `(sqrt_next, amount_in, amount_out, fee)`; `amount_remaining > 0` exact input, `< 0` exact output.
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) SwapStep(sqrt_start: int, sqrt_end: int, liquidity: int, amount_in: int, amount_out: int, fee: int, crossed_tick: int | None)`
    — `amount_in` excludes the fee; `crossed_tick` is the initialized tick crossed at the end of the step.
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) SwapRun(amount_in: int, amount_out: int, fee: int, sqrt_price_x96: int, tick: int, liquidity: int, steps: tuple[SwapStep, ...])`
    — `amount_in` includes fees.
  - `swap(book, zero_for_one, amount_specified, sqrt_price_limit_x96=None, fee_pips=FEE_PIPS, tick_spacing=TICK_SPACING) -> SwapRun`
  - `swap_exact_input(...) -> SwapResult` unchanged in behaviour (now a wrapper over `swap`).

```python
def encode_price_sqrt(reserve1: int, reserve0: int) -> int:
    """v3-core's encodePriceSqrt: floor(sqrt(reserve1 / reserve0) * 2^96)."""
    return math.isqrt((reserve1 << 192) // reserve0)


E18 = 10**18
SQRT_P = 20282409603651670423947251286016


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("price", "target", "liquidity", "amount", "fee", "expected"),
    [
        # exact amount in that gets capped at price target in one for zero

            encode_price_sqrt(1, 1),
            encode_price_sqrt(101, 100),
            2 * E18,
            E18,
            600,
            (encode_price_sqrt(101, 100), 9975124224178055, 9925619580021728, 5988667735148),
        ),
        # exact amount out that gets capped at price target in one for zero
        (
            encode_price_sqrt(1, 1),
            encode_price_sqrt(101, 100),
            2 * E18,
            -E18,
            600,
            (encode_price_sqrt(101, 100), 9975124224178055, 9925619580021728, 5988667735148),

        # amount out is capped at the desired amount out

            417332158212080721273783715441582,

            -1,
            1,

        # intermediate insufficient liquidity, exact output, price moving up
        (SQRT_P, SQRT_P * 11 // 10, 1024, -4, 3000, (SQRT_P * 11 // 10, 26215, 0, 79)),
        # intermediate insufficient liquidity, exact output, price moving down

) -> None:

        encode_price_sqrt(1, 1), target, 2 * E18, E18, 600
    )
```

After `next_sqrt_price_from_input` add:

```python
def next_sqrt_price_from_output(
    sqrt_price: int, liquidity: int, amount_out: int, zero_for_one: bool
) -> int:
    """SqrtPriceMath.getNextSqrtPriceFromOutput."""
    if liquidity <= 0:
        raise ValueError("liquidity must be positive")
    if amount_out == 0:
        return sqrt_price
    if zero_for_one:  # getNextSqrtPriceFromAmount1RoundingDown(add = false)
        quotient = div_rounding_up(amount_out << 96, liquidity)
        if sqrt_price <= quotient:
            raise ValueError("output exceeds what the pool holds in this range")
        return sqrt_price - quotient
    # getNextSqrtPriceFromAmount0RoundingUp(add = false)
    numerator1 = liquidity << 96
    product = amount_out * sqrt_price
    if product >= _U256 or numerator1 <= product:
        raise ValueError("output exceeds what the pool holds in this range")
    return mul_div_rounding_up(numerator1, sqrt_price, numerator1 - product)
```

Replace `compute_swap_step` with:

```python
def compute_swap_step(
    sqrt_current: int, sqrt_target: int, liquidity: int, amount_remaining: int, fee_pips: int
) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
    """SwapMath.computeSwapStep: (sqrt_next, amount_in, amount_out, fee).

    `amount_remaining > 0` is an exact input, `< 0` an exact output (as in V3).
    """
    zero_for_one = sqrt_current >= sqrt_target
    exact_in = amount_remaining >= 0
    amount_in = amount_out = 0
    if exact_in:
        remaining_less_fee = mul_div(amount_remaining, _PIPS - fee_pips, _PIPS)
        if zero_for_one:
            amount_in = get_amount0_delta(sqrt_target, sqrt_current, liquidity, True)
        else:
            amount_in = get_amount1_delta(sqrt_current, sqrt_target, liquidity, True)
        if remaining_less_fee >= amount_in:
            sqrt_next = sqrt_target
        else:
            sqrt_next = next_sqrt_price_from_input(
                sqrt_current, liquidity, remaining_less_fee, zero_for_one
            )
    else:
        if zero_for_one:
            amount_out = get_amount1_delta(sqrt_target, sqrt_current, liquidity, False)
        else:
            amount_out = get_amount0_delta(sqrt_current, sqrt_target, liquidity, False)
        if -amount_remaining >= amount_out:
            sqrt_next = sqrt_target
        else:
            sqrt_next = next_sqrt_price_from_output(
                sqrt_current, liquidity, -amount_remaining, zero_for_one
            )
    reached = sqrt_next == sqrt_target
    if zero_for_one:
        if not (reached and exact_in):
            amount_in = get_amount0_delta(sqrt_next, sqrt_current, liquidity, True)
        if not (reached and not exact_in):
            amount_out = get_amount1_delta(sqrt_next, sqrt_current, liquidity, False)
    else:
        if not (reached and exact_in):
            amount_in = get_amount1_delta(sqrt_current, sqrt_next, liquidity, True)
        if not (reached and not exact_in):
            amount_out = get_amount0_delta(sqrt_current, sqrt_next, liquidity, False)
    if not exact_in and amount_out > -amount_remaining:  # cap at the requested output
        amount_out = -amount_remaining
    if exact_in and not reached:
        fee = amount_remaining - amount_in
    else:
        fee = mul_div_rounding_up(amount_in, fee_pips, _PIPS - fee_pips)
    return sqrt_next, amount_in, amount_out, fee
```

After `SwapResult`, add the step and run types and the general loop, and turn `swap_exact_input`
into a wrapper:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SwapStep:
    sqrt_start: int
    sqrt_end: int
    liquidity: int  # active liquidity during the step
    amount_in: int  # input token, fee excluded
    amount_out: int
    fee: int  # input-token fee of this step (protocol cut included)
    crossed_tick: int | None  # initialized tick crossed at the end of the step


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SwapRun:
    amount_in: int  # input token consumed, fees included
    amount_out: int
    fee: int
    sqrt_price_x96: int
    tick: int
    liquidity: int
    steps: tuple[SwapStep, ...]


def swap(
    book: TickBook,
    zero_for_one: bool,
    amount_specified: int,
    sqrt_price_limit_x96: int | None = None,
    fee_pips: int = FEE_PIPS,
    tick_spacing: int = TICK_SPACING,
) -> SwapRun:
    """UniswapV3Pool.swap from the book's current state (not mutated).

    `amount_specified > 0` is an exact input, `< 0` an exact output, as in V3.
    """
    if amount_specified == 0:
        raise ValueError("amount_specified must be non-zero")
    exact_in = amount_specified > 0
    sqrt_price, tick, liquidity = book.sqrt_price_x96, book.tick, book.liquidity
    if sqrt_price_limit_x96 is None:
        limit = MIN_SQRT_RATIO + 1 if zero_for_one else MAX_SQRT_RATIO - 1
    else:
        limit = sqrt_price_limit_x96
    if zero_for_one:
        valid = MIN_SQRT_RATIO < limit < sqrt_price
    else:
        valid = sqrt_price < limit < MAX_SQRT_RATIO
    if not valid:
        raise ValueError("sqrt_price_limit_x96 is on the wrong side of the current price (SPL)")

    remaining = amount_specified
    total_in = total_out = fees = 0
    steps: list[SwapStep] = []
    while remaining != 0 and sqrt_price != limit:
        start, step_liquidity = sqrt_price, liquidity
        tick_next, initialized = next_initialized_tick_within_one_word(
            book, tick, zero_for_one, tick_spacing
        )
        tick_next = max(MIN_TICK, min(MAX_TICK, tick_next))
        sqrt_at_next = get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick(tick_next)
        if zero_for_one:
            target = limit if sqrt_at_next < limit else sqrt_at_next
        else:
            target = limit if sqrt_at_next > limit else sqrt_at_next
        sqrt_price, step_in, step_out, step_fee = compute_swap_step(
            sqrt_price, target, liquidity, remaining, fee_pips
        )
        if exact_in:
            remaining -= step_in + step_fee
        else:
            remaining += step_out
        total_in += step_in + step_fee
        total_out += step_out
        fees += step_fee
        crossed: int | None = None
        if sqrt_price == sqrt_at_next:
            if initialized:
                net = book.liquidity_net[tick_next]
                liquidity += -net if zero_for_one else net
                if liquidity < 0:
                    raise ValueError(f"liquidity went negative crossing tick {tick_next}")
                crossed = tick_next
            tick = tick_next - 1 if zero_for_one else tick_next
        elif sqrt_price != start:
            tick = get_tick_at_sqrt_ratio(sqrt_price)
        steps.append(
            SwapStep(start, sqrt_price, step_liquidity, step_in, step_out, step_fee, crossed)
        )
    return SwapRun(total_in, total_out, fees, sqrt_price, tick, liquidity, tuple(steps))


def swap_exact_input(
    book: TickBook,
    zero_for_one: bool,
    amount_in: int,
    sqrt_price_limit_x96: int | None = None,
    fee_pips: int = FEE_PIPS,
    tick_spacing: int = TICK_SPACING,
) -> SwapResult:
    """UniswapV3Pool.swap for an exact input, from the book's current state (not mutated)."""
    if amount_in <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"amount_in must be positive, got {amount_in}")
    r = swap(book, zero_for_one, amount_in, sqrt_price_limit_x96, fee_pips, tick_spacing)
    return SwapResult(r.amount_in, r.amount_out, r.fee, r.sqrt_price_x96, r.tick, r.liquidity)
```

Delete the old body of `swap_exact_input` (the loop now lives in `swap`).

```python
def logged(r: SwapRun, zero_for_one: bool) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """(amount0, amount1, sqrt) as the pool would log the run."""
    if zero_for_one:
        return r.amount_in, -r.amount_out, r.sqrt_price_x96
    return -r.amount_out, r.amount_in, r.sqrt_price_x96


def test_reproduce_exact_input_swap() -> None:
    b = two_range_book()
    a0, a1, s = logged(swap(b, True, 10**15), True)
    rep = reproduce_swap(b, a0, a1, s)
    assert rep is not None and rep.mode == "exact_input" and rep.zero_for_one
    assert rep.run is not None and rep.run.sqrt_price_x96 == s


def test_reproduce_finds_exact_output_swaps_that_exact_input_misses() -> None:
    b = two_range_book()
    found = 0
    for want in range(1_000, 400_000, 397):
        a0, a1, s = logged(swap(b, False, -want), False)
        if reproduce_swap(b, a0, a1, s, modes=("exact_input",)) is not None:
            continue
        rep = reproduce_swap(b, a0, a1, s)
        assert rep is not None and rep.mode == "exact_output", want
        found += 1
    assert found > 0  # the search must hit at least one rounding case


def test_reproduce_to_price_mode() -> None:
    b = two_range_book()
    limit = sqrt_at(-5)
    a0, a1, s = logged(swap(b, True, 2**128, limit), True)
    rep = reproduce_swap(b, a0, a1, s, modes=("to_price",))
    assert rep is not None and rep.mode == "to_price" and s == limit


def test_reproduce_returns_none_for_an_impossible_swap() -> None:
    b = two_range_book()
    a0, a1, s = logged(swap(b, True, 10**15), True)
    assert reproduce_swap(b, a0, a1 - 1_000, s) is None


def test_reproduce_no_input_swap() -> None:
    b = two_range_book()
    rep = reproduce_swap(b, 0, 0, b.sqrt_price_x96)
    assert rep == Reproduction("no_input", False, None)
    assert reproduce_swap(b, 0, 0, b.sqrt_price_x96 + 1) is None
```

Create `tests/test_swapfees.py`:

```python
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l1
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.swapfees import build_swap_fees, protocol_cut
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import swap

L = 10**18
SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "log_index": pl.Int64,
    "event": pl.String,
    "tick": pl.Int64,
    "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128,
    "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "fee_protocol0_new": pl.Int64,
    "fee_protocol1_new": pl.Int64,
}


def book() -> TickBook:
    b = TickBook(tick=0, sqrt_price_x96=sqrt_at(0), liquidity=0)
    b.apply_mint(-10, 10, L)
    b.apply_mint(10, 20, 2 * L)
    return b


def events(tamper: bool = False) -> pl.DataFrame:
    r = swap(book(), False, 10**15)  # WETH in: token1 fee, crosses nothing
    return pl.DataFrame(
        [
            {"block_number": 1, "log_index": 0, "event": "set_fee_protocol",
             "fee_protocol0_new": 4, "fee_protocol1_new": 4},
            {"block_number": 1, "log_index": 1, "event": "swap", "tick": r.tick,
             "sqrt_price_x96": r.sqrt_price_x96, "liquidity": r.liquidity,
             "amount0": -r.amount_out + (1 if tamper else 0), "amount1": r.amount_in},
        ],
        schema=SCHEMA,
    )


def test_protocol_cut_reads_the_input_tokens_nibble() -> None:
    assert protocol_cut(1001, 4 + (5 << 4), zero_for_one=True) == 250
    assert protocol_cut(1001, 4 + (5 << 4), zero_for_one=False) == 200
    assert protocol_cut(1001, 0, zero_for_one=True) == 0


def test_build_swap_fees_takes_the_protocol_cut_per_step() -> None:
    run = swap(book(), False, 10**15)
    fees = build_swap_fees(events(), book(), 0)
    row = fees.row(0, named=True)
    assert row["mode"] == "exact_input" and row["fee_token"] == "WETH"
    assert row["fee_raw"] == run.fee
    assert row["protocol_fee_raw"] == sum(s.fee // 4 for s in run.steps)
    assert row["lp_fee_raw"] == run.fee - row["protocol_fee_raw"]
    assert row["steps"] == len(run.steps)


def test_l1_fails_on_an_unreproducible_swap() -> None:
    assert check_l1(build_swap_fees(events(), book(), 0)).passed
    bad = check_l1(build_swap_fees(events(tamper=True), book(), 0))
    assert not bad.passed
    assert bad.summary["unreproduced"] == 1
    assert bad.mismatches[0]["block"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `v3swap.py`, move `_HUGE = 2**128` up to just below `A1_OUT_INPUT_UNITS` (it is used by both
`reproduce_swap` and `arb_to_price`), add `from collections.abc import Callable` to the imports, and
after `swap_check_ok` add:

```python
REPRODUCTION_MODES = ("exact_input", "exact_output", "to_price")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Reproduction:
    mode: str  # one of REPRODUCTION_MODES, or "no_input" (then run is None)
    zero_for_one: bool
    run: SwapRun | None


def reproduce_swap(
    book: TickBook,
    amount0: int,
    amount1: int,
    sqrt_price_x96: int,
    fee_pips: int = FEE_PIPS,
    modes: tuple[str, ...] = REPRODUCTION_MODES,
) -> Reproduction | None:
    """Re-run a logged swap from `book` (its pre-swap state) in the first mode that reproduces its
    logged amounts and final price bit-exactly (spec section 5); None when no mode does.

    exact_input: the logged input as an exact input. exact_output: the logged output as an exact
    output. to_price: an unlimited exact input stopped by the logged final price as the limit
    (swaps that hit their price limit).
    """
    if amount0 > 0:
        zero_for_one, amount_in, amount_out = True, amount0, -amount1
    elif amount1 > 0:
        zero_for_one, amount_in, amount_out = False, amount1, -amount0
    else:
        if sqrt_price_x96 == book.sqrt_price_x96:
            return Reproduction("no_input", False, None)
        return None

    attempts: dict[str, Callable[[], SwapRun]] = {
        "exact_input": lambda: swap(book, zero_for_one, amount_in, fee_pips=fee_pips),
        "exact_output": lambda: swap(book, zero_for_one, -amount_out, fee_pips=fee_pips),
        "to_price": lambda: swap(book, zero_for_one, _HUGE, sqrt_price_x96, fee_pips),
    }
    for mode in modes:
        if mode == "exact_output" and amount_out <= 0:
            continue
        try:
            r = attempts[mode]()
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if (
            r.amount_in == amount_in
            and r.amount_out == amount_out
            and r.sqrt_price_x96 == sqrt_price_x96
        ):
            return Reproduction(mode, zero_for_one, r)
    return None
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/swapfees.py`:

```python
"""Exact per-swap fees from the bit-exact re-simulation (spec P2).

The fee of each step is the contract's; the protocol takes `fee // feeProtocol` of each step, from
the input token's nibble of slot0.feeProtocol, exactly as UniswapV3Pool.swap does.
"""

from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.replay import apply_event, iter_event_rows, required
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import reproduce_swap

SWAP_FEES_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "log_index": pl.Int64,
    "mode": pl.String,
    "fee_token": pl.String,
    "fee_raw": pl.Int128,
    "protocol_fee_raw": pl.Int128,
    "lp_fee_raw": pl.Int128,
    "steps": pl.Int64,
}


def protocol_cut(fee: int, fee_protocol: int, zero_for_one: bool) -> int:
    """The protocol's share of one step's fee (UniswapV3Pool.swap: feeAmount / feeProtocol)."""
    denominator = fee_protocol % 16 if zero_for_one else fee_protocol >> 4
    return fee // denominator if denominator else 0


def build_swap_fees(events: pl.DataFrame, book: TickBook, fee_protocol: int) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """One row per swap in `events`; `book` (the start state) is advanced in place."""
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for row in iter_event_rows(events):
        if row.event == "swap":
            rep = reproduce_swap(
                book,
                required(row, "amount0"),
                required(row, "amount1"),
                required(row, "sqrt_price_x96"),
            )
            base = {"block_number": row.block_number, "log_index": row.log_index}
            if rep is None:
                rows.append({**base, "mode": None, "steps": 0})
            elif rep.run is None:
                rows.append(
                    {**base, "mode": rep.mode, "fee_raw": 0, "protocol_fee_raw": 0,
                     "lp_fee_raw": 0, "steps": 0}
                )
            else:
                cut = sum(protocol_cut(s.fee, fee_protocol, rep.zero_for_one) for s in rep.run.steps)
                rows.append(
                    {
                        **base,
                        "mode": rep.mode,
                        "fee_token": "USDC" if rep.zero_for_one else "WETH",
                        "fee_raw": rep.run.fee,
                        "protocol_fee_raw": cut,
                        "lp_fee_raw": rep.run.fee - cut,
                        "steps": len(rep.run.steps),
                    }
                )
        fee_protocol = apply_event(book, row, fee_protocol)
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=SWAP_FEES_SCHEMA)
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/checks.py`:

```python
"""Checks L1-L7 (gated) and the reported checks of sub-project 3 (spec section 9)."""

from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.validate import MAX_LISTED, CheckResult


def check_l1(swap_fees: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    """Every swap re-simulated bit-exactly in one of the reproduction modes."""
    modes = swap_fees.group_by("mode").len().drop_nulls("mode")
    counts: dict[str, Any] = dict(zip(modes["mode"].to_list(), modes["len"].to_list(), strict=True))
    bad = swap_fees.filter(pl.col("mode").is_null())
    listed = [
        {"block": b, "log_index": li}
        for b, li in bad.select("block_number", "log_index").head(MAX_LISTED).iter_rows()
    ]
    return CheckResult(
        "L1 every swap reproduced bit-exactly",
        bad.height == 0 and swap_fees.height > 0,
        {"swaps": swap_fees.height, "unreproduced": bad.height, "by_mode": counts},
        listed,
    )
```

```python
def build_aligned(
    labelled: pl.DataFrame,
    ref: pl.DataFrame,
    end_us: int,
    inp: Inputs,
    swap_fees: pl.DataFrame,
    shift_s: float = 0.0,

    a = markouts.add_markouts(a)
    scope = arbprofit.tx_scope(inp.other_logs)
    return arbprofit.add_costs(a, inp.blocks, inp.transfers, scope, inp.bots)
```

```python
U256 = 2**256


def u256_hex(value: int) -> str:
    """A uint256 (wrapped modulo 2^256) as 64 lowercase hex chars, for storing on disk."""
    return f"{value % U256:064x}"


def hex_u256(text: str) -> int:
    return int(text, 16)
```

`rpc.py` (after `_TICKS`):

```python
FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL0_CALL = abi.selector("feeGrowthGlobal0X128()")
FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL1_CALL = abi.selector("feeGrowthGlobal1X128()")
_POSITIONS = abi.selector("positions(bytes32)")
_NPM_POSITIONS = abi.selector("positions(uint256)")
```

`RpcClient` gains:

```python
    def get_code(self, address: str, block: int) -> str:
        result: str = self.request("eth_getCode", [address, hex(block)])

```

and at the end of the module:

```python
def decode_tick_fees(result: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """feeGrowthOutside0X128 and feeGrowthOutside1X128 from a ticks(int24) result."""
    w = abi.words(result)
    return abi.to_uint(w[2]), abi.to_uint(w[3])


def position_key(owner: str, tick_lower: int, tick_upper: int) -> str:
    """PositionKey.compute: keccak256(abi.encodePacked(owner, int24 tickLower, int24 tickUpper))."""
    packed = (
        bytes.fromhex(owner[2:])
        + (tick_lower % 2**24).to_bytes(3, "big")
        + (tick_upper % 2**24).to_bytes(3, "big")
    )
    return "0x" + abi.keccak256(packed).hex()


def pool_positions_call(key: str) -> str:
    return _POSITIONS + key[2:]


def decode_pool_position(result: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    w = abi.words(result)
    return {
        "liquidity": abi.to_uint(w[0]),
        "fee_growth_inside0_last": abi.to_uint(w[1]),
        "fee_growth_inside1_last": abi.to_uint(w[2]),
        "tokens_owed0": abi.to_uint(w[3]),
        "tokens_owed1": abi.to_uint(w[4]),
    }


def npm_positions_call(token_id: int) -> str:
    return _NPM_POSITIONS + abi.enc_uint(token_id)


def decode_npm_position(result: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """NonfungiblePositionManager.positions(tokenId), without nonce and operator."""
    w = abi.words(result)
    return {
        "token0": abi.to_addr(w[2]),
        "token1": abi.to_addr(w[3]),
        "fee": abi.to_uint(w[4]),
        "tick_lower": abi.to_int(w[5]),
        "tick_upper": abi.to_int(w[6]),
        "liquidity": abi.to_uint(w[7]),
        "fee_growth_inside0_last": abi.to_uint(w[8]),
        "fee_growth_inside1_last": abi.to_uint(w[9]),
        "tokens_owed0": abi.to_uint(w[10]),
        "tokens_owed1": abi.to_uint(w[11]),
    }
```

`snapshot.py`: rename `_batched` to `batched` (update its three callers in the module) and give it
the flag:

```python
def batched(
    client: RpcClient, calls: list[tuple[str, str]], block: int, allow_failure: bool = False
) -> list[str | None]:
    """Multicall in batches of BATCH. A failed sub-call raises, unless allow_failure (then None)."""
    out: list[str | None] = []
    for i in range(0, len(calls), BATCH):
        for result in client.multicall(calls[i : i + BATCH], block):
            if result is None and not allow_failure:
                raise RuntimeError(f"multicall sub-call failed at block {block}")
            out.append(result)
    return out
```

The existing callers pass results into decoders typed `str`; add `assert r is not None` where mypy
needs it, or keep a tiny private wrapper `_strict(...) -> list[str]` in `snapshot.py` that calls
`batched` and narrows the type.

  - `chain_state.fetch_sample_fees(client, raw_dir: Path, sample_ticks: pl.DataFrame) -> Path`
    (`sample_ticks` has `block, tick`; every sample block appears at least once)
  - CLI `ulpe fetch-chain-state` (fee part; the position part is added in Task 15)

```python
from pathlib import Path

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics import abi
from uniswap_lp_economics.data import chain_state
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import hex_u256
from uniswap_lp_economics.rpc import FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL0_CALL, FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL1_CALL


def words(values: list[int]) -> str:
    return "0x" + "".join(abi.enc_int(v) for v in values)


class FakePool:
    """Answers the pool calls chain_state makes, from fixed per-tick fee values."""

    def __init__(self, uninitialized: set[int] | None = None) -> None:
        self.uninitialized = uninitialized or set()
        self.blocks: list[int] = []

    def multicall(self, calls: list[tuple[str, str]], block: int) -> list[str | None]:
        self.blocks.append(block)
        out: list[str | None] = []
        for _, data in calls:
            if data == FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL0_CALL:
                out.append(words([2**255 + block]))
            elif data == FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL1_CALL:
                out.append(words([7]))
            else:  # ticks(int24)
                t = abi.to_int(data[10:])
                init = 0 if t in self.uninitialized else 1
                out.append(words([1, 1, 1000 + t, 2000 + t, 0, 0, 0, init]))
        return out


def test_read_fee_state_rows_are_hex_and_complete() -> None:
    df = chain_state.read_fee_state(FakePool(), 5, [-10, 20])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    g = df.filter(pl.col("kind") == "global").row(0, named=True)
    assert hex_u256(g["fee_growth0"]) == 2**255 + 5 and hex_u256(g["fee_growth1"]) == 7
    ticks = df.filter(pl.col("kind") == "tick").sort("tick")
    assert ticks["tick"].to_list() == [-10, 20]
    assert [hex_u256(x) for x in ticks["fee_growth0"].to_list()] == [990, 1020]
    assert df["block"].unique().to_list() == [5]


def test_read_fee_state_rejects_an_uninitialized_tick() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="tick 20 is not initialized at block 5"):
        chain_state.read_fee_state(FakePool({20}), 5, [-10, 20])  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_fetch_sample_fees_reads_each_block_once(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    sample_ticks = pl.DataFrame({"block": [8, 8, 9], "tick": [0, 10, -10]})
    pool = FakePool()
    path = chain_state.fetch_sample_fees(pool, tmp_path, sample_ticks)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    df = pl.read_parquet(path)
    assert sorted(set(pool.blocks)) == [8, 9]
    assert df.filter(pl.col("kind") == "global").height == 2
    assert df.filter(pl.col("block") == 8).filter(pl.col("kind") == "tick")["tick"].to_list() == [0, 10]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/chain_state.py`:

```python
"""On-chain pool state for sub-project 3 (spec D2-D4), read with Multicall3.

uint256 values (fee growth) are stored as 64-char hex strings: they do not fit Int128.
"""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import POOL_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import u256_hex, write_exact
from uniswap_lp_economics.rpc import (
    FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL0_CALL,
    FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL1_CALL,
    RpcClient,
    decode_tick_fees,
    decode_ticks,
    decode_uint,
    ticks_call,
)
from uniswap_lp_economics.snapshot import batched

CHAIN_DIR = "chain_state"
SNAPSHOT_FEES = "snapshot_fees.parquet"
SAMPLE_FEES = "sample_fees.parquet"
FEE_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block": pl.Int64,
    "kind": pl.String,
    "tick": pl.Int64,
    "fee_growth0": pl.String,
    "fee_growth1": pl.String,
}


def read_fee_state(client: RpcClient, block: int, ticks: list[int]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """feeGrowthGlobal0/1X128 and each tick's feeGrowthOutside0/1X128 at `block`."""
    calls = [(POOL_ADDRESS, FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL0_CALL), (POOL_ADDRESS, FEE_GROWTH_GLOBAL1_CALL)]
    calls += [(POOL_ADDRESS, ticks_call(t)) for t in ticks]
    results = batched(client, calls, block)
    g0, g1 = (decode_uint(r) for r in results[:2] if r is not None)
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"block": block, "kind": "global", "tick": None,
         "fee_growth0": u256_hex(g0), "fee_growth1": u256_hex(g1)}
    ]
    for t, result in zip(ticks, results[2:], strict=True):
        assert result is not None
        _, _, initialized = decode_ticks(result)
        if not initialized:
            raise RuntimeError(f"tick {t} is not initialized at block {block}")
        o0, o1 = decode_tick_fees(result)
        rows.append(
            {"block": block, "kind": "tick", "tick": t,
             "fee_growth0": u256_hex(o0), "fee_growth1": u256_hex(o1)}
        )
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=FEE_SCHEMA)


def fetch_snapshot_fees(client: RpcClient, raw_dir: Path, block: int, ticks: list[int]) -> Path:
    out = raw_dir / CHAIN_DIR / SNAPSHOT_FEES
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df = read_fee_state(client, block, sorted(ticks))
    write_exact(df, out)
    print(f"snapshot fee state at block {block}: {len(ticks)} ticks")
    return out


def fetch_sample_fees(client: RpcClient, raw_dir: Path, sample_ticks: pl.DataFrame) -> Path:
    """Fee state at every sample block, for the ticks C3 already sampled there (check L2)."""
    out = raw_dir / CHAIN_DIR / SAMPLE_FEES
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    frames = []
    groups = sample_ticks.partition_by("block", as_dict=True, maintain_order=True)
    for i, ((block,), g) in enumerate(sorted(groups.items())):
        frames.append(read_fee_state(client, int(block), sorted(g["tick"].to_list())))
        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:
            print(f"  {i + 1}/{len(groups)} sample blocks")
    write_exact(pl.concat(frames), out)
    print(f"sample fee state: {len(groups)} blocks")
    return out
```

`cli.py`: import `chain_state` (`from uniswap_lp_economics.data import bigquery, binance,
chain_state, transfers`) and add:

```python
def fetch_chain_state() -> None:
    """D2-D4 (spec 3.1). Fee state now; position state once the history exists (Task 15)."""
    client = _rpc()
    meta = build.load_snapshot_meta(config.RAW_DIR)
    folder = config.RAW_DIR / chain_state.CHAIN_DIR
    if (folder / chain_state.SNAPSHOT_FEES).exists():
        print("snapshot fee state already downloaded, skipping")
    else:
        ticks = read_exact(config.RAW_DIR / "tickbook_snapshot.parquet")["tick"].to_list()
        chain_state.fetch_snapshot_fees(client, config.RAW_DIR, int(meta["block"]), ticks)
    if (folder / chain_state.SAMPLE_FEES).exists():
        print("sample fee state already downloaded, skipping")
    else:
        sample_ticks = read_exact(config.RAW_DIR / "onchain_sample_ticks.parquet")
        chain_state.fetch_sample_fees(client, config.RAW_DIR, sample_ticks.select("block", "tick"))
```

### Task 10: Fee and LVR accumulators (`lp/state.py`)

The contract's fee-growth accounting (exact, uint256) plus float accumulators for USD fees and LVR
at 0 s and 12 s, all with the same global/outside/inside mechanics (spec section 5).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/state.py`
- Test: `tests/test_lp_state.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TickBook`; `SwapRun`/`SwapStep` (Task 4); `swapfees.protocol_cut` (Task 5);
  `io.hex_u256` (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - `Q128`, `U256`, `FLOAT_NAMES = ("fee_usd", "lvr_0", "lvr_12")`, `sub256(a, b) -> int`
  - `step_lvr(zero_for_one: bool, amount_in: int, amount_out: int, price: float) -> float`
  - `@dataclass FeeAccumulators(global0, global1, outside0, outside1, fglobal, foutside)` with
    `from_rows(fee_rows: pl.DataFrame, ticks: list[int]) -> FeeAccumulators`, `init_tick(t, current_tick)`,
    `clear_tick(t)`, `cross(t)`, `inside(lo, hi, current_tick) -> tuple[int, int, list[float]]`,
    `add(zero_for_one, lp_fee, liquidity, values: tuple[float, float, float]) -> bool`
  - `class StepRecord(NamedTuple)`: `block_number, log_index, step, zero_for_one, flow_label, ts_us,
    liquidity, amount_in, amount_out, fee, protocol_fee, lp_fee, crossed_tick, fee_usd, lvr_0, lvr_12,
    lvr_12_missing, accrued`
  - `class PoolState(book: TickBook, acc: FeeAccumulators, fee_protocol: int)` with
    `swap(block_number, log_index, run, zero_for_one, sqrt_after, tick_after, p0, p12, flow_label, ts_us) -> list[StepRecord]`,
    `mint(lo, hi, amount)`, `burn(lo, hi, amount)`, `inside(lo, hi) -> tuple[int, int, list[float]]`

Create `tests/test_lp_state.py`:

```python
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import (
    Q128,
    U256,
    FeeAccumulators,
    PoolState,
    step_lvr,
)
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import get_amount1_delta, swap

L = 10**18


def state() -> PoolState:
    s = PoolState(TickBook(tick=0, sqrt_price_x96=sqrt_at(0), liquidity=0), FeeAccumulators(), 0)
    s.mint(-10, 10, L)
    return s


def test_fee_growth_follows_the_contract_floor() -> None:
    s = state()
    assert s.acc.add(True, 1000, L, (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
    g0, g1, _ = s.inside(-10, 10)
    assert g0 == 1000 * Q128 // L and g1 == 0
    assert L * g0 // Q128 == 999  # mulDiv floors twice: the position is owed 999, not 1000


def test_crossing_flips_outside_and_keeps_inside() -> None:
    s = state()
    s.acc.add(True, 1000, L, (1.0, 2.0, 3.0))
    g = s.acc.global0
    s.book.apply_swap(sqrt_at(-11), -11)  # the price leaves the range downwards
    s.acc.cross(-10)
    assert s.acc.outside0[-10] == g
    inside0, _, floats = s.inside(-10, 10)
    assert inside0 == g and floats == pytest.approx([1.0 / L, 2.0 / L, 3.0 / L])
    s.acc.add(True, 5000, L, (1.0, 1.0, 1.0))  # fees out of range do not reach the position
    assert s.inside(-10, 10)[0] == g


def test_inside_handles_uint256_wrap() -> None:
    acc = FeeAccumulators(global0=5)
    acc.outside0[-10], acc.outside1[-10], acc.foutside[-10] = 9, 0, [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    acc.outside0[10], acc.outside1[10], acc.foutside[10] = 0, 0, [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    inside0, _, _ = acc.inside(-10, 10, 0)
    assert inside0 == (5 - 9) % U256


def test_add_with_zero_liquidity_accrues_nothing() -> None:
    s = state()
    before = (s.acc.global0, list(s.acc.fglobal))
    assert not s.acc.add(True, 1000, 0, (1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
    assert (s.acc.global0, s.acc.fglobal) == before


def test_reinitialized_tick_gets_fresh_outside_values() -> None:
    s = state()
    s.acc.add(True, 1000, L, (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
    assert s.acc.outside0[-10] == 0  # initialized at fee growth 0 (tick -10 <= 0)
    s.burn(-10, 10, L)
    assert -10 not in s.acc.outside0  # cleared with the tick
    s.book.apply_swap(sqrt_at(-20), -20)
    s.mint(-10, 10, L)  # -10 is now above the current tick: outside starts at 0, not global
    assert s.acc.outside0[-10] == 0
    s.burn(-10, 10, L)
    s.book.apply_swap(sqrt_at(0), 0)
    s.mint(-10, 10, L)  # -10 below the current tick again: outside = global
    assert s.acc.outside0[-10] == s.acc.global0


def test_step_lvr_sign() -> None:
    # The pool sells 1 ETH for 2000 USDC while Binance says 2010: the trader gained $10.
    assert step_lvr(True, 2_000 * 10**6, 10**18, 2010.0) == pytest.approx(10.0)
    assert step_lvr(False, 10**18, 2_020 * 10**6, 2010.0) == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_pool_state_swap_accumulates_and_crosses() -> None:
    s = state()
    s.mint(10, 20, 2 * L)
    to_10 = get_amount1_delta(sqrt_at(0), sqrt_at(10), L, True)
    run = swap(s.book, False, to_10 + to_10 // 4)
    s.fee_protocol = 4 + (4 << 4)
    records = s.swap(1, 0, run, False, run.sqrt_price_x96, run.tick, 2000.0, None, "searcher", 0)
    assert len(records) == len(run.steps) == 2
    assert sum(r.lp_fee + r.protocol_fee for r in records) == run.fee
    assert all(r.protocol_fee == r.fee // 4 for r in records)
    assert records[0].crossed_tick == 10 and records[1].crossed_tick is None
    assert all(r.lvr_12_missing for r in records)
    assert (s.book.tick, s.book.liquidity) == (run.tick, 2 * L)
    # tick 10 was initialized above the price (outside = 0), so after the cross it holds the
    # token1 growth accrued below it: exactly the first step's growth.
    assert s.acc.outside1[10] == records[0].lp_fee * Q128 // L
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/state.py`:

```python
"""Pool fee and LVR accumulators on top of the TickBook (spec section 5).

Fees follow UniswapV3Pool and Tick exactly: feeGrowthGlobal{0,1}X128 grows by
mulDiv(lpFee, 2^128, liquidity) per step, a crossed tick's outside value becomes
global - outside, and fee growth inside a range is global - below - above, all modulo 2^256.
Three float accumulators (USD fees, LVR at 0 s and 12 s) use the same mechanics; they start at
zero at the snapshot (plan Ruling 3) because only differences between checkpoints are used.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import NamedTuple

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import DECIMALS0, DECIMALS1
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import hex_u256
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.swapfees import protocol_cut
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import SwapRun

Q128 = 2**128
U256 = 2**256
FLOAT_NAMES = ("fee_usd", "lvr_0", "lvr_12")
_ZERO = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)


def sub256(a: int, b: int) -> int:
    return (a - b) % U256


def step_lvr(zero_for_one: bool, amount_in: int, amount_out: int, price: float) -> float:
    """-(pool's USDC change + pool's WETH change x price), the fee excluded (spec section 5).

    Positive when the trader gained against the reference price."""
    if zero_for_one:  # USDC in, WETH out
        d_usdc, d_eth = amount_in / 10**DECIMALS0, -amount_out / 10**DECIMALS1
    else:
        d_usdc, d_eth = -amount_out / 10**DECIMALS0, amount_in / 10**DECIMALS1
    return -(d_usdc + d_eth * price)


@dataclass
class FeeAccumulators:
    global0: int = 0
    global1: int = 0
    outside0: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    outside1: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    fglobal: list[float] = field(default_factory=lambda: list(_ZERO))
    foutside: dict[int, list[float]] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @classmethod
    def from_rows(cls, fee_rows: pl.DataFrame, ticks: list[int]) -> "FeeAccumulators":
        """Start state from on-chain fee rows (data.chain_state) for every initialized tick."""
        g = fee_rows.filter(pl.col("kind") == "global")
        if g.height != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"expected one global fee row, got {g.height}")
        acc = cls(global0=hex_u256(g["fee_growth0"][0]), global1=hex_u256(g["fee_growth1"][0]))
        tick_rows = fee_rows.filter(pl.col("kind") == "tick")
        values = {
            t: (hex_u256(a), hex_u256(b))
            for t, a, b in tick_rows.select("tick", "fee_growth0", "fee_growth1").iter_rows()
        }
        for t in ticks:
            if t not in values:
                raise ValueError(f"no on-chain fee row for initialized tick {t}")
            acc.outside0[t], acc.outside1[t] = values[t]
            acc.foutside[t] = list(_ZERO)
        return acc

    def init_tick(self, t: int, current_tick: int) -> None:
        """Tick.update when a tick flips to initialized."""
        if t <= current_tick:
            self.outside0[t], self.outside1[t] = self.global0, self.global1
            self.foutside[t] = list(self.fglobal)
        else:
            self.outside0[t], self.outside1[t] = 0, 0
            self.foutside[t] = list(_ZERO)

    def clear_tick(self, t: int) -> None:
        self.outside0.pop(t, None)
        self.outside1.pop(t, None)
        self.foutside.pop(t, None)

    def cross(self, t: int) -> None:
        """Tick.cross."""
        self.outside0[t] = sub256(self.global0, self.outside0[t])
        self.outside1[t] = sub256(self.global1, self.outside1[t])
        self.foutside[t] = [g - o for g, o in zip(self.fglobal, self.foutside[t], strict=True)]

    def inside(self, lo: int, hi: int, current_tick: int) -> tuple[int, int, list[float]]:
        """Tick.getFeeGrowthInside, for the exact pair and the float accumulators."""
        if current_tick >= lo:
            b0, b1, bf = self.outside0[lo], self.outside1[lo], self.foutside[lo]
        else:
            b0 = sub256(self.global0, self.outside0[lo])
            b1 = sub256(self.global1, self.outside1[lo])
            bf = [g - o for g, o in zip(self.fglobal, self.foutside[lo], strict=True)]
        if current_tick < hi:
            a0, a1, af = self.outside0[hi], self.outside1[hi], self.foutside[hi]
        else:
            a0 = sub256(self.global0, self.outside0[hi])
            a1 = sub256(self.global1, self.outside1[hi])
            af = [g - o for g, o in zip(self.fglobal, self.foutside[hi], strict=True)]
        floats = [g - b - a for g, b, a in zip(self.fglobal, bf, af, strict=True)]
        return sub256(sub256(self.global0, b0), a0), sub256(sub256(self.global1, b1), a1), floats

    def add(
        self, zero_for_one: bool, lp_fee: int, liquidity: int, values: tuple[float, float, float]
    ) -> bool:
        """One swap step. The contract updates fee growth only with liquidity in range."""
        if liquidity <= 0:
            return False
        growth = lp_fee * Q128 // liquidity
        if zero_for_one:
            self.global0 = (self.global0 + growth) % U256
        else:
            self.global1 = (self.global1 + growth) % U256
        for i, v in enumerate(values):
            self.fglobal[i] += v / liquidity
        return True


class StepRecord(NamedTuple):
    block_number: int
    log_index: int
    step: int
    zero_for_one: bool
    flow_label: str
    ts_us: int
    liquidity: int
    amount_in: int
    amount_out: int
    fee: int
    protocol_fee: int
    lp_fee: int
    crossed_tick: int | None
    fee_usd: float
    lvr_0: float
    lvr_12: float
    lvr_12_missing: bool
    accrued: bool


class PoolState:
    """TickBook + fee accumulators + slot0.feeProtocol, advanced event by event."""

    def __init__(self, book: TickBook, acc: FeeAccumulators, fee_protocol: int) -> None:
        self.book = book
        self.acc = acc
        self.fee_protocol = fee_protocol

    def swap(
        self,
        block_number: int,
        log_index: int,
        run: SwapRun,
        zero_for_one: bool,
        sqrt_after: int,
        tick_after: int,
        p0: float,
        p12: float | None,
        flow_label: str,
        ts_us: int,
    ) -> list[StepRecord]:
        """Apply a reproduced swap step by step, then move the book to the logged state."""
        usd_per_raw_in = 1 / 10**DECIMALS0 if zero_for_one else p0 / 10**DECIMALS1
        records = []
        for i, s in enumerate(run.steps):
            cut = protocol_cut(s.fee, self.fee_protocol, zero_for_one)
            lp_fee = s.fee - cut
            fee_usd = lp_fee * usd_per_raw_in
            lvr0 = step_lvr(zero_for_one, s.amount_in, s.amount_out, p0)
            lvr12 = 0.0 if p12 is None else step_lvr(zero_for_one, s.amount_in, s.amount_out, p12)
            accrued = self.acc.add(zero_for_one, lp_fee, s.liquidity, (fee_usd, lvr0, lvr12))
            if s.crossed_tick is not None:
                self.acc.cross(s.crossed_tick)
            records.append(
                StepRecord(
                    block_number, log_index, i, zero_for_one, flow_label, ts_us, s.liquidity,
                    s.amount_in, s.amount_out, s.fee, cut, lp_fee, s.crossed_tick, fee_usd, lvr0,
                    lvr12, p12 is None, accrued,
                )
            )
        self.book.apply_swap(sqrt_after, tick_after)
        return records

    def mint(self, lo: int, hi: int, amount: int) -> None:
        """Ticks flipping to initialized get their outside values first (Tick.update)."""
        for t in (lo, hi):
            if t not in self.book.liquidity_gross:
                self.acc.init_tick(t, self.book.tick)
        self.book.apply_mint(lo, hi, amount)

    def burn(self, lo: int, hi: int, amount: int) -> None:
        """Ticks flipping to uninitialized are cleared after the burn (Tick.clear)."""
        self.book.apply_burn(lo, hi, amount)
        for t in (lo, hi):
            if t not in self.book.liquidity_gross:
                self.acc.clear_tick(t)

    def inside(self, lo: int, hi: int) -> tuple[int, int, list[float]]:
        return self.acc.inside(lo, hi, self.book.tick)
```

### Task 11: Window replay engine and check L2 (`lp/engine.py`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/engine.py`
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/checks.py` (`check_l2`)
- Test: `tests/test_engine.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PoolState`, `StepRecord` (Task 10); `reproduce_swap` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `class EngineRow(NamedTuple)`: `source ("pool"|"npm"), block_number, log_index, ts_us, tx_hash,
    event, owner, tick_lower, tick_upper, amount, amount0, amount1, sqrt_price_x96, tick, liquidity,
    fee_protocol0_new, fee_protocol1_new, token_id` (missing columns come back None)
  - `iter_engine_rows(events: pl.DataFrame, npm_events: pl.DataFrame | None = None) -> Iterator[EngineRow]`
    (sorted by block, log index; NPM `transfer` rows are dropped)
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) SwapContext(p0: float, p12: float | None, flow_label: str, ts_us: int)`
  - `contexts_from_aligned(aligned: pl.DataFrame) -> dict[tuple[int, int], SwapContext]`
  - `class LedgerHooks(Protocol)`: `start(state)`, `on_pool_event(row, state)`, `on_npm_event(row, state)`,
    `new_day(day: date, state)`, `finish(state)`
  - `STEP_SCHEMA`; `@dataclass WindowRun(steps: pl.DataFrame, modes: dict[str, int], unmatched: list[dict[str, Any]], captures: dict[int, dict[str, Any]], zero_liquidity_steps: int, null_12s_steps: int)`
  - `replay_window(events, state, contexts, capture: dict[int, list[int]] | None = None, ledger: LedgerHooks | None = None, npm_events: pl.DataFrame | None = None) -> WindowRun`
  - `lp.checks.check_l2(captures, sample_fees: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult`

Hook order (the ledger relies on it): a pool `mint` is applied to the state **before**
`on_pool_event`; a pool `burn` calls `on_pool_event` **before** the state burns (so ticks still exist);
pool `collect` and every NPM event call their hook without changing the state. `new_day` is called
before the first event of each new UTC day; `start` before the first event, `finish` after the last.
A capture for block `b` is taken after every event of `b` (the state the chain reports at `b`).

```python
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.io import u256_hex
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l2
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import (
    EngineRow,
    SwapContext,
    iter_engine_rows,
    replay_window,
)
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import swap

L = 10**18
T0 = datetime(2026, 7, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "log_index": pl.Int64,
    "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String,
    "event": pl.String,
    "owner": pl.String,
    "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64,
    "amount": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128,
    "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128,
    "tick": pl.Int64,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
}
OWNER = "0x" + "aa" * 20


def fresh() -> PoolState:
    return PoolState(TickBook(tick=0, sqrt_price_x96=sqrt_at(0), liquidity=0), FeeAccumulators(), 0)


def window_events() -> pl.DataFrame:
    probe = fresh()
    probe.mint(-10, 10, L)
    run = swap(probe.book, True, 10**15)
    rows = [
        {"block_number": 1, "log_index": 0, "event": "mint", "owner": OWNER,
         "tick_lower": -10, "tick_upper": 10, "amount": L, "amount0": 1, "amount1": 1},
        {"block_number": 2, "log_index": 0, "event": "swap", "amount0": run.amount_in,
         "amount1": -run.amount_out, "sqrt_price_x96": run.sqrt_price_x96, "tick": run.tick,
         "liquidity": run.liquidity},
        {"block_number": 3, "log_index": 0, "event": "burn", "owner": OWNER,
         "tick_lower": -10, "tick_upper": 10, "amount": L // 2, "amount0": 1, "amount1": 1},
    ]
    for r in rows:
        r["block_timestamp"] = T0
        r["tx_hash"] = f"0x{r['block_number']:064x}"
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=SCHEMA)


CONTEXTS = {(2, 0): SwapContext(2000.0, 2001.0, "searcher", int(T0.timestamp()) * 1_000_000)}


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, Any]] = []

    def start(self, state: PoolState) -> None:
        self.calls.append(("start", None))

    def on_pool_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None:
        # mint: the ticks already exist; burn: they still exist
        assert -10 in state.acc.outside0
        self.calls.append(("pool", (row.event, state.book.liquidity)))

    def on_npm_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None:
        self.calls.append(("npm", row.event))

    def new_day(self, day: date, state: PoolState) -> None:
        self.calls.append(("day", day))

    def finish(self, state: PoolState) -> None:
        self.calls.append(("finish", None))


def test_iter_engine_rows_merges_and_drops_transfers() -> None:
    npm = pl.DataFrame(
        {
            "block_number": [1, 1],
            "log_index": [1, 2],
            "block_timestamp": [T0, T0],
            "tx_hash": ["0x01", "0x01"],
            "event": ["transfer", "increase"],
            "token_id": [7, 7],
            "liquidity": [None, L],
        },
        schema={**{k: SCHEMA[k] for k in ("block_number", "log_index", "block_timestamp",
                                          "tx_hash", "event", "liquidity")}, "token_id": pl.Int64},
    )
    rows = list(iter_engine_rows(window_events(), npm))
    assert [(r.source, r.event) for r in rows] == [
        ("pool", "mint"), ("npm", "increase"), ("pool", "swap"), ("pool", "burn")
    ]
    assert rows[1].token_id == 7 and rows[1].liquidity == L and rows[0].owner == OWNER


def test_replay_window_accumulates_calls_hooks_and_captures() -> None:
    st = fresh()
    rec = Recorder()
    run = replay_window(window_events(), st, CONTEXTS, capture={2: [-10, 10]}, ledger=rec)
    assert run.modes == {"exact_input": 1}
    assert run.steps.height == 1 and run.steps["flow_label"].to_list() == ["searcher"]
    assert rec.calls[0] == ("start", None) and rec.calls[-1] == ("finish", None)
    assert ("pool", ("mint", L)) in rec.calls  # mint applied before the hook
    assert ("pool", ("burn", L)) in rec.calls  # burn hook before the burn
    assert st.book.liquidity == L // 2
    cap = run.captures[2]
    assert cap["global0"] == st.acc.global0 and cap["ticks"][-10] == (0, 0)


def test_replay_window_needs_a_reference_for_every_swap() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no reference price for the swap at block 2 log 0"):
        replay_window(window_events(), fresh(), {})


def test_l2_compares_captures_with_chain_rows() -> None:
    st = fresh()
    run = replay_window(window_events(), st, CONTEXTS, capture={2: [-10]})
    cap = run.captures[2]
    rows = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            {"block": 2, "kind": "global", "tick": None,
             "fee_growth0": u256_hex(cap["global0"]), "fee_growth1": u256_hex(cap["global1"])},
            {"block": 2, "kind": "tick", "tick": -10,
             "fee_growth0": u256_hex(0), "fee_growth1": u256_hex(0)},
        ],
        schema={"block": pl.Int64, "kind": pl.String, "tick": pl.Int64,
                "fee_growth0": pl.String, "fee_growth1": pl.String},
    )
    assert check_l2(run.captures, rows).passed
    bad = rows.with_columns(fee_growth0=pl.lit(u256_hex(1)))
    r = check_l2(run.captures, bad)
    assert not r.passed and r.summary["mismatches"] == 2
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/engine.py`:

```python
"""Window replay of pool (and NPM) events through PoolState (spec section 5).

Every swap is reproduced bit-exactly (v3swap.reproduce_swap) and applied step by step to the fee
and LVR accumulators. A ledger, when given, sees every liquidity event in contract order.
"""

from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Iterator
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from typing import Any, NamedTuple, Protocol

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import PoolState, StepRecord
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import reproduce_swap


class EngineRow(NamedTuple):
    source: str
    block_number: int
    log_index: int
    ts_us: int
    tx_hash: str | None
    event: str
    owner: str | None
    tick_lower: int | None
    tick_upper: int | None
    amount: int | None
    amount0: int | None
    amount1: int | None
    sqrt_price_x96: int | None
    tick: int | None
    liquidity: int | None
    fee_protocol0_new: int | None
    fee_protocol1_new: int | None
    token_id: int | None


_DTYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "tx_hash": pl.String,
    "owner": pl.String,
    "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64,
    "amount": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128,
    "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128,
    "tick": pl.Int64,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "fee_protocol0_new": pl.Int64,
    "fee_protocol1_new": pl.Int64,
    "token_id": pl.Int64,
}


def _prepare(df: pl.DataFrame, source: str) -> pl.DataFrame:
    cols = [
        pl.lit(source).alias("source"),
        pl.col("block_number").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("log_index").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("block_timestamp").dt.epoch("us").alias("ts_us"),
        pl.col("event"),
        *[
            (pl.col(c).cast(t) if c in df.columns else pl.lit(None, dtype=t)).alias(c)
            for c, t in _DTYPES.items()
        ],
    ]
    return df.select(cols).select(list(EngineRow._fields))


def iter_engine_rows(
    events: pl.DataFrame, npm_events: pl.DataFrame | None = None
) -> Iterator[EngineRow]:
    """Pool and NPM events merged in (block, log index) order; NPM transfers are dropped."""
    frames = [_prepare(events, "pool")]
    if npm_events is not None and npm_events.height:
        frames.append(_prepare(npm_events.filter(pl.col("event") != "transfer"), "npm"))
    for r in pl.concat(frames).sort("block_number", "log_index").iter_rows():
        yield EngineRow(*r)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SwapContext:
    p0: float
    p12: float | None
    flow_label: str
    ts_us: int


def contexts_from_aligned(aligned: pl.DataFrame) -> dict[tuple[int, int], SwapContext]:
    """Reference prices and flow labels per swap, from sub-project 2's aligned table."""
    out: dict[tuple[int, int], SwapContext] = {}
    for b, li, p0, p12, label, t0 in aligned.select(
        "block_number", "log_index", "p_cex_0", "p_cex_12", "flow_label", "t0_us"
    ).iter_rows():
        out[(b, li)] = SwapContext(float(p0), None if p12 is None else float(p12), label, t0)
    return out


class LedgerHooks(Protocol):
    def start(self, state: PoolState) -> None: ...
    def on_pool_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None: ...
    def on_npm_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None: ...
    def new_day(self, day: date, state: PoolState) -> None: ...
    def finish(self, state: PoolState) -> None: ...


STEP_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "log_index": pl.Int64,
    "step": pl.Int64,
    "zero_for_one": pl.Boolean,
    "flow_label": pl.String,
    "ts_us": pl.Int64,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "amount_in": pl.Int128,
    "amount_out": pl.Int128,
    "fee": pl.Int128,
    "protocol_fee": pl.Int128,
    "lp_fee": pl.Int128,
    "crossed_tick": pl.Int64,
    "fee_usd": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_0": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_12": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_12_missing": pl.Boolean,
    "accrued": pl.Boolean,
}


@dataclass
class WindowRun:
    steps: pl.DataFrame
    modes: dict[str, int]
    unmatched: list[dict[str, Any]]
    captures: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]
    zero_liquidity_steps: int
    null_12s_steps: int


def _day(ts_us: int) -> date:
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(ts_us / 1_000_000, UTC).date()


def _capture(state: PoolState, ticks: list[int]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    acc = state.acc
    return {
        "global0": acc.global0,
        "global1": acc.global1,
        "ticks": {t: (acc.outside0[t], acc.outside1[t]) for t in ticks if t in acc.outside0},
    }


def _need(row: EngineRow, name: str) -> int:
    value = getattr(row, name)
    if value is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{row.event} at block {row.block_number} log {row.log_index} has no {name}")
    return int(value)


def replay_window(
    events: pl.DataFrame,
    state: PoolState,
    contexts: dict[tuple[int, int], SwapContext],
    capture: dict[int, list[int]] | None = None,
    ledger: LedgerHooks | None = None,
    npm_events: pl.DataFrame | None = None,
) -> WindowRun:
    """Replay the window from `state` (the snapshot state), advancing it in place."""
    pending = sorted(capture or {})
    captures: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    records: list[StepRecord] = []
    modes: Counter[str] = Counter()
    unmatched: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    current_day: date | None = None

    def flush(before_block: int | None) -> None:
        while pending and (before_block is None or pending[0] < before_block):
            b = pending.pop(0)
            captures[b] = _capture(state, (capture or {})[b])

    if ledger is not None:
        ledger.start(state)
    for row in iter_engine_rows(events, npm_events):
        flush(row.block_number)
        day = _day(row.ts_us)
        if day != current_day:
            if ledger is not None:
                ledger.new_day(day, state)
            current_day = day
        if row.source == "npm":
            if ledger is not None:
                ledger.on_npm_event(row, state)
            continue
        if row.event == "swap":
            ctx = contexts.get((row.block_number, row.log_index))
            if ctx is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"no reference price for the swap at block {row.block_number} "
                    f"log {row.log_index}"
                )
            sqrt, tick = _need(row, "sqrt_price_x96"), _need(row, "tick")
            rep = reproduce_swap(state.book, _need(row, "amount0"), _need(row, "amount1"), sqrt)
            if rep is None or rep.run is None:
                if rep is None:
                    unmatched.append({"block": row.block_number, "log_index": row.log_index})
                else:
                    modes[rep.mode] += 1
                state.book.apply_swap(sqrt, tick)
                continue
            modes[rep.mode] += 1
            records.extend(
                state.swap(
                    row.block_number, row.log_index, rep.run, rep.zero_for_one, sqrt, tick,
                    ctx.p0, ctx.p12, ctx.flow_label, ctx.ts_us,
                )
            )
        elif row.event == "mint":
            state.mint(_need(row, "tick_lower"), _need(row, "tick_upper"), _need(row, "amount"))
            if ledger is not None:
                ledger.on_pool_event(row, state)
        elif row.event == "burn":
            if ledger is not None:
                ledger.on_pool_event(row, state)
            state.burn(_need(row, "tick_lower"), _need(row, "tick_upper"), _need(row, "amount"))
        elif row.event == "collect":
            if ledger is not None:
                ledger.on_pool_event(row, state)
        elif row.event == "set_fee_protocol":
            state.fee_protocol = _need(row, "fee_protocol0_new") + (
                _need(row, "fee_protocol1_new") << 4
            )
    flush(None)
    if ledger is not None:
        ledger.finish(state)
    steps = pl.DataFrame(records, schema=STEP_SCHEMA, orient="row")
    return WindowRun(
        steps=steps,
        modes=dict(modes),
        unmatched=unmatched,
        captures=captures,
        zero_liquidity_steps=int((~steps["accrued"]).sum()) if steps.height else 0,
        null_12s_steps=int(steps["lvr_12_missing"].sum()) if steps.height else 0,
    )
```

Append to `lp/checks.py` (add `from uniswap_lp_economics.io import hex_u256`):

```python
def check_l2(captures: dict[int, dict[str, Any]], sample_fees: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    """Engine fee accumulators vs on-chain fee state at the sample blocks, exactly."""
    bad: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    n_bad = compared = 0
    blocks = sorted(set(sample_fees["block"].to_list()))
    for b, kind, tick, f0, f1 in sample_fees.select(
        "block", "kind", "tick", "fee_growth0", "fee_growth1"
    ).iter_rows():
        compared += 1
        cap = captures.get(b)
        if cap is None:
            got: tuple[int, int] | None = None
        elif kind == "global":
            got = (cap["global0"], cap["global1"])
        else:
            got = cap["ticks"].get(tick)
        want = (hex_u256(f0), hex_u256(f1))
        if got != want:
            n_bad += 1
            if len(bad) < MAX_LISTED:
                bad.append({"block": b, "kind": kind, "tick": tick,
                            "onchain": want, "engine": got})
    return CheckResult(
        "L2 fee accumulators match the chain",
        n_bad == 0 and compared > 0 and all(b in captures for b in blocks),
        {"blocks": len(blocks), "values_compared": compared, "mismatches": n_bad},
        bad,
    )
```

```python
import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics import config
from uniswap_lp_economics.build import load_snapshot_meta
from uniswap_lp_economics.data import chain_state
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import read_exact
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l2
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import contexts_from_aligned, replay_window
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook

raw = config.RAW_DIR
meta = load_snapshot_meta(raw)
ticks = read_exact(raw / "tickbook_snapshot.parquet")
fees = read_exact(raw / chain_state.CHAIN_DIR / chain_state.SNAPSHOT_FEES)
samples = read_exact(raw / chain_state.CHAIN_DIR / chain_state.SAMPLE_FEES)
state = PoolState(
    TickBook.from_snapshot(ticks, meta),
    FeeAccumulators.from_rows(fees, ticks["tick"].to_list()),
    int(meta["fee_protocol"]),
)
capture = {
    int(b): g.filter(pl.col("kind") == "tick")["tick"].to_list()
    for (b,), g in samples.partition_by("block", as_dict=True).items()
}
aligned = read_exact(config.PROCESSED_DIR / "aligned.parquet")
run = replay_window(read_exact(config.INTERIM_DIR / "events.parquet"), state,
                    contexts_from_aligned(aligned), capture)
print("modes", run.modes, "unmatched", len(run.unmatched), "zero-L steps", run.zero_liquidity_steps)
r = check_l2(run.captures, samples)
print(r.passed, r.summary, r.mismatches[:5])
```

**Interfaces:**

    (`"skip" | "dry_run" | "refuse" | "run"`)
  - `is_billing_error(exc: Exception) -> bool`
  - `fetch_history(raw_dir: Path, start: date, end: date, confirm_cost: bool, dry_run: bool = False) -> None`
  - `NPM_SCHEMA`; `decode_npm_event(topics: list[str], data: str) -> dict[str, Any]` (events
    `increase`, `decrease`, `npm_collect`, `transfer`); `decode_npm_logs(raw: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame`
  - `load_history(raw_dir: Path) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]` — decoded pool events (same
    columns as `events.parquet`) and NPM events (`block_number, block_timestamp, tx_hash,

Create `tests/test_history.py`:

```python
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics import abi
from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS, POOL_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.data import history
from uniswap_lp_economics.events import MINT_TOPIC

ALICE = "0x" + "a1" * 20


def t_addr(a: str) -> str:
    return "0x" + abi.enc_addr(a)


def t_uint(v: int) -> str:
    return "0x" + abi.enc_uint(v)


def data(*values: int) -> str:
    return "0x" + "".join(abi.enc_int(v) for v in values)


def test_stage1_query_scans_both_contracts_and_seven_topics() -> None:
    q = history.build_stage1_query(date(2021, 5, 4), date(2026, 10, 1))
    assert f"'{POOL_ADDRESS}'" in q and f"'{NPM_ADDRESS}'" in q
    for t in (MINT_TOPIC, history.INCREASE_TOPIC, history.TRANSFER_TOPIC):
        assert t in q
    assert "TIMESTAMP('2021-05-04')" in q and "TIMESTAMP('2026-10-01')" in q


def test_npm_query_selects_tokens_minted_into_our_pool() -> None:
    q = history.build_npm_query("p.d.t")
    assert "`p.d.t`" in q
    assert history.INCREASE_TOPIC in q and history.TRANSFER_TOPIC in q
    assert "topics[SAFE_OFFSET(3)]" in q  # Transfer carries the token in its third indexed topic
    assert t_addr(NPM_ADDRESS) in q  # pool Mint owned by the position manager


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("exists", "dry", "confirm", "expected"),
    [
        (True, False, False, "skip"),
        (False, True, False, "dry_run"),
        (False, False, False, "refuse"),
        (False, False, True, "run"),
        (False, True, True, "dry_run"),
    ],
)
def test_stage1_action(exists: bool, dry: bool, confirm: bool, expected: str) -> None:
    assert history.stage1_action(exists, dry, confirm) == expected


def test_is_billing_error() -> None:
    assert history.is_billing_error(RuntimeError("403 Billing has not been enabled"))
    assert history.is_billing_error(RuntimeError("Quota exceeded: free query bytes scanned"))
    assert not history.is_billing_error(RuntimeError("Syntax error"))


def test_decode_npm_events() -> None:
    inc = history.decode_npm_event([history.INCREASE_TOPIC, t_uint(7)], data(10**18, 5, 6))
    assert inc == {"event": "increase", "token_id": 7, "liquidity": 10**18, "amount0": 5,
                   "amount1": 6}
    col = history.decode_npm_event([history.NPM_COLLECT_TOPIC, t_uint(7)],
                                   "0x" + abi.enc_addr(ALICE) + abi.enc_uint(3) + abi.enc_uint(4))
    assert col == {"event": "npm_collect", "token_id": 7, "recipient": ALICE, "amount0": 3,
                   "amount1": 4}
    tr = history.decode_npm_event(
        [history.TRANSFER_TOPIC, t_addr("0x" + "00" * 20), t_addr(ALICE), t_uint(7)], "0x"
    )
    assert tr == {"event": "transfer", "from_owner": "0x" + "00" * 20, "to_owner": ALICE,
                  "token_id": 7}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown NonfungiblePositionManager topic"):
        history.decode_npm_event(["0x" + "00" * 32], "0x")


def test_load_history_decodes_both_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = tmp_path / history.HISTORY_DIR
    folder.mkdir()
    ts = datetime(2021, 6, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    base = {"block_timestamp": ts, "transaction_hash": "0x01", "transaction_index": 0}
    pool = pl.DataFrame(
        [{**base, "block_number": 1, "log_index": 0, "address": POOL_ADDRESS,
          "topics": [MINT_TOPIC, t_addr(NPM_ADDRESS), "0x" + abi.enc_int(-10),
                     "0x" + abi.enc_int(10)],
          "data": "0x" + abi.enc_addr(NPM_ADDRESS) + abi.enc_uint(10**18) + abi.enc_uint(1)
                  + abi.enc_uint(2)}]
    )
    npm = pl.DataFrame(
        [{**base, "block_number": 1, "log_index": 1, "address": NPM_ADDRESS,
          "topics": [history.INCREASE_TOPIC, t_uint(7)], "data": data(10**18, 1, 2)}]
    )
    pool.write_parquet(folder / history.POOL_FILE)
    npm.write_parquet(folder / history.NPM_FILE)
    p, n = history.load_history(tmp_path)
    assert p.row(0, named=True)["event"] == "mint" and p.row(0, named=True)["owner"] == NPM_ADDRESS
    assert p.row(0, named=True)["tx_hash"] == "0x01"
    assert n.row(0, named=True)["token_id"] == 7 and n.row(0, named=True)["tx_hash"] == "0x01"


def test_load_history_names_the_command(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="ulpe fetch-history"):
        history.load_history(tmp_path)
```
