- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/history.py`:

```python
"""Full pool and NonfungiblePositionManager history (spec D1, section 3.2).

Stage 1 is the one paid query: every pool Mint/Burn/Collect and every NPM IncreaseLiquidity/
DecreaseLiquidity/Collect/Transfer since the pool was created, written into a table in the user's
project. Stage 2 queries only that table (cents): the pool's events, and the NPM events of tokens
minted into our pool. Stage 1 never runs without --confirm-cost (the user confirms in chat first).
"""

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
from google.api_core.exceptions import NotFound
from google.cloud import bigquery

from uniswap_lp_economics.abi import enc_addr, to_addr, to_uint, topic, words
from uniswap_lp_economics.build import decode_logs
from uniswap_lp_economics.config import GCP_PROJECT, NPM_ADDRESS, POOL_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.data.bigquery import DATASET
from uniswap_lp_economics.events import BURN_TOPIC, COLLECT_TOPIC, MINT_TOPIC
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import write_exact

HISTORY_DIR = "history"
POOL_FILE = "pool_events.parquet"
NPM_FILE = "npm_events.parquet"
BQ_DATASET = "ulpe_history"
BQ_TABLE = "logs"
MAX_TB_BILLED = 3.1
STAGE2_MAX_GB_BILLED = 200
TABLE_EXPIRATION_MS = 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000

INCREASE_TOPIC = topic("IncreaseLiquidity(uint256,uint128,uint256,uint256)")
DECREASE_TOPIC = topic("DecreaseLiquidity(uint256,uint128,uint256,uint256)")
NPM_COLLECT_TOPIC = topic("Collect(uint256,address,uint256,uint256)")
TRANSFER_TOPIC = topic("Transfer(address,address,uint256)")
NPM_TOPICS = {
    INCREASE_TOPIC: "increase",
    DECREASE_TOPIC: "decrease",
    NPM_COLLECT_TOPIC: "npm_collect",
    TRANSFER_TOPIC: "transfer",
}
_COLUMNS = (
    "block_number, block_timestamp, transaction_hash, transaction_index, log_index, "
    "address, topics, data"
)
BILLING_HELP = (
    "BigQuery refused the query: billing is not enabled for this project, or the free tier is "
    "used up. Link a billing account to the project (console.cloud.google.com/billing → "
    "Account management → Link a project) and run the command again."
)
NPM_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "event": pl.String,
    "token_id": pl.Int64,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128,
    "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "recipient": pl.String,
    "from_owner": pl.String,
    "to_owner": pl.String,
}


def table_id(project: str = GCP_PROJECT) -> str:
    return f"{project}.{BQ_DATASET}.{BQ_TABLE}"


def build_stage1_query(start: date, end: date) -> str:
    topics = ", ".join(
        f"'{t}'"
        for t in (MINT_TOPIC, BURN_TOPIC, COLLECT_TOPIC, *NPM_TOPICS)
    )
    return f"""
SELECT {_COLUMNS}
FROM `{DATASET}.logs`
WHERE block_timestamp >= TIMESTAMP('{start.isoformat()}')
  AND block_timestamp < TIMESTAMP('{end.isoformat()}')
  AND address IN ('{POOL_ADDRESS}', '{NPM_ADDRESS}')
  AND topics[SAFE_OFFSET(0)] IN ({topics})
"""


def build_pool_query(table: str) -> str:
    return f"""
SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM `{table}`
WHERE address = '{POOL_ADDRESS}'
ORDER BY block_number, log_index
"""


def build_npm_query(table: str) -> str:
    """NPM events of every token whose IncreaseLiquidity shares a transaction with a pool Mint
    owned by the position manager. A token of another pool minted in the same transaction can
    slip in; lp.positions ignores it (its events never match a pool event)."""
    npm_owner = "0x" + enc_addr(NPM_ADDRESS)
    return f"""
WITH ours AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT n.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(1)] AS token
  FROM `{table}` n
  JOIN `{table}` p ON p.transaction_hash = n.transaction_hash
  WHERE n.address = '{NPM_ADDRESS}' AND n.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(0)] = '{INCREASE_TOPIC}'
    AND p.address = '{POOL_ADDRESS}' AND p.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(0)] = '{MINT_TOPIC}'
    AND p.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(1)] = '{npm_owner}'
)
SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM `{table}` t
WHERE t.address = '{NPM_ADDRESS}' AND (
  (t.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(0)] IN ('{INCREASE_TOPIC}', '{DECREASE_TOPIC}', '{NPM_COLLECT_TOPIC}')
   AND t.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(1)] IN (SELECT token FROM ours))
  OR (t.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(0)] = '{TRANSFER_TOPIC}'
   AND t.topics[SAFE_OFFSET(3)] IN (SELECT token FROM ours))
)
ORDER BY block_number, log_index
"""


def stage1_action(table_exists: bool, dry_run: bool, confirm_cost: bool) -> str:
    if table_exists:
        return "skip"
    if dry_run:
        return "dry_run"
    if not confirm_cost:
        return "refuse"
    return "run"


def is_billing_error(exc: Exception) -> bool:
    text = str(exc).lower()
    return any(s in text for s in ("billing", "free query bytes", "sandbox"))


def fetch_history(
    raw_dir: Path, start: date, end: date, confirm_cost: bool, dry_run: bool = False
) -> None:
    out = raw_dir / HISTORY_DIR
    if not dry_run and (out / POOL_FILE).exists() and (out / NPM_FILE).exists():
        print("history already downloaded, skipping")
        return
    client = bigquery.Client(project=GCP_PROJECT)
    tid = table_id()
    try:
        client.get_table(tid)
        exists = True
    except NotFound:
        exists = False
    query = build_stage1_query(start, end)
    action = stage1_action(exists, dry_run, confirm_cost)
    if action != "skip":
        dry = client.query(query, job_config=bigquery.QueryJobConfig(dry_run=True))
        tb = (dry.total_bytes_processed or 0) / 1e12
        print(f"stage 1 ({start} -> {end}) scans {tb:.2f} TB; it is billed to the project")
    if action == "dry_run":
        return
    if action == "refuse":
        raise SystemExit(
            "stage 1 is a paid query; after confirming the cost, run "
            "`ulpe fetch-history --confirm-cost`"
        )
    try:
        if action == "run":
            ds = bigquery.Dataset(f"{GCP_PROJECT}.{BQ_DATASET}")
            ds.location = "US"
            ds.default_table_expiration_ms = TABLE_EXPIRATION_MS
            client.create_dataset(ds, exists_ok=True)
            cfg = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(
                destination=tid,
                write_disposition="WRITE_TRUNCATE",
                maximum_bytes_billed=int(MAX_TB_BILLED * 1e12),
            )
            client.query(query, job_config=cfg).result()
            print(f"  stage 1 written to {tid}")
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # The pool file is the skip marker, so it is written last.
        for name, q in ((NPM_FILE, build_npm_query(tid)), (POOL_FILE, build_pool_query(tid))):
            cfg2 = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(maximum_bytes_billed=int(STAGE2_MAX_GB_BILLED * 1e9))
            df = pl.from_arrow(client.query(q, job_config=cfg2).result().to_arrow())
            assert isinstance(df, pl.DataFrame)
            write_exact(df, out / name)
            print(f"  wrote {df.height:,} rows to {out / name}")
    except Exception as e:
        if is_billing_error(e):
            raise SystemExit(BILLING_HELP) from None
        raise


def decode_npm_event(topics: list[str], data: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    kind = NPM_TOPICS.get(topics[0].lower())
    if kind is None:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown NonfungiblePositionManager topic {topics[0]}")
    t = [words(x)[0] for x in topics[1:]]
    d = words(data) if data and data != "0x" else []
    if kind == "transfer":
        return {"event": kind, "from_owner": to_addr(t[0]), "to_owner": to_addr(t[1]),
                "token_id": to_uint(t[2])}
    if kind == "npm_collect":
        return {"event": kind, "token_id": to_uint(t[0]), "recipient": to_addr(d[0]),
                "amount0": to_uint(d[1]), "amount1": to_uint(d[2])}
    return {"event": kind, "token_id": to_uint(t[0]), "liquidity": to_uint(d[0]),
            "amount0": to_uint(d[1]), "amount1": to_uint(d[2])}


def decode_npm_logs(raw: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    rows = [
        decode_npm_event(t, d)
        for t, d in zip(raw["topics"].to_list(), raw["data"].to_list(), strict=True)
    ]
    decoded = pl.from_dicts(rows, schema=NPM_SCHEMA)
    base = raw.select(
        pl.col("block_number").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("block_timestamp").cast(pl.Datetime("us", "UTC")),
        pl.col("transaction_hash").alias("tx_hash"),
        pl.col("transaction_index").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("log_index").cast(pl.Int64),
    )
    return pl.concat([base, decoded], how="horizontal").sort("block_number", "log_index")


def load_history(raw_dir: Path) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    folder = raw_dir / HISTORY_DIR
    for name in (POOL_FILE, NPM_FILE):
        if not (folder / name).exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{folder / name} missing; run `ulpe fetch-history` first")
    pool = decode_logs(pl.read_parquet(folder / POOL_FILE)).with_columns(
        pl.col("block_number").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("log_index").cast(pl.Int64),
        pl.col("block_timestamp").cast(pl.Datetime("us", "UTC")),
    )
    npm = decode_npm_logs(pl.read_parquet(folder / NPM_FILE))
    return pool.sort("block_number", "log_index"), npm
```

### Task 14: Position identity, flows, and checks L5 and L7 (`lp/positions.py`)

Rebuild every position of the pool from the history (spec section 4): NFT positions matched one to
one with their pool events, direct positions split at zero liquidity, owners from NFT transfers.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/positions.py`
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/checks.py` (`check_l5`, `check_l7`)
- Test: `tests/test_positions.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: decoded history frames from `history.load_history` (Task 13): pool events with
  `block_number, log_index, block_timestamp, tx_hash, event, owner, tick_lower, tick_upper, amount,
  amount0, amount1`; NPM events with `block_number, log_index, block_timestamp, tx_hash, event,
  token_id, liquidity, amount0, amount1, from_owner, to_owner`.
- Produces:
  - `FLOW_SCHEMA`: `position_id, kind, token_id, block_number, log_index, pool_log_index, tx_hash,
    ts_us, event ("mint"|"burn"|"poke"|"collect" for direct, "increase"|"decrease"|"collect" for
    NFT), liquidity_delta (Int128), amount0 (Int128), amount1 (Int128)`
  - `POSITION_SCHEMA`: `position_id, kind ("nft"|"direct"), token_id, owner (pool position owner),
    tick_lower, tick_upper, lp, transferred, first_block, first_ts_us, close_block, close_ts_us`
  - `@dataclass LedgerData(positions: pl.DataFrame, flows: pl.DataFrame,
    pool_event_position: dict[tuple[int, int], str], token_position: dict[int, str])`
    — `pool_event_position` maps every pool Mint/Burn/Collect (pokes included) to its position.
  - `build_positions(pool_events, npm_events, npm_address=NPM_ADDRESS) -> LedgerData`
  - `liquidity_at(data: LedgerData, block: int) -> pl.DataFrame` (`position_id, tick_lower,
    tick_upper, liquidity`, positions with liquidity > 0 after every event of `block`)
  - `lp.checks.check_l5(start: pl.DataFrame, snapshot_ticks: pl.DataFrame, meta: dict[str, int]) -> CheckResult`
  - `lp.checks.check_l7(window_events: pl.DataFrame, history_window: pl.DataFrame, data: LedgerData) -> CheckResult`

Position ids: `nft:<token_id>` and `direct:<owner>:<tick_lower>:<tick_upper>:<n>` (`n` = 0, 1, …
per reuse of the key).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_positions.py`:

```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l5, check_l7
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import build_positions, liquidity_at

L = 10**18
T0 = datetime(2026, 6, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
ZERO = "0x" + "00" * 20
ALICE, BOB, DIRECT = "0x" + "a1" * 20, "0x" + "b0" * 20, "0x" + "d1" * 20
POOL_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "event": pl.String, "owner": pl.String, "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64, "amount": pl.Int128, "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128,
}
NPM_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "event": pl.String, "token_id": pl.Int64, "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128, "from_owner": pl.String, "to_owner": pl.String,
}


def p(block: int, log: int, event: str, owner: str, amount: int = 0, a0: int = 0, a1: int = 0,
      lo: int = -10, hi: int = 10) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block_number": block, "log_index": log, "block_timestamp": T0 + timedelta(hours=block),
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "event": event, "owner": owner, "tick_lower": lo,
            "tick_upper": hi, "amount": amount, "amount0": a0, "amount1": a1}


def n(block: int, log: int, event: str, token: int, liquidity: int | None = None, a0: int = 0,
      a1: int = 0, frm: str | None = None, to: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block_number": block, "log_index": log, "block_timestamp": T0 + timedelta(hours=block),
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "event": event, "token_id": token, "liquidity": liquidity,
            "amount0": a0, "amount1": a1, "from_owner": frm, "to_owner": to}


def nft_life() -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    pool = [
        p(1, 0, "mint", NPM_ADDRESS, L, 5, 6),
        p(2, 0, "burn", NPM_ADDRESS, L // 2, 2, 3),
        p(3, 0, "burn", NPM_ADDRESS, 0),  # poke before the collect
        p(3, 1, "collect", NPM_ADDRESS, 0, 4, 5),
        p(5, 0, "burn", NPM_ADDRESS, L // 2, 2, 3),
    ]
    npm = [
        n(1, 1, "transfer", 5, frm=ZERO, to=ALICE),
        n(1, 2, "increase", 5, L, 5, 6),
        n(2, 1, "decrease", 5, L // 2, 2, 3),
        n(3, 2, "npm_collect", 5, None, 4, 5),
        n(4, 0, "transfer", 5, frm=ALICE, to=BOB),
        n(5, 1, "decrease", 5, L // 2, 2, 3),
    ]
    return pl.DataFrame(pool, schema=POOL_SCHEMA), pl.DataFrame(npm, schema=NPM_SCHEMA)


def test_nft_position_is_matched_through_its_life() -> None:
    data = build_positions(*nft_life())
    pos = data.positions.row(0, named=True)
    assert data.positions.height == 1
    assert pos["position_id"] == "nft:5" and pos["kind"] == "nft"
    assert (pos["tick_lower"], pos["tick_upper"]) == (-10, 10)
    assert pos["lp"] == BOB and pos["transferred"] is True
    assert pos["close_block"] == 5
    assert data.flows["event"].to_list() == ["increase", "decrease", "collect", "decrease"]
    assert data.flows["pool_log_index"].to_list() == [0, 0, 1, 0]
    assert len(data.pool_event_position) == 5  # the poke is mapped too
    assert data.token_position == {5: "nft:5"}


def test_direct_key_reuse_starts_a_new_position() -> None:
    pool = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            p(1, 0, "mint", DIRECT, L, 5, 6),
            p(2, 0, "burn", DIRECT, L, 5, 6),
            p(3, 0, "collect", DIRECT, 0, 7, 8),  # old owed: still the first position
            p(4, 0, "mint", DIRECT, 2 * L, 9, 9),
            p(5, 0, "collect", DIRECT, 0, 1, 1),
        ],
        schema=POOL_SCHEMA,
    )
    data = build_positions(pool, pl.DataFrame(schema=NPM_SCHEMA))
    first, second = f"direct:{DIRECT}:-10:10:0", f"direct:{DIRECT}:-10:10:1"
    assert data.positions["position_id"].to_list() == [first, second]
    assert data.pool_event_position[(3, 0)] == first
    assert data.pool_event_position[(5, 0)] == second
    assert data.positions.filter(pl.col("position_id") == first)["close_block"].item() == 2


def test_unmatched_position_manager_pool_event_raises() -> None:
    pool = pl.DataFrame([p(1, 0, "mint", NPM_ADDRESS, L, 5, 6)], schema=POOL_SCHEMA)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no matching NonfungiblePositionManager event"):
        build_positions(pool, pl.DataFrame(schema=NPM_SCHEMA))


def test_tokens_of_other_pools_are_ignored() -> None:
    npm = pl.DataFrame([n(1, 0, "increase", 9, L, 1, 1)], schema=NPM_SCHEMA)
    data = build_positions(pl.DataFrame(schema=POOL_SCHEMA), npm)
    assert data.positions.height == 0 and data.token_position == {}


def test_liquidity_at_and_l5() -> None:
    data = build_positions(*nft_life())
    start = liquidity_at(data, 2)
    assert start.rows() == [("nft:5", -10, 10, L // 2)]
    ticks = pl.DataFrame(
        {"tick": [-10, 10], "liquidity_gross": [L // 2, L // 2], "liquidity_net": [L // 2, -L // 2]},
        schema={"tick": pl.Int64, "liquidity_gross": pl.Int128, "liquidity_net": pl.Int128},
    )
    assert check_l5(start, ticks, {"tick": 0, "liquidity": L // 2}).passed
    bad = check_l5(start, ticks, {"tick": 0, "liquidity": L})
    assert not bad.passed and bad.summary["active_liquidity_matches"] is False


def test_l7_matches_window_events_and_positions() -> None:
    pool, npm = nft_life()
    data = build_positions(pool, npm)
    window = pool.filter(pl.col("block_number") >= 2)
    assert check_l7(window, window, data).passed
    r = check_l7(window, window.filter(pl.col("block_number") != 5), data)
    assert not r.passed and r.summary["missing_in_history"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/positions.py`:

```python
"""Position identity from the full history (spec section 4).

NFT positions: one per token, each NPM IncreaseLiquidity / DecreaseLiquidity / Collect matched to
the pool Mint / Burn / (poke Burn +) Collect of the same transaction that the position manager
emitted just before it. Direct positions: keyed like the contract, (owner, lower, upper), and split
into a new position each time the key's liquidity returns to zero. Nothing is dropped silently:
an unmatched event raises with its block and log index.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS

ZERO_ADDRESS = "0x" + "00" * 20
FLOW_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "position_id": pl.String,
    "kind": pl.String,
    "token_id": pl.Int64,
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "log_index": pl.Int64,
    "pool_log_index": pl.Int64,
    "tx_hash": pl.String,
    "ts_us": pl.Int64,
    "event": pl.String,
    "liquidity_delta": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128,
    "amount1": pl.Int128,
}
POSITION_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "position_id": pl.String,
    "kind": pl.String,
    "token_id": pl.Int64,
    "owner": pl.String,
    "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64,
    "lp": pl.String,
    "transferred": pl.Boolean,
    "first_block": pl.Int64,
    "first_ts_us": pl.Int64,
    "close_block": pl.Int64,
    "close_ts_us": pl.Int64,
}


@dataclass
class _Position:
    position_id: str
    kind: str
    token_id: int | None
    owner: str
    tick_lower: int
    tick_upper: int
    first_block: int
    first_ts_us: int
    liquidity: int = 0
    transferred: bool = False
    close_block: int | None = None
    close_log: int | None = None
    close_ts_us: int | None = None


@dataclass
class LedgerData:
    positions: pl.DataFrame
    flows: pl.DataFrame
    pool_event_position: dict[tuple[int, int], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    token_position: dict[int, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _rows(df: pl.DataFrame, source: str, columns: list[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    present = [c for c in columns if c in df.columns]
    out = df.select(
        *present, ts_us=pl.col("block_timestamp").dt.epoch("us")
    ).with_columns(src=pl.lit(source))
    return out.to_dicts()


def build_positions(
    pool_events: pl.DataFrame, npm_events: pl.DataFrame, npm_address: str = NPM_ADDRESS
) -> LedgerData:
    pool_cols = ["block_number", "log_index", "tx_hash", "event", "owner", "tick_lower",
                 "tick_upper", "amount", "amount0", "amount1"]
    npm_cols = ["block_number", "log_index", "tx_hash", "event", "token_id", "liquidity",
                "amount0", "amount1", "from_owner", "to_owner"]
    pool = pool_events.filter(pl.col("event").is_in(["mint", "burn", "collect"]))
    merged = sorted(
        _rows(pool, "pool", pool_cols) + _rows(npm_events, "npm", npm_cols),
        key=lambda r: (r["block_number"], r["log_index"]),
    )

    positions: dict[str, _Position] = {}
    flows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    direct_current: dict[tuple[str, int, int], _Position] = {}
    episodes: dict[tuple[str, int, int], int] = {}
    tokens: dict[int, _Position] = {}
    owners: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, str]]] = {}
    pool_event_position: dict[tuple[int, int], str] = {}
    pending: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    current_tx: str | None = None

    def where(r: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        return f"block {r['block_number']} log {r['log_index']}"

    def flush_tx() -> None:
        if pending:
            r = pending[0]
            raise ValueError(
                f"pool {r['event']} at {where(r)} owned by the position manager has no matching "
                "NonfungiblePositionManager event"
            )

    def take(pred: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        for i, cand in enumerate(pending):
            if pred(cand):
                return pending.pop(i)
        return None

    def flow(pos: _Position, r: dict[str, Any], event: str, delta: int, pool_log: int) -> None:
        flows.append(
            {
                "position_id": pos.position_id, "kind": pos.kind, "token_id": pos.token_id,
                "block_number": r["block_number"], "log_index": r["log_index"],
                "pool_log_index": pool_log, "tx_hash": r["tx_hash"], "ts_us": r["ts_us"],
                "event": event, "liquidity_delta": delta, "amount0": r["amount0"] or 0,
                "amount1": r["amount1"] or 0,
            }
        )

    def close_if_empty(pos: _Position, r: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if pos.liquidity == 0:
            pos.close_block, pos.close_log, pos.close_ts_us = (
                r["block_number"], r["log_index"], r["ts_us"]
            )

    def same_range(pos: _Position) -> Any:
        return lambda c: (c["tick_lower"], c["tick_upper"]) == (pos.tick_lower, pos.tick_upper)

    def direct(r: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        key = (r["owner"], r["tick_lower"], r["tick_upper"])
        pos = direct_current.get(key)
        if r["event"] == "mint":
            if pos is None or pos.liquidity == 0:
                k = episodes.get(key, 0)
                episodes[key] = k + 1
                pos = _Position(f"direct:{key[0]}:{key[1]}:{key[2]}:{k}", "direct", None, key[0],
                                key[1], key[2], r["block_number"], r["ts_us"])
                positions[pos.position_id] = pos
                direct_current[key] = pos
            pos.liquidity += r["amount"]
            flow(pos, r, "mint", r["amount"], r["log_index"])
        elif pos is None:
            raise ValueError(f"pool {r['event']} at {where(r)} for a position never minted")
        elif r["event"] == "burn":
            if r["amount"] > pos.liquidity:
                raise ValueError(f"burn at {where(r)} exceeds the position's liquidity")
            pos.liquidity -= r["amount"]
            flow(pos, r, "burn" if r["amount"] else "poke", -r["amount"], r["log_index"])
            if r["amount"]:
                close_if_empty(pos, r)
        else:
            flow(pos, r, "collect", 0, r["log_index"])
        pool_event_position[(r["block_number"], r["log_index"])] = pos.position_id

    def nft(r: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        tid = r["token_id"]
        if r["event"] == "transfer":
            owners.setdefault(tid, []).append((r["block_number"], r["log_index"], r["to_owner"]))
            pos = tokens.get(tid)
            moved = r["from_owner"] != ZERO_ADDRESS and r["to_owner"] != ZERO_ADDRESS
            if pos is not None and pos.liquidity > 0 and moved:
                pos.transferred = True
            return
        pos = tokens.get(tid)
        if r["event"] == "increase":
            m = take(lambda c: c["event"] == "mint" and c["amount"] == r["liquidity"])
            if m is None:
                if pos is not None:
                    raise ValueError(f"IncreaseLiquidity of token {tid} at {where(r)} has no pool Mint")
                return  # a token of another pool
            if pos is None:
                pos = _Position(f"nft:{tid}", "nft", tid, npm_address, m["tick_lower"],
                                m["tick_upper"], r["block_number"], r["ts_us"])
                positions[pos.position_id] = pos
                tokens[tid] = pos
            elif (pos.tick_lower, pos.tick_upper) != (m["tick_lower"], m["tick_upper"]):
                raise ValueError(f"token {tid} at {where(r)} matched a Mint of another range")
            pos.liquidity += r["liquidity"]
            pos.close_block = pos.close_log = pos.close_ts_us = None
            flow(pos, r, "increase", r["liquidity"], m["log_index"])
            pool_event_position[(m["block_number"], m["log_index"])] = pos.position_id
            return
        if pos is None:
            return  # a token of another pool
        if r["event"] == "decrease":
            m = take(lambda c: c["event"] == "burn" and c["amount"] == r["liquidity"]
                     and same_range(pos)(c))
            if m is None:
                raise ValueError(f"DecreaseLiquidity of token {tid} at {where(r)} has no pool Burn")
            pos.liquidity -= r["liquidity"]
            flow(pos, r, "decrease", -r["liquidity"], m["log_index"])
            pool_event_position[(m["block_number"], m["log_index"])] = pos.position_id
            close_if_empty(pos, r)
            return
        # npm_collect: a poke Burn(0) first when the token still holds liquidity
        if pos.liquidity > 0:
            poke = take(lambda c: c["event"] == "burn" and c["amount"] == 0 and same_range(pos)(c))
            if poke is None:
                raise ValueError(f"Collect of token {tid} at {where(r)} has no poke Burn")
            pool_event_position[(poke["block_number"], poke["log_index"])] = pos.position_id
        c = take(lambda c: c["event"] == "collect" and same_range(pos)(c)
                 and (c["amount0"], c["amount1"]) == (r["amount0"], r["amount1"]))
        if c is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Collect of token {tid} at {where(r)} has no pool Collect")
        flow(pos, r, "collect", 0, c["log_index"])
        pool_event_position[(c["block_number"], c["log_index"])] = pos.position_id

    for r in merged:
        if r["tx_hash"] != current_tx:
            flush_tx()
            current_tx = r["tx_hash"]
        if r["src"] == "pool":
            if r["owner"] == npm_address:
                pending.append(r)
            else:
                direct(r)
        else:
            nft(r)
    flush_tx()

    rows = []
    for pos in positions.values():
        lp = pos.owner
        if pos.kind == "nft":
            ref = (pos.close_block, pos.close_log) if pos.close_block is not None else None
            history = owners.get(pos.token_id or -1, [])
            before = [h for h in history if ref is None or (h[0], h[1]) <= ref]
            lp = before[-1][2] if before else ZERO_ADDRESS
        rows.append(
            {
                "position_id": pos.position_id, "kind": pos.kind, "token_id": pos.token_id,
                "owner": pos.owner, "tick_lower": pos.tick_lower, "tick_upper": pos.tick_upper,
                "lp": lp, "transferred": pos.transferred, "first_block": pos.first_block,
                "first_ts_us": pos.first_ts_us, "close_block": pos.close_block,
                "close_ts_us": pos.close_ts_us,
            }
        )
    return LedgerData(
        positions=pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=POSITION_SCHEMA),
        flows=pl.DataFrame(flows, schema=FLOW_SCHEMA),
        pool_event_position=pool_event_position,
        token_position={tid: pos.position_id for tid, pos in tokens.items()},
    )


def liquidity_at(data: LedgerData, block: int) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Positions with liquidity > 0 after every event of `block`."""
    liq = (
        data.flows.filter(pl.col("block_number") <= block)
        .group_by("position_id")
        .agg(liquidity=pl.col("liquidity_delta").sum())
        .filter(pl.col("liquidity") > 0)
    )
    return (
        data.positions.select("position_id", "tick_lower", "tick_upper")
        .join(liq, on="position_id", how="inner")
        .sort("position_id")
    )
```

Append to `lp/checks.py` (add `from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import LedgerData`):

```python
def check_l5(start: pl.DataFrame, snapshot_ticks: pl.DataFrame, meta: dict[str, int]) -> CheckResult:
    """Positions open at the snapshot rebuild the snapshot tick book exactly."""
    gross: dict[int, int] = {}
    net: dict[int, int] = {}
    active = 0
    for lo, hi, liq in start.select("tick_lower", "tick_upper", "liquidity").iter_rows():
        for t, sign in ((lo, 1), (hi, -1)):
            gross[t] = gross.get(t, 0) + liq
            net[t] = net.get(t, 0) + sign * liq
        if lo <= meta["tick"] < hi:
            active += liq
    chain = {
        t: (g, nn)
        for t, g, nn in snapshot_ticks.select("tick", "liquidity_gross", "liquidity_net").iter_rows()
    }
    bad: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    n_bad = 0
    for t in sorted(set(chain) | set(gross)):
        rebuilt = (gross.get(t, 0), net.get(t, 0))
        if chain.get(t, (0, 0)) != rebuilt:
            n_bad += 1
            if len(bad) < MAX_LISTED:
                bad.append({"tick": t, "onchain": chain.get(t), "rebuilt": rebuilt})
    active_ok = active == meta["liquidity"]
    return CheckResult(
        "L5 positions rebuild the snapshot tick book",
        n_bad == 0 and active_ok and start.height > 0,
        {
            "positions_open_at_snapshot": start.height,
            "ticks_compared": len(set(chain) | set(gross)),
            "tick_mismatches": n_bad,
            "active_liquidity_matches": active_ok,
        },
        bad,
    )


_L7_KEY = ["block_number", "log_index", "event", "owner", "tick_lower", "tick_upper", "amount",
           "amount0", "amount1"]


def check_l7(
    window_events: pl.DataFrame, history_window: pl.DataFrame, data: LedgerData
) -> CheckResult:
    """The history's window events equal events.parquet, and each belongs to one position."""
    kinds = ["mint", "burn", "collect"]

    def keyed(df: pl.DataFrame) -> set[tuple[Any, ...]]:
        return set(df.filter(pl.col("event").is_in(kinds)).select(_L7_KEY).iter_rows())

    ours, theirs = keyed(window_events), keyed(history_window)
    missing, extra = ours - theirs, theirs - ours
    unassigned = [k for k in ours if (k[0], k[1]) not in data.pool_event_position]
    listed = [{"block": k[0], "log_index": k[1], "issue": "missing_in_history"}
              for k in sorted(missing)[:MAX_LISTED]]
    listed += [{"block": k[0], "log_index": k[1], "issue": "no_position"}
               for k in sorted(unassigned)[:MAX_LISTED]]
    return CheckResult(
        "L7 history complete and every event assigned",
        not missing and not extra and not unassigned and len(ours) > 0,
        {
            "window_liquidity_events": len(ours),
            "missing_in_history": len(missing),
            "extra_in_history": len(extra),
            "without_position": len(unassigned),
        },
        listed,
    )
```

class FakePositions:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.code_calls: list[str] = []

    def multicall(self, calls: list[tuple[str, str]], block: int) -> list[str | None]:
        out: list[str | None] = []
        for _, data in calls:
            if data.startswith(npm_positions_call(0)[:10]):
                token = int(data[10:], 16)
                if token == 99:
                    out.append(None)  # burned NFT: the call reverts
                else:
                    out.append(words([0, 0, 1, 2, 500, -10, 10, 7, 2**250, 3, 4, 5]))
            else:
                out.append(words([11, 2**255, 1, 2, 3]))
        return out

    def get_code(self, address: str, block: int) -> str:
        self.code_calls.append(address)
        return "0x6080" if address.endswith("c0") else "0x"


def test_read_position_state_rows(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    owner = "0x" + "d1" * 20
    df = chain_state.read_position_state(  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        FakePositions(), 5, [(owner, -10, 10)], [7, 99]
    )
    key = df.filter(pl.col("kind") == "pool_key").row(0, named=True)
    assert key["owner"] == owner and key["liquidity"] == 11
    assert key["fee_growth_inside0_last"] == u256_hex(2**255)
    tok = df.filter(pl.col("token_id") == 7).row(0, named=True)
    assert tok["found"] and tok["liquidity"] == 7 and tok["tokens_owed1"] == 5
    assert df.filter(pl.col("token_id") == 99)["found"].to_list() == [False]


def test_owner_code_is_cached(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    a, b = "0x" + "a1" * 20, "0x" + "c0" * 20
    chain_state.fetch_owner_code(fake, tmp_path, [a, b], 9)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    path = chain_state.fetch_owner_code(fake, tmp_path, [a, b], 9)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    df = pl.read_parquet(path).sort("address")
    assert df.rows() == [(a, False), (b, True)]
    assert fake.code_calls == [a, b]  # the second call read nothing

Append to `data/chain_state.py` (extend the imports with `NPM_ADDRESS`, the new rpc codecs,
`liquidity_at`/`LedgerData` from `uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions`, and `u256_hex`):

```python
START_POSITIONS = "start_positions.parquet"
END_POSITIONS = "end_positions.parquet"
OWNER_CODE = "owner_code.parquet"
POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block": pl.Int64,
    "kind": pl.String,
    "owner": pl.String,
    "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64,
    "token_id": pl.Int64,
    "found": pl.Boolean,
    "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "fee_growth_inside0_last": pl.String,
    "fee_growth_inside1_last": pl.String,
    "tokens_owed0": pl.Int128,
    "tokens_owed1": pl.Int128,
}
_I128_MAX = 2**127 - 1


def _owed(value: int) -> int:
    if value > _I128_MAX:
        raise ValueError(f"tokens owed {value} does not fit Int128")
    return value


def read_position_state(
    client: RpcClient, block: int, keys: list[tuple[str, int, int]], tokens: list[int]
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """pool.positions(key) for each (owner, lower, upper) and NPM.positions(tokenId) at `block`.

    A token whose call reverts (burned NFT) comes back with found = False."""
    calls = [(POOL_ADDRESS, pool_positions_call(position_key(*k))) for k in keys]
    calls += [(NPM_ADDRESS, npm_positions_call(t)) for t in tokens]
    results = batched(client, calls, block, allow_failure=True)
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for (owner, lo, hi), res in zip(keys, results[: len(keys)], strict=True):
        if res is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"pool.positions failed for {owner} {lo} {hi} at block {block}")
        d = decode_pool_position(res)
        rows.append(
            {"block": block, "kind": "pool_key", "owner": owner, "tick_lower": lo,
             "tick_upper": hi, "token_id": None, "found": True, "liquidity": d["liquidity"],
             "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(d["fee_growth_inside0_last"]),
             "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(d["fee_growth_inside1_last"]),
             "tokens_owed0": _owed(d["tokens_owed0"]), "tokens_owed1": _owed(d["tokens_owed1"])}
        )
    for token, res in zip(tokens, results[len(keys) :], strict=True):
        if res is None:
            rows.append({"block": block, "kind": "npm_token", "token_id": token, "found": False})
            continue
        n = decode_npm_position(res)
        rows.append(
            {"block": block, "kind": "npm_token", "owner": NPM_ADDRESS,
             "tick_lower": n["tick_lower"], "tick_upper": n["tick_upper"], "token_id": token,
             "found": True, "liquidity": n["liquidity"],
             "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(n["fee_growth_inside0_last"]),
             "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(n["fee_growth_inside1_last"]),
             "tokens_owed0": _owed(n["tokens_owed0"]), "tokens_owed1": _owed(n["tokens_owed1"])}
        )
    return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA)


def state_targets(
    data: LedgerData, block: int, snapshot_block: int, include_new: bool
) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, int, int]], list[int]]:
    """Pool keys and NPM tokens whose on-chain state the ledger needs at `block`: liquidity > 0
    there, or any flow after the snapshot. Without include_new, only positions that existed by
    the snapshot."""
    open_ids = set(liquidity_at(data, block)["position_id"].to_list())
    touched = set(
        data.flows.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snapshot_block)["position_id"].to_list()
    )
    pos = data.positions.filter(pl.col("position_id").is_in(list(open_ids | touched)))
    if not include_new:
        pos = pos.filter(pl.col("first_block") <= snapshot_block)
    keys = sorted(
        {(o, lo, hi) for o, lo, hi in pos.select("owner", "tick_lower", "tick_upper").iter_rows()},
        key=lambda k: (k[0], k[1], k[2]),
    )
    tokens = sorted(int(t) for t in pos["token_id"].drop_nulls().to_list())
    return keys, tokens


def window_active_ids(data: LedgerData, snapshot_block: int) -> list[str]:
    """Positions holding liquidity at some moment of the window (spec section 4)."""
    open_at_start = set(liquidity_at(data, snapshot_block)["position_id"].to_list())
    added = set(
        data.flows.filter(
            (pl.col("block_number") > snapshot_block) & (pl.col("liquidity_delta") > 0)
        )["position_id"].to_list()
    )
    return sorted(open_at_start | added)


def fetch_position_state(
    client: RpcClient,
    raw_dir: Path,
    block: int,
    keys: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
    tokens: list[int],
    name: str,
) -> Path:
    out = raw_dir / CHAIN_DIR / name
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df = read_position_state(client, block, keys, tokens)
    write_exact(df, out)
    print(f"{name}: {len(keys)} pool keys, {len(tokens)} NFT positions at block {block}")
    return out


def fetch_owner_code(client: RpcClient, raw_dir: Path, addresses: list[str], block: int) -> Path:
    """Whether each address has contract code at `block`; cached, so reruns read only new ones."""
    out = raw_dir / CHAIN_DIR / OWNER_CODE
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    known = pl.read_parquet(out) if out.exists() else pl.DataFrame(
        schema={"address": pl.String, "has_code": pl.Boolean}
    )
    done = set(known["address"].to_list())
    todo = [a for a in sorted(set(addresses)) if a not in done]
    rows = []
    for i, a in enumerate(todo):
        rows.append({"address": a, "has_code": client.get_code(a, block) not in ("0x", "")})
        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:
            print(f"  {i + 1}/{len(todo)} owner addresses")
    new = pl.DataFrame(rows, schema={"address": pl.String, "has_code": pl.Boolean})
    write_exact(pl.concat([known, new]), out)
    return out
```

### Task 16: Contract mirror and attribution (`lp/ledger.py`), checks L3 and L4

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/ledger.py`
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/checks.py` (`check_l3`, `check_l4`)
- Test: `tests/test_ledger.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PoolState`, `Q128`, `sub256` (Task 10); `EngineRow`, `replay_window`, `LedgerHooks`
  (Task 11); `LedgerData`, `liquidity_at`, `build_positions` (Task 14); `POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA`
  rows (Task 15).
- Produces:
  - `accrue(liquidity: int, inside: int, last: int) -> int` (`uint128(mulDiv(inside - last, liquidity, 2^128))`)
  - `@dataclass KeyMirror(liquidity, last0, last1, owed0, owed1)`
  - `@dataclass Account(...)` (see code)
  - `class Ledger(data: LedgerData, start_state: pl.DataFrame, snapshot_block: int, npm_address: str = NPM_ADDRESS)`
    implementing `LedgerHooks`, with `keys: dict[tuple[str, int, int], KeyMirror]`,
    `accounts: dict[str, Account]`, `start_mismatches: list[dict]`, `token_mirror(token_id) -> KeyMirror | None`,
    `accounts_frame() -> pl.DataFrame` (`ACCOUNT_SCHEMA`), `days_frame() -> pl.DataFrame` (`DAY_SCHEMA`)
  - `lp.checks.check_l3(ledger: Ledger, end_state: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult`
  - `lp.checks.check_l4(ledger: Ledger, end_state: pl.DataFrame, snapshot_block: int) -> CheckResult`

Rules the code implements (from UniswapV3Pool, Position and NonfungiblePositionManager):
- Pool key on `Mint`: accrue on the old liquidity, store the new inside value, add liquidity (a new
  key starts with `last = inside`, owed 0). On `Burn` (amount 0 included): accrue, store inside,
  subtract liquidity, **add the burned principal to owed**. On `Collect`: subtract the amounts.
- NPM token on `IncreaseLiquidity`: accrue on the token's old liquidity, store inside, add liquidity
  (a new token starts with `last = inside`). On `DecreaseLiquidity`: owed += principal + accrual,
  store inside, subtract. On `Collect`: if the token holds liquidity, accrue and store inside (the
  pool was poked); then subtract the collected amounts.
- NPM events take the fee growth inside from the pool key `(NPM, lower, upper)` as stored by the
  pool event just before them (NPM reads `pool.positions(key)`), never from the ticks: the pool
  `Burn` may have just cleared them. Pool `Collect` needs no fee growth.
- Attribution: raw fees accrue at the same events as the mirror and at the window start/end; floats
  also at each new UTC day. A direct position shares its pool key's mirror (the key's current
  episode).

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.data.chain_state import POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import u256_hex
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l3, check_l4
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import SwapContext, replay_window
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import Ledger
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import build_positions
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import Q128, FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import SwapRun, swap

L = 10**18
T0 = datetime(2026, 7, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
DIRECT = "0x" + "d1" * 20
POOL: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "event": pl.String, "owner": pl.String, "tick_lower": pl.Int64,
    "tick_upper": pl.Int64, "amount": pl.Int128, "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128, "tick": pl.Int64, "liquidity": pl.Int128,
}
NPM: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "event": pl.String, "token_id": pl.Int64, "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128, "from_owner": pl.String, "to_owner": pl.String,
}


def ts(block: int) -> datetime:
    return T0 + timedelta(days=block // 10, seconds=block)  # blocks 10+ fall on the next day


def pool_row(block: int, log: int, event: str, **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block_number": block, "log_index": log, "block_timestamp": ts(block),
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "event": event, **kw}


def npm_row(block: int, log: int, event: str, **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block_number": block, "log_index": log, "block_timestamp": ts(block),
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "event": event, **kw}


def fresh() -> PoolState:
    return PoolState(TickBook(tick=0, sqrt_price_x96=sqrt_at(0), liquidity=0), FeeAccumulators(), 0)


def a_swap(probe_liquidity: int = L) -> SwapRun:
    probe = fresh()
    probe.mint(-10, 10, probe_liquidity)
    return swap(probe.book, True, 10**15)


def swap_row(block: int, run: SwapRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return pool_row(block, 0, "swap", amount0=run.amount_in, amount1=-run.amount_out,
                    sqrt_price_x96=run.sqrt_price_x96, tick=run.tick, liquidity=run.liquidity)


def ctx(block: int) -> SwapContext:
    return SwapContext(2000.0, 2000.0, "searcher", int(ts(block).timestamp()) * 1_000_000)


def owed_for(run: SwapRun) -> int:
    return L * (run.fee * Q128 // L) // Q128


def key_row(owner: str, liquidity: int, last0: int, owed0: int, owed1: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block": 9, "kind": "pool_key", "owner": owner, "tick_lower": -10, "tick_upper": 10,
            "token_id": None, "found": True, "liquidity": liquidity,
            "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(last0), "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(0),
            "tokens_owed0": owed0, "tokens_owed1": owed1}


def direct_case(extra: int = 0) -> tuple[Ledger, int, int]:
    run = a_swap()
    owed = owed_for(run)
    events = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            pool_row(1, 0, "mint", owner=DIRECT, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                     amount0=7, amount1=8),
            swap_row(2, run),
            pool_row(3, 0, "burn", owner=DIRECT, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                     amount0=5, amount1=6),
            pool_row(3, 1, "collect", owner=DIRECT, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=0,
                     amount0=5 + owed + extra, amount1=6),
        ],
        schema=POOL,
    )
    data = build_positions(events, pl.DataFrame(schema=NPM))
    ledger = Ledger(data, pl.DataFrame(schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA), 0)
    replay_window(events, fresh(), {(2, 0): ctx(2)}, ledger=ledger)
    return ledger, owed, run.fee


def test_direct_position_fees_equal_collect_minus_principal() -> None:
    ledger, owed, _ = direct_case()
    acc = ledger.accounts[f"direct:{DIRECT}:-10:10:0"]
    assert acc.fees0 == owed and acc.fees1 == 0
    km = ledger.keys[(DIRECT, -10, 10)]
    assert (km.liquidity, km.owed0, km.owed1) == (0, 0, 0)
    end = pl.DataFrame([key_row(DIRECT, 0, km.last0, 0)], schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA)
    assert check_l3(ledger, end).passed
    r = check_l4(ledger, end, 0)
    assert r.passed and r.summary["positions_checked"] == 1


def test_l3_and_l4_catch_a_wrong_collect() -> None:
    ledger, _, _ = direct_case(extra=1)
    km = ledger.keys[(DIRECT, -10, 10)]
    end = pl.DataFrame([key_row(DIRECT, 0, km.last0, 0)], schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA)
    assert not check_l3(ledger, end).passed  # the mirror owes -1, the chain 0
    assert not check_l4(ledger, end, 0).passed  # fees != collected - principal


def test_npm_collect_without_liquidity_does_not_accrue() -> None:
    run = a_swap()
    owed = owed_for(run)
    events = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            pool_row(1, 0, "mint", owner=NPM_ADDRESS, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                     amount0=7, amount1=8),
            swap_row(2, run),
            pool_row(3, 0, "burn", owner=NPM_ADDRESS, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                     amount0=5, amount1=6),
            pool_row(4, 0, "collect", owner=NPM_ADDRESS, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10,
                     amount=0, amount0=5 + owed, amount1=6),
        ],
        schema=POOL,
    )
    npm = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            npm_row(1, 1, "increase", token_id=1, liquidity=L, amount0=7, amount1=8),
            npm_row(3, 1, "decrease", token_id=1, liquidity=L, amount0=5, amount1=6),
            npm_row(4, 1, "npm_collect", token_id=1, amount0=5 + owed, amount1=6),  # no poke
        ],
        schema=NPM,
    )
    data = build_positions(events, npm)
    ledger = Ledger(data, pl.DataFrame(schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA), 0)
    replay_window(events, fresh(), {(2, 0): ctx(2)}, ledger=ledger, npm_events=npm)
    tok = ledger.token_mirror(1)
    assert tok is not None and (tok.liquidity, tok.owed0, tok.owed1) == (0, 0, 0)
    assert ledger.accounts["nft:1"].fees0 == owed
    burned = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"block": 9, "kind": "npm_token", "token_id": 1, "found": False}],
        schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA,
    )
    assert check_l4(ledger, burned, 0).summary["positions_checked"] == 1


def test_day_boundary_splits_float_attribution() -> None:
    first, second = a_swap(), None
    probe = fresh()
    probe.mint(-10, 10, L)
    probe.book.apply_swap(first.sqrt_price_x96, first.tick)
    second = swap(probe.book, True, 10**15)
    events = pl.DataFrame(
        [
            pool_row(1, 0, "mint", owner=DIRECT, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                     amount0=7, amount1=8),
            swap_row(2, first),
            swap_row(12, second),  # the next day
        ],
        schema=POOL,
    )
    ledger = Ledger(build_positions(events, pl.DataFrame(schema=NPM)),
                    pl.DataFrame(schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA), 0)
    replay_window(events, fresh(), {(2, 0): ctx(2), (12, 0): ctx(12)}, ledger=ledger)
    days = ledger.days_frame()
    assert days.height == 2
    acc = ledger.accounts[f"direct:{DIRECT}:-10:10:0"]
    assert days["fee_usd"].sum() == acc.fees_f[0] and acc.fees_f[0] > 0


def test_pre_window_position_starts_from_chain_rows() -> None:
    hist = pl.DataFrame(
        [pool_row(1, 0, "mint", owner=DIRECT, tick_lower=-10, tick_upper=10, amount=L,
                  amount0=7, amount1=8)],
        schema=POOL,
    )
    data = build_positions(hist, pl.DataFrame(schema=NPM))
    st = fresh()
    st.mint(-10, 10, L)  # the snapshot state: the position already exists
    run = swap(st.book, True, 10**15)
    window = pl.DataFrame([swap_row(6, run)], schema=POOL)
    good = pl.DataFrame([key_row(DIRECT, L, 0, 3)], schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA)
    ledger = Ledger(data, good, 5)
    replay_window(window, st, {(6, 0): ctx(6)}, ledger=ledger)
    acc = ledger.accounts[f"direct:{DIRECT}:-10:10:0"]
    assert acc.liquidity_start == L and acc.fees0 == owed_for(run) and not ledger.start_mismatches
    assert ledger.keys[(DIRECT, -10, 10)].owed0 == 3  # carried from the chain, no event since
    wrong = pl.DataFrame([key_row(DIRECT, L // 2, 0, 3)], schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA)
    bad = Ledger(data, wrong, 5)
    st2 = fresh()
    st2.mint(-10, 10, L)
    replay_window(window, st2, {(6, 0): ctx(6)}, ledger=bad)
    assert bad.start_mismatches

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/ledger.py`:

```python
"""Contract mirror and attribution per position during the window replay (spec section 5).

The contract mirror holds what the chain stores for each pool position key and each NPM token,
updated only by events with the contract's arithmetic (check L3 compares it with the chain).
The attribution layer holds what each position earned inside the window: raw fees checkpoint at
the mirror's events and at the window boundaries (so their floors match the contract's); USD fees
and LVR also checkpoint at each new UTC day (plan Ruling 9).
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import hex_u256
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import EngineRow
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import LedgerData, liquidity_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import Q128, PoolState, sub256

U128 = 2**128
ACCOUNT_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "position_id": pl.String,
    "liquidity_start": pl.Int128,
    "liquidity_end": pl.Int128,
    "active": pl.Boolean,
    "carried_owed": pl.Boolean,
    "fees_raw0": pl.Int128,
    "fees_raw1": pl.Int128,
    "fee_usd": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_0": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_12": pl.Float64,
    "owed_end0": pl.Int128,
    "owed_end1": pl.Int128,
    "pending_end0": pl.Int128,
    "pending_end1": pl.Int128,
}
DAY_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "position_id": pl.String,
    "day": pl.Date,
    "fee_usd": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_0": pl.Float64,
    "lvr_12": pl.Float64,
}


def accrue(liquidity: int, inside: int, last: int) -> int:
    """uint128(mulDiv(inside - last, liquidity, 2^128)) with uint256 wrap-around."""
    return sub256(inside, last) * liquidity // Q128 % U128


@dataclass
class KeyMirror:
    liquidity: int
    last0: int
    last1: int
    owed0: int
    owed1: int
    # Float accumulators' inside value at the last update. NPM events read the pool key's stored
    # values (as NonfungiblePositionManager reads pool.positions), because the pool Burn just
    # before them may have cleared the ticks.
    last_f: list[float] = field(default_factory=lambda: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0])


@dataclass
class Account:
    position_id: str
    kind: str
    tick_lower: int
    tick_upper: int
    mirror: KeyMirror  # NPM token state, or the shared pool-key state of a direct position
    att0: int
    att1: int
    att_f: list[float]
    liquidity: int = 0
    liquidity_start: int = 0
    carried_owed: bool = False
    active: bool = False
    fees0: int = 0
    fees1: int = 0
    fees_f: list[float] = field(default_factory=lambda: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
    days: dict[date, list[float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    pending0: int = 0
    pending1: int = 0


def _mirror(row: dict[str, Any]) -> KeyMirror:
    return KeyMirror(
        int(row["liquidity"]),
        hex_u256(row["fee_growth_inside0_last"]),
        hex_u256(row["fee_growth_inside1_last"]),
        int(row["tokens_owed0"]),
        int(row["tokens_owed1"]),
    )


def _accrue_mirror(m: KeyMirror, inside: tuple[int, int, list[float]]) -> None:
    m.owed0 += accrue(m.liquidity, inside[0], m.last0)
    m.owed1 += accrue(m.liquidity, inside[1], m.last1)
    m.last0, m.last1, m.last_f = inside[0], inside[1], list(inside[2])


def _has_ticks(state: PoolState, lo: int, hi: int) -> bool:
    return lo in state.acc.foutside and hi in state.acc.foutside


class Ledger:
    def __init__(
        self,
        data: LedgerData,
        start_state: pl.DataFrame,
        snapshot_block: int,
        npm_address: str = NPM_ADDRESS,
    ) -> None:
        self.data = data
        self.npm = npm_address
        self.snapshot_block = snapshot_block
        self.keys: dict[tuple[str, int, int], KeyMirror] = {}
        self.accounts: dict[str, Account] = {}
        self.current_direct: dict[tuple[str, int, int], str] = {}
        self.start_mismatches: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.day: date | None = None
        self._start_rows = start_state
        self._pos = {r["position_id"]: r for r in data.positions.iter_rows(named=True)}
        self._start_liq: dict[str, int] = dict(
            liquidity_at(data, snapshot_block).select("position_id", "liquidity").iter_rows()
        )
        self._touched = set(
            data.flows.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snapshot_block)["position_id"].to_list()
        )

    # --- hooks ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def start(self, state: PoolState) -> None:
        found = self._start_rows.filter(pl.col("found"))
        for r in found.filter(pl.col("kind") == "pool_key").iter_rows(named=True):
            km = _mirror(r)
            lo, hi = r["tick_lower"], r["tick_upper"]
            if _has_ticks(state, lo, hi):
                km.last_f = state.inside(lo, hi)[2]
            self.keys[(r["owner"], lo, hi)] = km
        tokens = {
            r["token_id"]: r
            for r in found.filter(pl.col("kind") == "npm_token").iter_rows(named=True)
        }
        for pid, p in self._pos.items():
            if p["first_block"] > self.snapshot_block:
                continue
            liq = self._start_liq.get(pid, 0)
            if liq == 0 and pid not in self._touched:
                continue
            key = (p["owner"], p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"])
            if p["kind"] == "nft":
                row = tokens.get(p["token_id"])
                mirror = None if row is None else _mirror(row)
            else:
                mirror = self.keys.get(key)
                self.current_direct[key] = pid
            if mirror is None:
                self.start_mismatches.append({"position_id": pid, "issue": "no start state"})
                continue
            if mirror.liquidity != liq:
                self.start_mismatches.append(
                    {"position_id": pid, "ledger": liq, "onchain": mirror.liquidity}
                )
            if _has_ticks(state, p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"]):
                i0, i1, f = state.inside(p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"])
            else:  # no liquidity on the range now: checkpoints are reset at the next increase
                i0, i1, f = mirror.last0, mirror.last1, [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
            self.accounts[pid] = Account(
                pid, p["kind"], p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"], mirror, i0, i1, list(f),
                liquidity=liq, liquidity_start=liq, carried_owed=True, active=liq > 0,
            )

    def on_pool_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None:
        assert row.owner is not None and row.tick_lower is not None and row.tick_upper is not None
        key = (row.owner, row.tick_lower, row.tick_upper)
        where = f"{row.event} at block {row.block_number} log {row.log_index}"
        km = self.keys.get(key)
        amount = int(row.amount or 0)
        a0, a1 = int(row.amount0 or 0), int(row.amount1 or 0)
        # Collect needs no fee growth (and its ticks may already be cleared).
        inside = state.inside(*key[1:]) if row.event in ("mint", "burn") else None
        if inside is not None and row.event == "mint" and km is None:
            km = KeyMirror(0, inside[0], inside[1], 0, 0, list(inside[2]))
            self.keys[key] = km
        elif km is None:
            raise ValueError(f"{where} for a pool key with no state")
        elif inside is not None:
            _accrue_mirror(km, inside)
        if row.owner != self.npm:
            pid = self.data.pool_event_position[(row.block_number, row.log_index)]
            acc = self.accounts.get(pid)
            if acc is None:
                if inside is None or row.event != "mint":
                    raise ValueError(f"{where} before its position has any state")
                acc = self._new_account(pid, inside, km, carried=(km.owed0, km.owed1) != (0, 0))
                self.current_direct[key] = pid
            if inside is not None:
                self._attribute(acc, inside, raw=True)
                acc.liquidity += amount if row.event == "mint" else -amount
                acc.active = acc.active or acc.liquidity > 0
        if row.event == "mint":
            km.liquidity += amount
        elif row.event == "burn":
            km.liquidity -= amount
            km.owed0 += a0
            km.owed1 += a1
        else:
            km.owed0 -= a0
            km.owed1 -= a1

    def on_npm_event(self, row: EngineRow, state: PoolState) -> None:
        pid = self.data.token_position.get(int(row.token_id or -1))
        if pid is None:
            return  # a token of another pool
        p = self._pos[pid]
        km = self.keys.get((self.npm, p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"]))
        if km is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{row.event} of token {row.token_id} at block {row.block_number} has no pool key"
            )
        # NonfungiblePositionManager reads pool.positions(key) right after the pool call.
        inside = (km.last0, km.last1, list(km.last_f))
        acc = self.accounts.get(pid)
        if acc is None:
            if row.event != "increase":
                raise ValueError(
                    f"{row.event} of token {row.token_id} at block {row.block_number} "
                    "before the token has any state"
                )
            mirror = KeyMirror(0, inside[0], inside[1], 0, 0, list(inside[2]))
            acc = self._new_account(pid, inside, mirror, False)
        m = acc.mirror
        liquidity = int(row.liquidity or 0)
        a0, a1 = int(row.amount0 or 0), int(row.amount1 or 0)
        if row.event == "increase":
            _accrue_mirror(m, inside)
            self._attribute(acc, inside, raw=True)
            m.liquidity += liquidity
            acc.liquidity += liquidity
        elif row.event == "decrease":
            _accrue_mirror(m, inside)
            m.owed0 += a0
            m.owed1 += a1
            self._attribute(acc, inside, raw=True)
            m.liquidity -= liquidity
            acc.liquidity -= liquidity
        elif row.event == "npm_collect":
            if m.liquidity > 0:  # NPM pokes the pool first only when the token holds liquidity
                _accrue_mirror(m, inside)
                self._attribute(acc, inside, raw=True)
            m.owed0 -= a0
            m.owed1 -= a1
        acc.active = acc.active or acc.liquidity > 0

    def new_day(self, day: date, state: PoolState) -> None:
        if self.day is not None:
            for acc in self.accounts.values():
                if acc.liquidity > 0:
                    self._attribute(acc, state.inside(acc.tick_lower, acc.tick_upper), raw=False)
        self.day = day

    def finish(self, state: PoolState) -> None:
        for acc in self.accounts.values():
            if acc.liquidity <= 0:
                continue
            inside = state.inside(acc.tick_lower, acc.tick_upper)
            self._attribute(acc, inside, raw=True)
            acc.pending0 = accrue(acc.mirror.liquidity, inside[0], acc.mirror.last0)
            acc.pending1 = accrue(acc.mirror.liquidity, inside[1], acc.mirror.last1)

    # --- helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _new_account(
        self, pid: str, inside: tuple[int, int, list[float]], mirror: KeyMirror, carried: bool
    ) -> Account:
        p = self._pos[pid]
        acc = Account(
            pid, p["kind"], p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"], mirror, inside[0], inside[1],
            list(inside[2]), carried_owed=carried,
        )
        self.accounts[pid] = acc
        return acc

    def _attribute(self, acc: Account, inside: tuple[int, int, list[float]], raw: bool) -> None:
        i0, i1, f = inside
        if raw:
            acc.fees0 += sub256(i0, acc.att0) * acc.liquidity // Q128
            acc.fees1 += sub256(i1, acc.att1) * acc.liquidity // Q128
            acc.att0, acc.att1 = i0, i1
        bucket = acc.days.setdefault(self.day, [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]) if self.day else None
        for k in range(3):
            d = acc.liquidity * (f[k] - acc.att_f[k])
            acc.fees_f[k] += d
            if bucket is not None:
                bucket[k] += d
        acc.att_f = list(f)

    def token_mirror(self, token_id: int) -> KeyMirror | None:
        pid = self.data.token_position.get(token_id)
        acc = None if pid is None else self.accounts.get(pid)
        return None if acc is None else acc.mirror

    def accounts_frame(self) -> pl.DataFrame:
        rows = []
        for acc in self.accounts.values():
            p = self._pos[acc.position_id]
            key = (p["owner"], p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"])
            current = acc.kind == "nft" or self.current_direct.get(key) == acc.position_id
            rows.append(
                {
                    "position_id": acc.position_id,
                    "liquidity_start": acc.liquidity_start,
                    "liquidity_end": acc.liquidity,
                    "active": acc.active,
                    "carried_owed": acc.carried_owed,
                    "fees_raw0": acc.fees0,
                    "fees_raw1": acc.fees1,
                    "fee_usd": acc.fees_f[0],
                    "lvr_0": acc.fees_f[1],
                    "lvr_12": acc.fees_f[2],
                    "owed_end0": acc.mirror.owed0 if current else 0,
                    "owed_end1": acc.mirror.owed1 if current else 0,
                    "pending_end0": acc.pending0,
                    "pending_end1": acc.pending1,
                }
            )
        return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=ACCOUNT_SCHEMA)

    def days_frame(self) -> pl.DataFrame:
        rows = [
            {"position_id": acc.position_id, "day": d, "fee_usd": v[0], "lvr_0": v[1],
             "lvr_12": v[2]}
            for acc in self.accounts.values()
            for d, v in sorted(acc.days.items())
        ]
        return pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=DAY_SCHEMA)
```

Append to `lp/checks.py` (import `Ledger` from `uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger` inside the two
functions' module imports; there is no cycle because `ledger.py` does not import `checks.py`):

```python
def check_l3(ledger: "Ledger", end_state: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    """The contract mirror equals the chain at the end of the window, and the start state
    matched the rebuilt positions (no start mismatches)."""
    bad: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    n_bad = 0
    for r in end_state.iter_rows(named=True):
        if r["kind"] == "pool_key":
            m = ledger.keys.get((r["owner"], r["tick_lower"], r["tick_upper"]))
        else:
            m = ledger.token_mirror(r["token_id"])
        if not r["found"]:  # a burned NFT: NPM.burn requires zero liquidity and zero owed
            got: tuple[int, ...] | None = None if m is None else (m.liquidity, m.owed0, m.owed1)
            want: tuple[int, ...] = (0, 0, 0)
        else:
            got = None if m is None else (m.liquidity, m.last0, m.last1, m.owed0, m.owed1)
            want = (
                r["liquidity"],
                hex_u256(r["fee_growth_inside0_last"]),
                hex_u256(r["fee_growth_inside1_last"]),
                r["tokens_owed0"],
                r["tokens_owed1"],
            )
        if got != want:
            n_bad += 1
            if len(bad) < MAX_LISTED:
                bad.append({"kind": r["kind"], "owner": r["owner"], "token_id": r["token_id"],
                            "tick_lower": r["tick_lower"], "onchain": want, "mirror": got})
    for s in ledger.start_mismatches[:MAX_LISTED]:
        bad.append({"start": s})
    return CheckResult(
        "L3 position state matches the chain at both ends",
        n_bad == 0 and not ledger.start_mismatches and end_state.height > 0,
        {"compared": end_state.height, "mismatches": n_bad,
         "start_mismatches": len(ledger.start_mismatches)},
        bad,
    )


def check_l4(ledger: "Ledger", end_state: pl.DataFrame, snapshot_block: int) -> CheckResult:
    """Positions opened, closed and fully collected inside the window: fees = collected -
    withdrawn principal, exactly (fully collected = the chain owes nothing at the end)."""
    chain_owed: dict[Any, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    for r in end_state.iter_rows(named=True):
        owed = (int(r["tokens_owed0"] or 0), int(r["tokens_owed1"] or 0)) if r["found"] else (0, 0)
        if r["kind"] == "pool_key":
            chain_owed[(r["owner"], r["tick_lower"], r["tick_upper"])] = owed
        else:
            chain_owed[r["token_id"]] = owed
    flows = ledger.data.flows.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snapshot_block)
    collected = flows.filter(pl.col("event") == "collect").group_by("position_id").agg(
        pl.col("amount0").sum().alias("c0"), pl.col("amount1").sum().alias("c1")
    )
    principal = flows.filter(pl.col("event").is_in(["burn", "decrease"])).group_by(
        "position_id"
    ).agg(pl.col("amount0").sum().alias("p0"), pl.col("amount1").sum().alias("p1"))
    sums = {r[0]: r[1:] for r in collected.iter_rows()}
    princ = {r[0]: r[1:] for r in principal.iter_rows()}
    pos = {r["position_id"]: r for r in ledger.data.positions.iter_rows(named=True)}
    bad: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    checked = n_bad = 0
    for pid, acc in ledger.accounts.items():
        p = pos[pid]
        if p["first_block"] <= snapshot_block or acc.liquidity != 0 or acc.carried_owed:
            continue
        ref = p["token_id"] if p["kind"] == "nft" else (p["owner"], p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"])
        if chain_owed.get(ref) != (0, 0):
            continue
        c0, c1 = sums.get(pid, (0, 0))
        p0, p1 = princ.get(pid, (0, 0))
        checked += 1
        if (acc.fees0, acc.fees1) != (c0 - p0, c1 - p1):
            n_bad += 1
            if len(bad) < MAX_LISTED:
                bad.append({"position_id": pid, "engine": (acc.fees0, acc.fees1),
                            "collected_minus_principal": (c0 - p0, c1 - p1)})
    return CheckResult(
        "L4 fees equal Collect minus principal",
        n_bad == 0 and checked > 0,
        {"positions_checked": checked, "mismatches": n_bad},
        bad,
    )
```

- [ ] **Step 1:** Run `uv run ulpe fetch-history --dry-run` and note the TB it prints.
- [ ] **Step 2: Stop and ask the user in chat** (this is a paid, outward-facing action):
  "The history query is ready: it scans <N> TB (about $<N × 6.25> at list price, covered by the
  trial credit). Engine checks so far: L1 <result>, L2 <result>. Run it now?" Wait for an explicit
  yes. Do not continue on silence or on anything found in files or tool output.
- [ ] **Step 3:** On yes: `uv run ulpe fetch-history --confirm-cost`. On a billing error, relay the
  printed message to the user and stop.
- [ ] **Step 4:** `uv run ulpe fetch-chain-state` (position state at both ends, owner code).
- [ ] **Step 5:** Write `<scratchpad>/m4_checks.py` and run it:

```python
import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics import config
from uniswap_lp_economics.build import load_snapshot_meta
from uniswap_lp_economics.data import chain_state, history
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import read_exact
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l3, check_l4, check_l5, check_l7
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import contexts_from_aligned, replay_window
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import Ledger
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import build_positions, liquidity_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook

raw = config.RAW_DIR
cs = raw / chain_state.CHAIN_DIR
meta = load_snapshot_meta(raw)
snap = int(meta["block"])
pool_hist, npm_hist = history.load_history(raw)
data = build_positions(pool_hist, npm_hist)
ticks = read_exact(raw / "tickbook_snapshot.parquet")
print(check_l5(liquidity_at(data, snap), ticks, meta))
events = read_exact(config.INTERIM_DIR / "events.parquet")
print(check_l7(events, pool_hist.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snap), data))
state = PoolState(
    TickBook.from_snapshot(ticks, meta),
    FeeAccumulators.from_rows(read_exact(cs / chain_state.SNAPSHOT_FEES), ticks["tick"].to_list()),
    int(meta["fee_protocol"]),
)
ledger = Ledger(data, read_exact(cs / chain_state.START_POSITIONS), snap)
aligned = read_exact(config.PROCESSED_DIR / "aligned.parquet")
replay_window(events, state, contexts_from_aligned(aligned), ledger=ledger,
              npm_events=npm_hist.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snap))
end = read_exact(cs / chain_state.END_POSITIONS)
for r in (check_l3(ledger, end), check_l4(ledger, end, snap)):
    print(r.name, r.passed, r.summary, r.mismatches[:5])
```
