## Milestone M5 — P&L, theory, regimes, lifetime

### Task 18: Window P&L per position and by group (`lp/pnl.py`, `labels/lp_addresses.csv`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/pnl.py`
- Create: `labels/lp_addresses.csv` (header only: `address,name,category,source_url,notes`)
- Test: `tests/test_pnl.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `POSITION_SCHEMA`, `FLOW_SCHEMA` (Task 14); `ACCOUNT_SCHEMA` (Task 16);
  `get_amount0_delta`/`get_amount1_delta` (v3swap); `get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick`, `Q96` (v3math).
- Produces:
  - `WIDTH_BUCKETS`, `HOLD_BUCKETS`, `LP_TYPES`, `COHORTS`, `load_lp_labels(path) -> pl.DataFrame`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Edge(block: int, ts_us: int, sqrt_price_x96: int, p_cex: float)`
  - `width_bp(lo, hi) -> float`, `width_bucket(bp) -> str`,
    `hold_bucket(seconds: float, same_block: bool, whole_window: bool) -> str`
  - `position_amounts(liquidity, lo, hi, sqrt_price_x96) -> tuple[int, int]` (exact raw tokens)
  - `usd(amount0: float, amount1: float, price: float) -> float`
  - `gas_by_position(flows_window, events, prices: dict[int, tuple[int, float]]) -> dict[str, float]`
  - `time_weighted_value(liq_path, spans, grid) -> pl.DataFrame` (`position_id, twv`)
  - `window_pnl(positions, accounts, flows_window, prices, gas, twv: dict[str, float], vaults: set[str], has_code: dict[str, bool], start: Edge, end: Edge) -> pl.DataFrame`
    (one row per active account; columns in `PNL_COLUMNS`)
  - `bootstrap_ratio(num, den, n_boot=1000, seed=42) -> tuple[float, float]`
  - `group_table(pnl: pl.DataFrame, by: str, order: tuple[str, ...]) -> pl.DataFrame`

`prices` maps a block number to `(timestamp µs, P_cex at the block start)`; it comes from
`blocks_state.parquet` (Task 21 builds it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_pnl.py`:

```python
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import ACCOUNT_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.pnl import (
    Edge,
    group_table,
    hold_bucket,
    load_lp_labels,
    position_amounts,
    time_weighted_value,
    usd,
    width_bp,
    width_bucket,
    window_pnl,
)
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import FLOW_SCHEMA, POSITION_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import Q96
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import get_amount0_delta, get_amount1_delta

L = 10**18
US = 1_000_000
WALLET, DIRECT = "0x" + "a1" * 20, "0x" + "d1" * 20


def test_position_amounts_exact() -> None:
    lo, hi = sqrt_at(-10), sqrt_at(10)
    assert position_amounts(L, -10, 10, sqrt_at(-20)) == (get_amount0_delta(lo, hi, L, False), 0)
    assert position_amounts(L, -10, 10, sqrt_at(20)) == (0, get_amount1_delta(lo, hi, L, False))
    a0, a1 = position_amounts(L, -10, 10, sqrt_at(0))
    assert a0 == get_amount0_delta(sqrt_at(0), hi, L, False)
    assert a1 == get_amount1_delta(lo, sqrt_at(0), L, False)


def test_width_and_hold_buckets() -> None:
    assert width_bp(0, 10) == pytest.approx(10.004, rel=1e-3)
    assert [width_bucket(b) for b in (10.0, 50.0, 999.0, 1000.0, 1e9)] == [
        "<50", "50-200", "200-1000", "1000-5000", ">=5000"
    ]
    assert hold_bucket(5.0, True, False) == "same_block"
    assert hold_bucket(10 * 86400.0, False, True) == "whole_window"
    assert [hold_bucket(s, False, False) for s in (60.0, 7200.0, 3 * 86400.0, 8 * 86400.0)] == [
        "<1h", "1h-1d", "1d-7d", ">=7d"
    ]


def test_load_lp_labels_rejects_wrong_category(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    p = tmp_path / "lp.csv"
    p.write_text(
        "address,name,category,source_url,notes\n"
        f"{DIRECT},Some vault,aggregator,https://example.org,\n"
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="line 2: category"):
        load_lp_labels(p)


def pos(pid: str, kind: str, owner: str, lp: str, token: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"position_id": pid, "kind": kind, "token_id": token, "owner": owner,
            "tick_lower": -10, "tick_upper": 10, "lp": lp, "transferred": False,
            "first_block": 1, "first_ts_us": 0, "close_block": None, "close_ts_us": None}


def acc(pid: str, start: int, end: int, fees: tuple[int, int], fee_usd: float, lvr: float,
        active: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"position_id": pid, "liquidity_start": start, "liquidity_end": end,
            "active": active, "carried_owed": False, "fees_raw0": fees[0], "fees_raw1": fees[1],
            "fee_usd": fee_usd, "lvr_0": lvr, "lvr_12": lvr / 2, "owed_end0": 0, "owed_end1": 0,
            "pending_end0": 0, "pending_end1": 0}


def flow(pid: str, block: int, event: str, delta: int, a0: int, a1: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"position_id": pid, "kind": "nft" if pid.startswith("nft") else "direct",
            "token_id": None, "block_number": block, "log_index": 0, "pool_log_index": 0,
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "ts_us": block * 12 * US, "event": event,
            "liquidity_delta": delta, "amount0": a0, "amount1": a1}


PRICES = {1: (12 * US, 2000.0), 20: (240 * US, 2000.0), 25: (300 * US, 2000.0),
          30: (360 * US, 2100.0), 99: (1188 * US, 2050.0)}
START = Edge(1, 12 * US, sqrt_at(0), 2000.0)
END = Edge(99, 1188 * US, sqrt_at(0), 2050.0)


def pnl_frame(has_code: dict[str, bool] | None = None) -> pl.DataFrame:
    positions = pl.DataFrame(
        [pos("direct:a", "direct", DIRECT, DIRECT), pos("nft:1", "nft", NPM_ADDRESS, WALLET, 1),
         pos("direct:j", "direct", DIRECT, DIRECT), pos("nft:2", "nft", NPM_ADDRESS, WALLET, 2)],
        schema=POSITION_SCHEMA,
    )
    accounts = pl.DataFrame(
        [acc("direct:a", L, L, (100, 0), 5.0, 2.0), acc("nft:1", 0, 0, (50, 10**12), 1.0, 0.5),
         acc("direct:j", 0, 0, (3, 0), 0.1, 0.05), acc("nft:2", 0, 0, (0, 0), 0.0, 0.0, False)],
        schema=ACCOUNT_SCHEMA,
    )
    flows = pl.DataFrame(
        [flow("nft:1", 20, "increase", L, 1000, 10**15),
         flow("nft:1", 30, "decrease", -L, 900, 2 * 10**15),
         flow("direct:j", 25, "mint", L, 10, 10), flow("direct:j", 25, "burn", -L, 12, 9),
         flow("nft:2", 40, "collect", 0, 1, 1)],
        schema=FLOW_SCHEMA,
    )
    return window_pnl(
        positions, accounts, flows, PRICES, {"nft:1": 0.5}, {"direct:a": 100.0, "nft:1": 50.0},
        set(), {WALLET: False} if has_code is None else has_code, START, END,
    )


def test_collect_only_position_is_not_window_active() -> None:
    assert "nft:2" not in pnl_frame()["position_id"].to_list()


def test_window_pnl_rows() -> None:
    df = {r["position_id"]: r for r in pnl_frame().iter_rows(named=True)}
    a, n, j = df["direct:a"], df["nft:1"], df["direct:j"]
    assert a["cohort"] == "pre_window" and a["holding"] == "whole_window"
    assert a["pnl_rebal_usd"] == pytest.approx(3.0)
    expected = usd(900 + 50 - 1000, 2 * 10**15 + 10**12 - 10**15, 2100.0) - 0.5
    assert n["pnl_hold_usd"] == pytest.approx(expected)
    assert n["lp_type"] == "nft_wallet" and n["cohort"] == "in_window"
    assert n["bp_per_day"] == pytest.approx(
        n["pnl_rebal_usd"] / (50.0 * (360 - 240) / 86400) * 1e4
    )
    assert j["lp_type"] == "jit" and j["holding"] == "same_block"
    assert j["days_active"] == pytest.approx(12 / 86400)


def test_missing_owner_code_names_the_command() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ulpe fetch-chain-state"):
        pnl_frame(has_code={})


def test_time_weighted_value_on_an_hourly_grid() -> None:
    g0 = 0
    grid = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": [g0 + k * 3600 * US for k in range(4)],
                         "sqrt": [sqrt_at(0) / Q96] * 4, "p_cex": [2000.0] * 4})
    path = pl.DataFrame({"position_id": ["p", "q"], "ts_us": [0, 0], "liquidity": [L, L]},
                        schema={"position_id": pl.String, "ts_us": pl.Int64,
                                "liquidity": pl.Int128})
    spans = pl.DataFrame({"position_id": ["p", "q"], "tick_lower": [-10, -10],
                          "tick_upper": [10, 10], "t_start_us": [0, 0],
                          "t_exit_us": [2 * 3600 * US, 1800 * US]})
    twv = dict(time_weighted_value(path, spans, grid).iter_rows())
    exact = usd(*position_amounts(L, -10, 10, sqrt_at(0)), 2000.0)
    assert twv["p"] == pytest.approx(exact, rel=1e-9)
    assert "q" in twv  # the grid point at t = 0 lies inside q's 30 minutes


def test_group_table_ratio_and_ci() -> None:
    pnl = pl.DataFrame({
        "lp_type": ["jit", "jit", "nft_wallet"],
        "fee_usd": [1.0, 1.0, 2.0], "lvr_0": [0.5, 0.5, 1.0], "gas_usd": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        "pnl_rebal_usd": [1.0, 3.0, 1.0], "pnl_hold_usd": [1.0, -1.0, 2.0],
        "capital_days": [100.0, 100.0, 50.0],
    })
    t = group_table(pnl, "lp_type", ("jit", "lp_vault", "nft_wallet"))
    assert t["lp_type"].to_list() == ["jit", "nft_wallet"]  # empty groups are left out
    jit = t.row(0, named=True)
    assert jit["positions"] == 2 and jit["bp_per_day"] == pytest.approx(4.0 / 200.0 * 1e4)
    assert jit["share_beat_hold"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert jit["ci_lo_bp"] <= jit["bp_per_day"] <= jit["ci_hi_bp"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `labels/lp_addresses.csv` with the single line `address,name,category,source_url,notes`.

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/pnl.py`:

```python
"""Window P&L per position and by group (spec section 6).

Per active position: fees and LVR from the ledger's attribution, LP gas in the window, P&L
against a rebalancing portfolio (fees - LVR - gas) and against holding (everything the LP got
out minus everything put in, all at the exit price). Rates are bp of time-weighted position value
per day; the value is sampled hourly (plan Ruling 5).
"""

import math
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import polars as pl
from numpy.typing import NDArray

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import BLOCK_TIME_S, DECIMALS0, DECIMALS1
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import get_amount0_delta, get_amount1_delta

WIDTH_BUCKETS = ("<50", "50-200", "200-1000", "1000-5000", ">=5000")
HOLD_BUCKETS = ("same_block", "<1h", "1h-1d", "1d-7d", ">=7d", "whole_window")
LP_TYPES = ("jit", "lp_vault", "nft_wallet", "nft_contract", "direct_other")
COHORTS = ("pre_window", "in_window")
LP_LABEL_COLUMNS = ["address", "name", "category", "source_url", "notes"]
N_BOOT = 1000
SEED = 42
US = 1_000_000
HOUR_US = 3600 * US
DAY_S = 86_400
_ADDRESS = re.compile(r"^0x[0-9a-f]{40}$")


def load_lp_labels(path: Path) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Read and validate the LP vault list; a ValueError names the offending line."""
    df = pl.read_csv(path, infer_schema=False)
    if df.columns != LP_LABEL_COLUMNS:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: columns must be {LP_LABEL_COLUMNS}, got {df.columns}")
    for line, row in enumerate(df.iter_rows(named=True), start=2):
        if not _ADDRESS.match(row["address"] or ""):
            raise ValueError(f"{path} line {line}: address {row['address']!r} is not 0x + 40 hex")
        if row["category"] != "lp_vault":
            raise ValueError(f"{path} line {line}: category {row['category']!r} is not lp_vault")
        if not row["name"]:
            raise ValueError(f"{path} line {line}: name is empty")
        if not (row["source_url"] or "").startswith("https://"):
            raise ValueError(f"{path} line {line}: source_url must be an https:// link")
    dupes = sorted(set(df.filter(pl.col("address").is_duplicated())["address"].to_list()))
    if dupes:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate addresses {dupes}")
    return df


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Edge:
    block: int
    ts_us: int
    sqrt_price_x96: int
    p_cex: float


def width_bp(lo: int, hi: int) -> float:
    return (1.0001 ** (hi - lo) - 1.0) * 1e4


def width_bucket(bp: float) -> str:
    for limit, name in zip((50, 200, 1000, 5000), WIDTH_BUCKETS, strict=False):
        if bp < limit:
            return name
    return WIDTH_BUCKETS[-1]


def hold_bucket(seconds: float, same_block: bool, whole_window: bool) -> str:
    if same_block:
        return "same_block"
    if whole_window:
        return "whole_window"
    if seconds < 3600:
        return "<1h"
    if seconds < DAY_S:
        return "1h-1d"
    if seconds < 7 * DAY_S:
        return "1d-7d"
    return ">=7d"


def position_amounts(liquidity: int, lo: int, hi: int, sqrt_price_x96: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Raw token amounts a position holds at a pool price (rounded down, as burn pays)."""
    sa, sb = get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick(lo), get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick(hi)
    if sqrt_price_x96 <= sa:
        return get_amount0_delta(sa, sb, liquidity, False), 0
    if sqrt_price_x96 < sb:
        return (
            get_amount0_delta(sqrt_price_x96, sb, liquidity, False),
            get_amount1_delta(sa, sqrt_price_x96, liquidity, False),
        )
    return 0, get_amount1_delta(sa, sb, liquidity, False)


def usd(amount0: float, amount1: float, price: float) -> float:
    return amount0 / 10**DECIMALS0 + amount1 / 10**DECIMALS1 * price


def gas_by_position(
    flows_window: pl.DataFrame, events: pl.DataFrame, prices: dict[int, tuple[int, float]]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Gas of each window transaction that changed a position, split equally across the
    positions it touched, at the Binance price of its block."""
    txs = events.group_by("tx_hash").agg(
        pl.col("block_number").first(),
        pl.col("receipt_gas_used").first(),
        pl.col("receipt_effective_gas_price").first(),
    )
    cost = {
        tx: int(g or 0) * int(p or 0) / 1e18 * prices[b][1]
        for tx, b, g, p in txs.iter_rows()
        if b in prices
    }
    out: dict[str, float] = {}
    for tx, pids in flows_window.group_by("tx_hash").agg(pl.col("position_id").unique()).iter_rows():
        share = cost.get(tx, 0.0) / len(pids)
        for pid in pids:
            out[pid] = out.get(pid, 0.0) + share
    return out


def time_weighted_value(
    liq_path: pl.DataFrame, spans: pl.DataFrame, grid: pl.DataFrame
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Mean USD value over the hourly grid points in [t_start, t_exit) of each position.

    liq_path: position_id, ts_us, liquidity (after each change). spans: position_id,
    tick_lower, tick_upper, t_start_us, t_exit_us. grid: ts_us (hourly), sqrt (sqrtPrice/2^96 as
    a float), p_cex. Positions without a grid point are left out (the caller falls back)."""
    g0 = int(grid["ts_us"].min())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    n = grid.height
    idx = spans.with_columns(
        first=((pl.col("t_start_us") - g0 + HOUR_US - 1) // HOUR_US).clip(lower_bound=0),
        last=((pl.col("t_exit_us") - g0 - 1) // HOUR_US).clip(upper_bound=n - 1),
    ).filter(pl.col("last") >= pl.col("first"))
    points = (
        idx.with_columns(k=pl.int_ranges("first", pl.col("last") + 1))
        .explode("k")
        .with_columns(ts_us=g0 + pl.col("k") * HOUR_US)
        .sort("ts_us")
    )
    path = liq_path.with_columns(pl.col("liquidity").cast(pl.Float64)).sort("ts_us")
    joined = points.join_asof(path, on="ts_us", by="position_id", strategy="backward").join(
        grid, on="ts_us", how="left"
    )
    sa = pl.lit(1.0001).pow(pl.col("tick_lower") / 2)
    sb = pl.lit(1.0001).pow(pl.col("tick_upper") / 2)
    sp = pl.col("sqrt").clip(sa, sb)
    liq = pl.col("liquidity").fill_null(0.0)
    value = liq * (1 / sp - 1 / sb) / 10**DECIMALS0 + liq * (sp - sa) / 10**DECIMALS1 * pl.col(
        "p_cex"
    )
    return joined.group_by("position_id").agg(twv=value.mean())


PNL_COLUMNS = [
    "position_id", "kind", "lp", "lp_type", "cohort", "tick_lower", "tick_upper", "width_bp",
    "width_bucket", "holding", "start_block", "exit_block", "days_active", "twv_usd",
    "capital_days", "fee_usd", "lvr_0", "lvr_12", "gas_usd", "pnl_rebal_usd", "pnl_rebal_12_usd",
    "pnl_hold_usd", "bp_per_day", "bp_per_day_hold",
]


def window_pnl(
    positions: pl.DataFrame,
    accounts: pl.DataFrame,
    flows_window: pl.DataFrame,
    prices: dict[int, tuple[int, float]],
    gas: dict[str, float],
    twv: dict[str, float],
    vaults: set[str],
    has_code: dict[str, bool],
    start: Edge,
    end: Edge,
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    pos = {r["position_id"]: r for r in positions.iter_rows(named=True)}
    by_pos: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for f in flows_window.sort("block_number", "log_index").iter_rows(named=True):
        by_pos.setdefault(f["position_id"], []).append(f)
    rows = []
    for a in accounts.filter(pl.col("active")).iter_rows(named=True):
        pid = a["position_id"]
        p = pos[pid]
        lo, hi = p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"]
        fl = by_pos.get(pid, [])
        dep0 = dep1 = wd0 = wd1 = 0
        open_at_start = a["liquidity_start"] > 0
        if open_at_start:
            d0, d1 = position_amounts(a["liquidity_start"], lo, hi, start.sqrt_price_x96)
            dep0, dep1, t_start, b_start = d0, d1, start.ts_us, start.block
        else:
            first = next(f for f in fl if f["liquidity_delta"] > 0)
            t_start, b_start = first["ts_us"], first["block_number"]
        for f in fl:
            if f["event"] in ("mint", "increase"):
                dep0 += f["amount0"]
                dep1 += f["amount1"]
            elif f["event"] in ("burn", "decrease"):
                wd0 += f["amount0"]
                wd1 += f["amount1"]
        liq_end = a["liquidity_end"]
        if liq_end > 0:
            e0, e1 = position_amounts(liq_end, lo, hi, end.sqrt_price_x96)
            exit_block, exit_ts, p_exit = end.block, end.ts_us, end.p_cex
        else:
            last = [f for f in fl if f["liquidity_delta"] < 0][-1]
            e0 = e1 = 0
            exit_block, exit_ts = last["block_number"], last["ts_us"]
            p_exit = prices[exit_block][1]
        g = gas.get(pid, 0.0)
        pnl_hold = usd(
            wd0 + e0 + a["fees_raw0"] - dep0, wd1 + e1 + a["fees_raw1"] - dep1, p_exit
        ) - g
        jit = not open_at_start and b_start == exit_block and liq_end == 0
        days = BLOCK_TIME_S / DAY_S if jit else max(exit_ts - t_start, 0) / (DAY_S * US)
        tw = twv.get(pid)
        if tw is None:  # no hourly grid point while open (plan Ruling 5)
            tw = usd(dep0, dep1, prices[b_start][1])
        lp, kind = p["lp"], p["kind"]
        if jit:
            lp_type = "jit"
        elif lp in vaults:
            lp_type = "lp_vault"
        elif kind == "nft":
            if lp not in has_code:
                raise ValueError(f"no owner code for {lp}; run `ulpe fetch-chain-state`")
            lp_type = "nft_contract" if has_code[lp] else "nft_wallet"
        else:
            lp_type = "direct_other"
        w = width_bp(lo, hi)
        capital = tw * days
        rebal = a["fee_usd"] - a["lvr_0"] - g
        rows.append(
            {
                "position_id": pid, "kind": kind, "lp": lp, "lp_type": lp_type,
                "cohort": "pre_window" if open_at_start else "in_window",
                "tick_lower": lo, "tick_upper": hi, "width_bp": w, "width_bucket": width_bucket(w),
                "holding": hold_bucket(
                    (exit_ts - t_start) / US, jit, open_at_start and liq_end > 0
                ),
                "start_block": b_start, "exit_block": exit_block, "days_active": days,
                "twv_usd": tw, "capital_days": capital, "fee_usd": a["fee_usd"],
                "lvr_0": a["lvr_0"], "lvr_12": a["lvr_12"], "gas_usd": g,
                "pnl_rebal_usd": rebal, "pnl_rebal_12_usd": a["fee_usd"] - a["lvr_12"] - g,
                "pnl_hold_usd": pnl_hold,
                "bp_per_day": rebal / capital * 1e4 if capital > 0 else None,
                "bp_per_day_hold": pnl_hold / capital * 1e4 if capital > 0 else None,
            }
        )
    return pl.DataFrame(rows).select(PNL_COLUMNS) if rows else pl.DataFrame(
        schema=dict.fromkeys(PNL_COLUMNS, pl.Float64)
    )


def bootstrap_ratio(
    num: NDArray[np.float64], den: NDArray[np.float64], n_boot: int = N_BOOT, seed: int = SEED
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """95% interval of sum(num) / sum(den) x 1e4, resampling positions."""
    n = len(num)
    if n == 0:
        return math.nan, math.nan
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stats = np.empty(n_boot)
    for b in range(n_boot):
        idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
        d = den[idx].sum()
        stats[b] = num[idx].sum() / d * 1e4 if d > 0 else np.nan
    if np.isnan(stats).all():
        return math.nan, math.nan
    lo, hi = np.nanpercentile(stats, [2.5, 97.5])
    return float(lo), float(hi)


def group_table(pnl: pl.DataFrame, by: str, order: tuple[str, ...]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    rows = []
    for value in order:
        g = pnl.filter(pl.col(by) == value)
        if g.height == 0:
            continue
        capital = float(g["capital_days"].sum())
        rebal = float(g["pnl_rebal_usd"].sum())
        hold = float(g["pnl_hold_usd"].sum())
        lo, hi = bootstrap_ratio(
            g["pnl_rebal_usd"].to_numpy().astype(np.float64),
            g["capital_days"].to_numpy().astype(np.float64),
        )
        rows.append(
            {
                by: value,
                "positions": g.height,
                "fees_usd": float(g["fee_usd"].sum()),
                "lvr_0_usd": float(g["lvr_0"].sum()),
                "gas_usd": float(g["gas_usd"].sum()),
                "pnl_rebal_usd": rebal,
                "pnl_hold_usd": hold,
                "share_beat_hold": float((g["pnl_hold_usd"] > 0).mean()),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                "bp_per_day": rebal / capital * 1e4 if capital > 0 else None,
                "ci_lo_bp": lo,
                "ci_hi_bp": hi,
                "bp_per_day_hold": hold / capital * 1e4 if capital > 0 else None,
            }
        )
    return pl.DataFrame(rows)
```

### Task 19: Theoretical LVR, `P_trade` and volatility regimes (`lp/theory.py`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/theory.py`
- Test: `tests/test_theory.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `align.reference_at`; the engine's steps frame (`STEP_SCHEMA`, Task 11);
  `blocks_state.parquet` columns `block_number, ts_us, liquidity, p_cex`; aligned `block_timestamp,
  protocol_fee_usd`.
- Produces:
  - `GAMMA = math.log1p(0.0005)`, `LAMBDA = 1 / 12`, `p_trade(sigma_per_sqrt_s: float, gamma=GAMMA, lam=LAMBDA) -> float`
  - `price_grid(ref, start_us, end_us, step_s, data_end_us) -> pl.DataFrame` (`ts_us, price`)
  - `interval_theory(grid, blocks_state) -> pl.DataFrame` (`ts_us, block_number, r2, lvr_theory`)
  - `daily_theory(int_1s, int_1m) -> pl.DataFrame` (`day, rv_1s, sigma_annual, theory_1s,
    theory_1m, p_trade, theory_arb, theory_arb_fees`)
  - `measured_daily(steps, aligned) -> pl.DataFrame` (`day, fees_usd, protocol_fee_usd, lvr_0,
    lvr_12, lvr_0_searcher, fees_searcher`)
  - `regimes(daily) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]` (daily with `regime`, and a per-regime summary)
  - `regime_by_group(days, groups, daily) -> pl.DataFrame` (Σ fees, LVR and fees − LVR per regime and LP type)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_theory.py`:

```python
import math
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.theory import (
    GAMMA,
    LAMBDA,
    daily_theory,
    interval_theory,
    measured_daily,
    p_trade,
    regime_by_group,
    regimes,
)

US = 1_000_000


def test_p_trade_matches_the_paper() -> None:
    assert p_trade(math.sqrt(2 * LAMBDA) * GAMMA) == pytest.approx(0.5)  # eta = 1
    assert p_trade(0.0) == 0.0
    assert p_trade(1.0) == pytest.approx(1 / (1 + math.sqrt(2 * LAMBDA) * GAMMA))
    assert GAMMA == pytest.approx(math.log(1.0005))


def test_full_range_theory_is_sigma2_over_8_times_value() -> None:
    liq, price = 10**24, 2000.0  # raw liquidity; L_dec = 1e12
    grid = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": [0, US, 2 * US], "price": [price, price * 1.001, price]})
    state = pl.DataFrame({"block_number": [1], "ts_us": [0], "liquidity": [liq], "p_cex": [price]},
                         schema={"block_number": pl.Int64, "ts_us": pl.Int64,
                                 "liquidity": pl.Int128, "p_cex": pl.Float64})
    t = interval_theory(grid, state)
    assert t.height == 2
    r2 = float(t["r2"].sum())
    value = 2 * liq * 1e-12 * math.sqrt(price)  # full-range pool value, USD
    # Each interval uses its own start price; the second starts at price * 1.001.
    expected = sum(
        float(r) * liq * 1e-12 * math.sqrt(p) / 4
        for r, p in zip(t["r2"].to_list(), [price, price * 1.001], strict=True)
    )
    assert float(t["lvr_theory"].sum()) == pytest.approx(expected)
    assert float(t["lvr_theory"].head(1).item()) == pytest.approx(
        float(t["r2"].head(1).item()) / 8 * value
    )
    assert r2 > 0


def test_daily_theory_and_regimes() -> None:
    days = [date(2026, 7, d) for d in range(1, 10)]
    ts = [int(datetime(2026, 7, d, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp()) * US for d in range(1, 10)]
    one = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": ts, "block_number": list(range(9)),
                        "r2": [1e-4 * (i + 1) for i in range(9)], "lvr_theory": [10.0] * 9})
    d = daily_theory(one, one)
    assert d["day"].to_list() == days
    row = d.row(0, named=True)
    assert row["sigma_annual"] == pytest.approx(math.sqrt(1e-4 * 365))
    assert row["theory_arb"] == pytest.approx(10.0 * row["p_trade"])
    tagged, summary = regimes(d)
    assert tagged["regime"].to_list() == ["low"] * 3 + ["mid"] * 3 + ["high"] * 3
    assert summary["regime"].to_list() == ["low", "mid", "high"]


def test_measured_daily_splits_searcher_flow() -> None:
    t = int(datetime(2026, 7, 1, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp()) * US
    steps = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": [t, t, t + 86400 * US], "flow_label": ["searcher", "x", "x"],
                          "fee_usd": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0], "lvr_0": [4.0, -1.0, 0.5],
                          "lvr_12": [4.0, -1.0, 0.5]})
    aligned = pl.DataFrame(
        {"block_timestamp": [datetime(2026, 7, 1, tzinfo=UTC)], "protocol_fee_usd": [0.25]}
    )
    m = measured_daily(steps, aligned)
    first = m.row(0, named=True)
    assert (first["fees_usd"], first["lvr_0"], first["lvr_0_searcher"]) == (3.0, 3.0, 4.0)
    assert first["fees_searcher"] == 1.0 and first["protocol_fee_usd"] == 0.25
    assert m.row(1, named=True)["protocol_fee_usd"] == 0.0


def test_regime_by_group() -> None:
    days = pl.DataFrame({"position_id": ["a", "a", "b"],
                         "day": [date(2026, 7, 1), date(2026, 7, 2), date(2026, 7, 1)],
                         "fee_usd": [1.0, 2.0, 4.0], "lvr_0": [0.5, 0.5, 1.0],
                         "lvr_12": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]})
    groups = pl.DataFrame({"position_id": ["a", "b"], "lp_type": ["jit", "nft_wallet"]})
    daily = pl.DataFrame({"day": [date(2026, 7, 1), date(2026, 7, 2)], "regime": ["low", "high"]})
    t = regime_by_group(days, groups, daily)
    low_jit = t.filter((pl.col("regime") == "low") & (pl.col("lp_type") == "jit")).row(0, named=True)
    assert low_jit["fees_minus_lvr_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/theory.py`:

```python
"""Theoretical LVR and volatility regimes (spec section 5).

Concentrated liquidity L at price p loses LVR at sigma^2 * L_dec * sqrt(p) / 4 per unit time
(full range: sigma^2/8 x pool value). Over each grid interval the realised r^2 of the Binance
price replaces sigma^2 dt. With fees and discrete blocks, arbitrage happens only in a fraction
P_trade = 1 / (1 + eta), eta = sqrt(2 lambda) gamma / sigma of blocks (Milionis, Moallemi,
Roughgarden 2023, arXiv:2305.14604, Theorem 1 and Eq. 12): expected arbitrage profit is about
LVR x P_trade and the fees it pays about LVR x (1 - P_trade).
"""

import math

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.align import reference_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.config import BLOCK_TIME_S, DECIMALS0, DECIMALS1, FEE_PIPS

GAMMA = math.log1p(FEE_PIPS / 1_000_000)
LAMBDA = 1 / BLOCK_TIME_S
US = 1_000_000
DAY_S = 86_400
L_DEC = 10.0 ** (-(DECIMALS0 + DECIMALS1) / 2)  # raw liquidity -> sqrt(USDC x WETH) units
REGIMES = ("low", "mid", "high")


def p_trade(sigma_per_sqrt_s: float, gamma: float = GAMMA, lam: float = LAMBDA) -> float:
    if sigma_per_sqrt_s <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.sqrt(2 * lam) * gamma / sigma_per_sqrt_s)


def price_grid(
    ref: pl.DataFrame, start_us: int, end_us: int, step_s: int, data_end_us: int
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """The reference price as of every step in [start_us, end_us]."""
    times = pl.Series("ts_us", list(range(start_us, end_us + 1, step_s * US)), dtype=pl.Int64)
    p = reference_at(times, ref, data_end_us)["price"]
    return pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": times, "price": p})


def _day(col: str) -> pl.Expr:
    return pl.from_epoch(pl.col(col), time_unit="us").dt.date().alias("day")


def interval_theory(grid: pl.DataFrame, blocks_state: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Per grid interval: r^2 of the price and r^2 x L_dec x sqrt(p) / 4, with the active
    liquidity of the block in force at the interval start."""
    g = (
        grid.sort("ts_us")
        .with_columns(r=(pl.col("price").shift(-1) / pl.col("price")).log())
        .filter(pl.col("r").is_not_null() & pl.col("r").is_finite())
    )
    state = blocks_state.select(
        "ts_us", "block_number", liq=pl.col("liquidity").cast(pl.Float64)
    ).sort("ts_us")
    j = g.join_asof(state, on="ts_us", strategy="backward")
    return j.select(
        "ts_us",
        "block_number",
        r2=pl.col("r") ** 2,
        lvr_theory=pl.col("r") ** 2 * pl.col("liq") * L_DEC * pl.col("price").sqrt() / 4,
    )


def daily_theory(int_1s: pl.DataFrame, int_1m: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    one = int_1s.group_by(_day("ts_us")).agg(
        rv_1s=pl.col("r2").sum(), theory_1s=pl.col("lvr_theory").sum()
    )
    minute = int_1m.group_by(_day("ts_us")).agg(theory_1m=pl.col("lvr_theory").sum())
    d = one.join(minute, on="day", how="left").sort("day")
    pt = [p_trade(math.sqrt(rv / DAY_S)) for rv in d["rv_1s"].to_list()]
    return d.with_columns(
        sigma_annual=(pl.col("rv_1s") * 365).sqrt(),
        p_trade=pl.Series(pt, dtype=pl.Float64),
    ).with_columns(
        theory_arb=pl.col("theory_1s") * pl.col("p_trade"),
        theory_arb_fees=pl.col("theory_1s") * (1 - pl.col("p_trade")),
    ).select(
        "day", "rv_1s", "sigma_annual", "theory_1s", "theory_1m", "p_trade", "theory_arb",
        "theory_arb_fees",
    )


def measured_daily(steps: pl.DataFrame, aligned: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    searcher = pl.col("flow_label") == "searcher"
    m = steps.group_by(_day("ts_us")).agg(
        fees_usd=pl.col("fee_usd").sum(),
        lvr_0=pl.col("lvr_0").sum(),
        lvr_12=pl.col("lvr_12").sum(),
        lvr_0_searcher=pl.col("lvr_0").filter(searcher).sum(),
        fees_searcher=pl.col("fee_usd").filter(searcher).sum(),
    )
    proto = aligned.group_by(day=pl.col("block_timestamp").dt.date()).agg(
        protocol_fee_usd=pl.col("protocol_fee_usd").sum()
    )
    return (
        m.join(proto, on="day", how="left")
        .with_columns(pl.col("protocol_fee_usd").fill_null(0.0))
        .sort("day")
        .select("day", "fees_usd", "protocol_fee_usd", "lvr_0", "lvr_12", "lvr_0_searcher",
                "fees_searcher")
    )


def regimes(daily: pl.DataFrame) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    """Terciles of daily realised volatility (by rank), and per-regime totals."""
    n = daily.height
    rank = pl.col("sigma_annual").rank("ordinal")
    tagged = daily.with_columns(
        regime=pl.when(rank <= n / 3)
        .then(pl.lit("low"))
        .when(rank <= 2 * n / 3)
        .then(pl.lit("mid"))
        .otherwise(pl.lit("high"))
    )
    cols = [c for c in ("fees_usd", "lvr_0", "theory_1s", "theory_arb") if c in tagged.columns]
    summary = tagged.group_by("regime").agg(
        days=pl.len(),
        sigma_annual_mean=pl.col("sigma_annual").mean(),
        **{c: pl.col(c).sum() for c in cols},
    )
    if {"fees_usd", "lvr_0"} <= set(cols):
        summary = summary.with_columns(fees_minus_lvr_usd=pl.col("fees_usd") - pl.col("lvr_0"))
    order = pl.DataFrame({"regime": list(REGIMES), "_o": [0, 1, 2]})
    return tagged, summary.join(order, on="regime").sort("_o").drop("_o")


def regime_by_group(days: pl.DataFrame, groups: pl.DataFrame, daily: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Fees, LVR and fees - LVR per volatility regime and LP type, from per-day attribution."""
    return (
        days.join(groups, on="position_id", how="inner")
        .join(daily.select("day", "regime"), on="day", how="inner")
        .group_by("regime", "lp_type")
        .agg(fees_usd=pl.col("fee_usd").sum(), lvr_0_usd=pl.col("lvr_0").sum())
        .with_columns(fees_minus_lvr_usd=pl.col("fees_usd") - pl.col("lvr_0_usd"))
        .sort("regime", "lp_type")
    )
```

In `test_daily_theory_and_regimes`, `daily_theory` gets the same frame for both grids; the
`regimes` call needs `fees_usd`/`lvr_0` only when present (it is called with the joined measured +
theory frame in Task 21).

```python
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import ACCOUNT_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.lifetime import life_bucket, lifetime_summary, lifetime_table
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.pnl import Edge, position_amounts, usd
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import FLOW_SCHEMA, POSITION_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at

L = 10**18
US = 1_000_000
DAY = 86_400 * US
WINDOW_START = 400 * DAY


def flow(pid: str, block: int, ts: int, event: str, delta: int, a0: int, a1: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"position_id": pid, "kind": "nft", "token_id": 1, "block_number": block,
            "log_index": 0, "pool_log_index": 0, "tx_hash": "0x", "ts_us": ts, "event": event,
            "liquidity_delta": delta, "amount0": a0, "amount1": a1}


def test_lifetime_of_a_position_opened_before_the_window() -> None:
    positions = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"position_id": "nft:1", "kind": "nft", "token_id": 1, "owner": NPM_ADDRESS,
          "tick_lower": -10, "tick_upper": 10, "lp": "0x" + "a1" * 20, "transferred": False,
          "first_block": 5, "first_ts_us": 10 * DAY, "close_block": None, "close_ts_us": None}],
        schema=POSITION_SCHEMA,
    )
    flows = pl.DataFrame(
        [flow("nft:1", 5, 10 * DAY, "increase", L, 1000, 10**15),
         flow("nft:1", 7, 20 * DAY, "collect", 0, 30, 0)],
        schema=FLOW_SCHEMA,
    )
    accounts = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"position_id": "nft:1", "liquidity_start": L, "liquidity_end": L, "active": True,
          "carried_owed": True, "fees_raw0": 0, "fees_raw1": 0, "fee_usd": 0.0, "lvr_0": 0.0,
          "lvr_12": 0.0, "owed_end0": 4, "owed_end1": 0, "pending_end0": 6, "pending_end1": 0}],
        schema=ACCOUNT_SCHEMA,
    )
    minute = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": [9 * DAY], "price": [1500.0]})
    end = Edge(900, 410 * DAY, sqrt_at(0), 2000.0)
    life = lifetime_table(positions, flows, accounts, ["nft:1"], {}, minute, WINDOW_START, end)
    row = life.row(0, named=True)
    e0, e1 = position_amounts(L, -10, 10, sqrt_at(0))
    expected = usd(30 + 4 + 6 + e0 - 1000, e1 - 10**15, 2000.0)
    assert row["pnl_hold_life_usd"] == pytest.approx(expected)
    assert row["deposits_usd"] == pytest.approx(usd(1000, 10**15, 1500.0))
    assert row["life_bucket"] == ">=180d"
    overall, by_width, by_life = lifetime_summary(life)
    assert overall["positions"] == 1
    assert by_life["life_bucket"].to_list() == [">=180d"]


def test_life_bucket() -> None:

                                     200 * 86400.0)] == ["<1d", "1-7d", "7-30d", "30-180d",
                                                         ">=180d"]
```

```python
def kline_reference_table(klines: pl.DataFrame, bar_s: int = 1) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Klines as a reference. A bar's close is only known when the bar ends."""
    return klines.select(
        ts_us=pl.col("ts_us") + bar_s * US, price=pl.col("close")
    ).sort("ts_us")
```

`data/binance.py`:

```python
def fetch_price_history(months: list[tuple[int, int]], raw_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
    """ETHUSDT 1-minute klines (spec D7): USD prices for deposits made before the window."""
    out = []
    with httpx.Client(timeout=120, follow_redirects=True) as client:
        for year, month in months:
            url = klines_url("ETHUSDT", "1m", year, month)
            dest = raw_dir / "binance" / url.rsplit("/", 1)[1]
            out.append(download_verified(url, dest, client))
    print(f"  {len(out)} monthly 1-minute files")
    return out


def load_minute_klines(raw_dir: Path) -> pl.DataFrame:
    files = sorted((raw_dir / "binance").glob("ETHUSDT-1m-*.zip"))
    if not files:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"no ETHUSDT 1-minute klines in {raw_dir / 'binance'}; run `ulpe fetch-price-history`"
        )
    return pl.concat([parse_klines_close(p) for p in files]).sort("ts_us")
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/lifetime.py`:

```python
"""Lifetime P&L against holding (spec section 6), for every window-active position.

Everything the LP got out (collected tokens, tokens still owed and accrued at the window end,
tokens still in the position) minus everything put in, valued at the exit price. Collected
amounts include withdrawn principal, so withdrawals are not added again. Deposits are also
valued at their own time for the lifetime return (Binance 1-minute closes before the window).
"""

from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.align import reference_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.pnl import Edge, position_amounts, usd, width_bp, width_bucket

LIFE_BUCKETS = ("<1d", "1-7d", "7-30d", "30-180d", ">=180d")
US = 1_000_000
DAY_S = 86_400


def life_bucket(seconds: float) -> str:
    for limit, name in zip((1, 7, 30, 180), LIFE_BUCKETS, strict=False):
        if seconds < limit * DAY_S:
            return name
    return LIFE_BUCKETS[-1]


def lifetime_table(
    positions: pl.DataFrame,
    flows: pl.DataFrame,
    accounts: pl.DataFrame,
    active_ids: list[str],
    prices: dict[int, tuple[int, float]],
    minute_ref: pl.DataFrame,
    window_start_us: int,
    end: Edge,
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    pos = {r["position_id"]: r for r in positions.iter_rows(named=True)}
    acc = {r["position_id"]: r for r in accounts.iter_rows(named=True)}
    fl = flows.filter(pl.col("position_id").is_in(active_ids)).sort("block_number", "log_index")
    deposits = fl.filter(pl.col("event").is_in(["mint", "increase"]))
    pre = reference_at(deposits["ts_us"], minute_ref, window_start_us)["price"].to_list()
    dep_price = [
        p if t < window_start_us else prices.get(b, (0, None))[1]
        for t, b, p in zip(
            deposits["ts_us"].to_list(), deposits["block_number"].to_list(), pre, strict=True
        )
    ]
    deposits = deposits.with_columns(price=pl.Series(dep_price, dtype=pl.Float64))
    by_pos: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for f in fl.iter_rows(named=True):
        by_pos.setdefault(f["position_id"], []).append(f)
    dep_by: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for d in deposits.iter_rows(named=True):
        dep_by.setdefault(d["position_id"], []).append(d)
    rows = []
    for pid in active_ids:
        p, a, f = pos[pid], acc[pid], by_pos.get(pid, [])
        lo, hi = p["tick_lower"], p["tick_upper"]
        d0 = sum(x["amount0"] for x in dep_by.get(pid, []))
        d1 = sum(x["amount1"] for x in dep_by.get(pid, []))
        dep_usd = sum(
            usd(x["amount0"], x["amount1"], x["price"])
            for x in dep_by.get(pid, [])
            if x["price"] is not None
        )
        c0 = sum(x["amount0"] for x in f if x["event"] == "collect")
        c1 = sum(x["amount1"] for x in f if x["event"] == "collect")
        o0 = a["owed_end0"] + a["pending_end0"]
        o1 = a["owed_end1"] + a["pending_end1"]
        if a["liquidity_end"] > 0:
            e0, e1 = position_amounts(a["liquidity_end"], lo, hi, end.sqrt_price_x96)
            exit_ts, p_exit = end.ts_us, end.p_cex
        else:
            last = [x for x in f if x["liquidity_delta"] < 0][-1]
            e0 = e1 = 0
            exit_ts, p_exit = last["ts_us"], prices[last["block_number"]][1]
        pnl = usd(c0 + o0 + e0 - d0, c1 + o1 + e1 - d1, p_exit)
        life_s = (exit_ts - p["first_ts_us"]) / US
        w = width_bp(lo, hi)
        rows.append(
            {
                "position_id": pid,
                "kind": p["kind"],
                "width_bucket": width_bucket(w),
                "life_s": life_s,
                "life_bucket": life_bucket(life_s),
                "deposits_usd": dep_usd,
                "pnl_hold_life_usd": pnl,
                "return_life": pnl / dep_usd if dep_usd > 0 else None,
                "beat_hold": pnl > 0,
            }
        )
    return pl.DataFrame(rows)


def lifetime_summary(life: pl.DataFrame) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    def table(by: str, order: tuple[str, ...]) -> pl.DataFrame:
        t = life.group_by(by).agg(
            positions=pl.len(),
            share_beat_hold=pl.col("beat_hold").mean(),
            median_return=pl.col("return_life").median(),
        )
        o = pl.DataFrame({by: list(order), "_o": list(range(len(order)))})
        return t.join(o, on=by).sort("_o").drop("_o")

    from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.pnl import WIDTH_BUCKETS

    overall = {
        "positions": life.height,
        "share_beat_hold": float(life["beat_hold"].mean()) if life.height else None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        "median_return": life["return_life"].median() if life.height else None,
    }
    return overall, table("width_bucket", WIDTH_BUCKETS), table("life_bucket", LIFE_BUCKETS)
```

(Move the `WIDTH_BUCKETS` import to the top of the module; it is shown inline only for clarity.)

- Create: `tests/lpworld.py` (synthetic world for the end-to-end test)
- Test: `tests/test_lp_analyze.py`, `tests/test_lp_figures.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 5–20.
- Produces:
  - `pnl.position_spans(positions, accounts, flows_window, start: Edge, end: Edge) -> pl.DataFrame`
    (`position_id, tick_lower, tick_upper, t_start_us, t_exit_us`, active accounts only)
  - `pnl.liquidity_path(accounts, flows_window, start: Edge) -> pl.DataFrame` (`position_id, ts_us, liquidity`)
  - `lp.checks.check_l6(steps, accounts, aligned, swap_fees, n_window_flows: int) -> CheckResult`
  - `lp.figures`: `fees_vs_lvr_daily(daily, path)`, `measured_vs_theory(daily, path)`,
    `regime_chart(summary, path)`, `group_chart(groups, path)`, `lifetime_chart(by_width, by_life, path)`
  - `lp.analyze`: `@dataclass LpInputs`, `load_lp_inputs(raw, interim, processed, lp_labels_csv) -> LpInputs`,
    `lp_analysis(inp, results_dir, processed_dir, provenance, end_us=None) -> str`, `run_lp() -> str`,
    `LP_FIGURES` (file names 7–11)
  - CLI `ulpe lp`

**Plan Ruling 13 (L6 tolerance):** position raw fees floor at every checkpoint, not only per step,
so Σ position fees may fall short of the pool's LP fees by at most one raw unit per accrued step plus
one per window flow plus two per account (start and end); it can never exceed them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/lpworld.py` (a synthetic window: one position open before the window, one NFT
position opened, poked-free closed and collected inside it, one just-in-time position, swaps on two
UTC days; chain-state frames are derived from an "oracle" run of the same engine, so this test
exercises the plumbing — the real chain checks are L2/L3 on real data):

```python
"""A small synthetic pool and window for the `ulpe lp` end-to-end test."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import NPM_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.data.chain_state import FEE_SCHEMA, POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import u256_hex
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.analyze import LpInputs
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import contexts_from_aligned, replay_window
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import Ledger
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import build_positions
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.swapfees import build_swap_fees
from uniswap_lp_economics.replay import iter_block_starts
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import get_sqrt_ratio_at_tick as sqrt_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import sqrt_price_x96_to_eth_price
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3swap import swap

T = 200_310  # ~2,000 USD per ETH
L = 10**18
SNAP = 5
T0 = datetime(2026, 7, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
US = 1_000_000
D, J = "0x" + "d1" * 20, "0x" + "e1" * 20
WALLET, ZERO = "0x" + "a1" * 20, "0x" + "00" * 20
EVENT_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "transaction_index": pl.Int64, "event": pl.String, "owner": pl.String,
    "sender": pl.String, "to_address": pl.String, "tick_lower": pl.Int64, "tick_upper": pl.Int64,
    "amount": pl.Int128, "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128, "tick": pl.Int64, "liquidity": pl.Int128,
    "receipt_gas_used": pl.Int64, "receipt_effective_gas_price": pl.Int64,
}
NPM_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64, "block_timestamp": pl.Datetime("us", "UTC"),
    "tx_hash": pl.String, "transaction_index": pl.Int64, "event": pl.String,
    "token_id": pl.Int64, "liquidity": pl.Int128, "amount0": pl.Int128, "amount1": pl.Int128,
    "recipient": pl.String, "from_owner": pl.String, "to_owner": pl.String,
}


def ts(block: int) -> datetime:
    if block <= SNAP:
        return T0 - timedelta(seconds=12 * (SNAP + 1 - block))
    if block >= 11:
        return T0 + timedelta(days=1, seconds=12 * (block - 11))
    return T0 + timedelta(seconds=12 * (block - SNAP))


def ev(block: int, log: int, event: str, **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"block_number": block, "log_index": log, "block_timestamp": ts(block),
            "tx_hash": f"0x{block:064x}", "transaction_index": 0, "event": event,
            "receipt_gas_used": 100_000, "receipt_effective_gas_price": 10**9, **kw}


def snapshot_book() -> TickBook:
    b = TickBook(tick=T, sqrt_price_x96=sqrt_at(T), liquidity=0)
    b.apply_mint(T - 10, T + 10, L)
    return b


class World:
    """Builds the events; chain-state frames come from an oracle replay."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.book = snapshot_book()
        self.pool: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            ev(1, 0, "mint", owner=D, tick_lower=T - 10, tick_upper=T + 10, amount=L,
               amount0=10**9, amount1=10**18)
        ]
        self.npm: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def swap(self, block: int, log: int, usdc_in: int) -> None:
        r = swap(self.book, True, usdc_in)
        self.pool.append(ev(block, log, "swap", sender=J, to_address=J, amount0=r.amount_in,
                            amount1=-r.amount_out, sqrt_price_x96=r.sqrt_price_x96, tick=r.tick,
                            liquidity=r.liquidity))
        self.book.apply_swap(r.sqrt_price_x96, r.tick)

    def mint(self, block: int, log: int, owner: str, lo: int, hi: int) -> None:
        self.pool.append(ev(block, log, "mint", owner=owner, tick_lower=lo, tick_upper=hi,
                            amount=L, amount0=10**9, amount1=10**17))
        self.book.apply_mint(lo, hi, L)

    def burn(self, block: int, log: int, owner: str, lo: int, hi: int) -> None:
        self.pool.append(ev(block, log, "burn", owner=owner, tick_lower=lo, tick_upper=hi,
                            amount=L, amount0=5 * 10**8, amount1=5 * 10**16))
        self.book.apply_burn(lo, hi, L)


def build(collects: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] | None = None) -> tuple[World, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    w = World()
    w.mint(6, 0, NPM_ADDRESS, T - 20, T + 20)
    w.npm += [ev(6, 1, "transfer", token_id=1, from_owner=ZERO, to_owner=WALLET),
              ev(6, 2, "increase", token_id=1, liquidity=L, amount0=10**9, amount1=10**17)]
    w.swap(7, 0, 10**9)
    w.mint(9, 0, J, T - 10, T + 10)
    w.swap(9, 1, 2 * 10**9)
    w.burn(9, 2, J, T - 10, T + 10)
    w.burn(10, 0, NPM_ADDRESS, T - 20, T + 20)
    w.npm.append(ev(10, 1, "decrease", token_id=1, liquidity=L, amount0=5 * 10**8,
                    amount1=5 * 10**16))
    if collects is not None:
        j0, j1 = collects["J"]
        w.pool.append(ev(9, 3, "collect", owner=J, tick_lower=T - 10, tick_upper=T + 10,
                         amount=0, amount0=j0, amount1=j1))
        n0, n1 = collects["nft"]
        w.pool.append(ev(10, 2, "collect", owner=NPM_ADDRESS, tick_lower=T - 20,
                         tick_upper=T + 20, amount=0, amount0=n0, amount1=n1))
        w.npm.append(ev(10, 3, "npm_collect", token_id=1, recipient=WALLET, amount0=n0,
                        amount1=n1))
    w.swap(11, 0, 10**9)
    w.swap(12, 0, 10**9)
    return w, w.pool


def frames(w: World) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    pool = pl.DataFrame(w.pool, schema=EVENT_SCHEMA).sort("block_number", "log_index")
    npm = pl.DataFrame(w.npm, schema=NPM_SCHEMA).sort("block_number", "log_index")
    return pool, npm


def aligned_for(events: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    swaps = events.filter(pl.col("event") == "swap")
    p = [sqrt_price_x96_to_eth_price(s) for s in swaps["sqrt_price_x96"].to_list()]
    return swaps.select(
        "block_number", "log_index", "block_timestamp",
        t0_us=pl.col("block_timestamp").dt.epoch("us"),
        p_cex_0=pl.Series(p), p_cex_12=pl.Series(p),
        flow_label=pl.lit("searcher"),
        markout_usd_0=-(pl.col("amount0").cast(pl.Float64) / 1e6
                        + pl.col("amount1").cast(pl.Float64) / 1e18 * pl.Series(p)),
        protocol_fee_usd=pl.lit(0.0),
    )


def snapshot_frames(book: TickBook) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame, dict[str, int]]:
    ticks = pl.DataFrame(
        {"tick": [T - 10, T + 10], "liquidity_gross": [L, L], "liquidity_net": [L, -L]},
        schema={"tick": pl.Int64, "liquidity_gross": pl.Int128, "liquidity_net": pl.Int128},
    )
    fees = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"block": SNAP, "kind": "global", "tick": None, "fee_growth0": u256_hex(0),
          "fee_growth1": u256_hex(0)}]
        + [{"block": SNAP, "kind": "tick", "tick": t, "fee_growth0": u256_hex(0),
            "fee_growth1": u256_hex(0)} for t in (T - 10, T + 10)],
        schema=FEE_SCHEMA,
    )
    meta = {"block": SNAP, "tick": T, "sqrt_price_x96": sqrt_at(T), "liquidity": L,
            "fee_protocol": 0, "timestamp": int(ts(SNAP).timestamp())}
    return ticks, fees, meta


def oracle(events: pl.DataFrame, npm: pl.DataFrame) -> tuple[Ledger, Any]:
    data = build_positions(events, npm)
    ticks, fees, meta = snapshot_frames(snapshot_book())
    start = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"block": SNAP, "kind": "pool_key", "owner": D, "tick_lower": T - 10,
          "tick_upper": T + 10, "token_id": None, "found": True, "liquidity": L,
          "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(0), "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(0),
          "tokens_owed0": 0, "tokens_owed1": 0}],
        schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA,
    )
    state = PoolState(TickBook.from_snapshot(ticks, meta),
                      FeeAccumulators.from_rows(fees, [T - 10, T + 10]), 0)
    ledger = Ledger(data, start, SNAP)
    window = events.filter(pl.col("block_number") > SNAP)
    run = replay_window(window, state, contexts_from_aligned(aligned_for(window)),
                        {7: [T - 10, T + 10, T - 20, T + 20]}, ledger,
                        npm.filter(pl.col("block_number") > SNAP))
    return ledger, run


def world_inputs() -> LpInputs:
    first, _ = build()
    ledger, _ = oracle(*frames(first))
    j = ledger.keys[(J, T - 10, T + 10)]
    tok = ledger.token_mirror(1)
    assert tok is not None
    w, _ = build({"J": (j.owed0, j.owed1), "nft": (tok.owed0, tok.owed1)})
    pool, npm = frames(w)
    ledger, run = oracle(pool, npm)
    ticks, fees, meta = snapshot_frames(snapshot_book())
    window = pool.filter(pl.col("block_number") > SNAP)

    def state_row(kind: str, owner: str | None, lo: int | None, hi: int | None,
                  token: int | None, m: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"block": 12, "kind": kind, "owner": owner, "tick_lower": lo, "tick_upper": hi,
                "token_id": token, "found": True, "liquidity": m.liquidity,
                "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(m.last0),
                "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(m.last1),
                "tokens_owed0": m.owed0, "tokens_owed1": m.owed1}

    end = pl.DataFrame(
        [state_row("pool_key", o, lo, hi, None, m) for (o, lo, hi), m in ledger.keys.items()]
        + [{"block": 12, "kind": "npm_token", "token_id": 1, "found": False}],
        schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA,
    )
    cap = run.captures[7]
    sample = pl.DataFrame(
        [{"block": 7, "kind": "global", "tick": None, "fee_growth0": u256_hex(cap["global0"]),
          "fee_growth1": u256_hex(cap["global1"])}]
        + [{"block": 7, "kind": "tick", "tick": t, "fee_growth0": u256_hex(o0),
            "fee_growth1": u256_hex(o1)} for t, (o0, o1) in cap["ticks"].items()],
        schema=FEE_SCHEMA,
    )
    book = TickBook.from_snapshot(ticks, meta)
    rows = []
    blocks = list(range(SNAP + 1, 13))
    for b in iter_block_starts(window, book, 0, blocks[0], blocks[-1]):
        rows.append({"block_number": b, "sqrt_price_x96": book.sqrt_price_x96,
                     "liquidity": book.liquidity})
    for i in range(len(blocks)):
        rows[i]["end_sqrt_price_x96"] = rows[i + 1]["sqrt_price_x96"]
    state_rows = rows[: len(blocks)]
    blocks_state = pl.DataFrame(
        state_rows,
        schema={"block_number": pl.Int64, "sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128, "liquidity": pl.Int128,
                "end_sqrt_price_x96": pl.Int128},
    ).with_columns(
        ts_us=pl.Series([int(ts(b).timestamp()) * US for b in blocks]),
        p_cex=pl.Series([sqrt_price_x96_to_eth_price(r["sqrt_price_x96"]) for r in state_rows]),
    )
    t_lo = int(ts(SNAP).timestamp()) * US - 60 * US
    t_hi = int(ts(12).timestamp()) * US + 3_700 * US
    seconds = list(range(t_lo, t_hi, US))
    trades = pl.DataFrame({
        "agg_trade_id": list(range(len(seconds))), "ts_us": seconds,
        "price": [2000.0 * (1 + 0.0005 * ((i % 7) - 3) / 3) for i in range(len(seconds))],
        "qty": [1.0] * len(seconds), "is_buyer_maker": [True] * len(seconds),
    })
    minute = pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": [int((T0 - timedelta(days=2)).timestamp()) * US],
                           "close": [1900.0]})
    return LpInputs(
        events=window, ticks=ticks, meta=meta, aligned=aligned_for(window),
        blocks_state=blocks_state,
        swap_fees=build_swap_fees(window, TickBook.from_snapshot(ticks, meta), 0),
        trades=trades, pool_history=pool, npm_history=npm, snapshot_fees=fees,
        sample_fees=sample,
        start_positions=pl.DataFrame(
            [{"block": SNAP, "kind": "pool_key", "owner": D, "tick_lower": T - 10,
              "tick_upper": T + 10, "token_id": None, "found": True, "liquidity": L,
              "fee_growth_inside0_last": u256_hex(0), "fee_growth_inside1_last": u256_hex(0),
              "tokens_owed0": 0, "tokens_owed1": 0}],
            schema=POSITION_STATE_SCHEMA,
        ),
        end_positions=end,
        owner_code=pl.DataFrame({"address": [WALLET, D, J], "has_code": [False, True, True]}),
        minute_klines=minute,
        lp_labels=pl.DataFrame(schema={c: pl.String for c in
                                       ("address", "name", "category", "source_url", "notes")}),
        window_end_us=t_hi,
    )
```

Create `tests/test_lp_analyze.py`:

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

import polars as pl

from lpworld import world_inputs
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp import analyze as lp
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import check_l6


def test_lp_analysis_end_to_end(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    inp = world_inputs()
    results, processed = tmp_path / "results", tmp_path / "processed"
    (results).mkdir()
    (results / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps({"swaps": 4}))
    status = lp.lp_analysis(inp, results, processed, {"git_commit": "test"}, end_us=inp.window_end_us)
    assert status == "PASS", (results / "lp_checks.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for name in ("lp_pnl.md", "lvr_daily.csv", "vol_regimes.md", "lp_groups.csv", "lp_groups.md",
                 "positions.csv", "lifetime.md"):
        assert (results / name).exists(), name
    for name in lp.LP_FIGURES.values():
        assert (results / "figures" / name).exists(), name
    summary = json.loads((results / "summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert summary["swaps"] == 4  # sub-project 2 keys are kept
    assert summary["lp"]["git_commit"] == "test"
    assert summary["lp"]["positions_window_active"] == 3
    positions = pl.read_csv(results / "positions.csv")
    assert set(positions["lp_type"].to_list()) == {"jit", "nft_wallet", "direct_other"}
    for name in ("swap_steps.parquet", "positions.parquet", "position_flows.parquet",
                 "position_days.parquet", "lp_blocks.parquet"):
        assert (processed / name).exists(), name


def test_failed_gate_writes_no_lp_results(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    inp = world_inputs()
    inp.end_positions = inp.end_positions.with_columns(tokens_owed0=pl.lit(1, dtype=pl.Int128))
    results, processed = tmp_path / "results", tmp_path / "processed"
    status = lp.lp_analysis(inp, results, processed, {"git_commit": "test"}, end_us=inp.window_end_us)
    assert status == "FAIL"
    assert "L3" in (results / "lp_checks.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert not (results / "lp_pnl.md").exists() and not processed.exists()


def test_l6_tolerance_and_identity() -> None:
    steps = pl.DataFrame({"zero_for_one": [True, True], "lp_fee": [10, 10], "accrued": [True, True],
                          "lvr_0": [1.0, 2.0], "block_number": [1, 2], "log_index": [0, 0]},
                         schema={"zero_for_one": pl.Boolean, "lp_fee": pl.Int128,
                                 "accrued": pl.Boolean, "lvr_0": pl.Float64,
                                 "block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64})
    accounts = pl.DataFrame({"fees_raw0": [19], "fees_raw1": [0], "lvr_0": [3.0]},
                            schema={"fees_raw0": pl.Int128, "fees_raw1": pl.Int128,
                                    "lvr_0": pl.Float64})
    aligned = pl.DataFrame({"block_number": [1, 2], "log_index": [0, 0],
                            "markout_usd_0": [0.5, 1.5], "p_cex_0": [2000.0, 2000.0]})
    swap_fees = pl.DataFrame({"block_number": [1, 2], "log_index": [0, 0],
                              "fee_token": ["USDC", "USDC"], "fee_raw": [500_000, 500_000]},
                             schema={"block_number": pl.Int64, "log_index": pl.Int64,
                                     "fee_token": pl.String, "fee_raw": pl.Int128})
    assert check_l6(steps, accounts, aligned, swap_fees, 0).passed
    over = accounts.with_columns(fees_raw0=pl.lit(21, dtype=pl.Int128))
    assert not check_l6(steps, over, aligned, swap_fees, 0).passed  # more than the pool paid
```

```python
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.lp import figures


def test_lp_figures_render_even_when_empty(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    empty = pl.DataFrame()
    figures.fees_vs_lvr_daily(empty, tmp_path / "a.png")
    figures.measured_vs_theory(empty, tmp_path / "b.png")
    figures.regime_chart(empty, tmp_path / "c.png")
    figures.group_chart(empty, tmp_path / "d.png")
    figures.lifetime_chart(empty, empty, tmp_path / "e.png")
    assert len(list(tmp_path.glob("*.png"))) == 5


def test_daily_chart_with_data(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    daily = pl.DataFrame({"day": [date(2026, 7, 1), date(2026, 7, 2)], "fees_usd": [1.0, 2.0],
                          "lvr_0": [0.5, 3.0], "lvr_12": [0.4, 2.5], "lvr_0_searcher": [0.4, 2.0],
                          "theory_1s": [0.6, 2.2], "theory_arb": [0.3, 1.1]})
    figures.fees_vs_lvr_daily(daily, tmp_path / "a.png")
    figures.measured_vs_theory(daily, tmp_path / "b.png")
    assert (tmp_path / "a.png").stat().st_size > 0
```

`lp/pnl.py` (append):

```python
def position_spans(
    positions: pl.DataFrame,
    accounts: pl.DataFrame,
    flows_window: pl.DataFrame,
    start: Edge,
    end: Edge,
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """[t_start, t_exit) of every active position inside the window."""
    f = flows_window.sort("block_number", "log_index")
    first_in = f.filter(pl.col("liquidity_delta") > 0).group_by("position_id").agg(
        t_first=pl.col("ts_us").first()
    )
    last_out = f.filter(pl.col("liquidity_delta") < 0).group_by("position_id").agg(
        t_last=pl.col("ts_us").last()
    )
    return (
        accounts.filter(pl.col("active"))
        .select("position_id", "liquidity_start", "liquidity_end")
        .join(positions.select("position_id", "tick_lower", "tick_upper"), on="position_id")
        .join(first_in, on="position_id", how="left")
        .join(last_out, on="position_id", how="left")
        .select(
            "position_id",
            "tick_lower",
            "tick_upper",
            t_start_us=pl.when(pl.col("liquidity_start") > 0)
            .then(pl.lit(start.ts_us))
            .otherwise(pl.col("t_first")),
            t_exit_us=pl.when(pl.col("liquidity_end") > 0)
            .then(pl.lit(end.ts_us))
            .otherwise(pl.col("t_last")),
        )
    )


def liquidity_path(accounts: pl.DataFrame, flows_window: pl.DataFrame, start: Edge) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Each position's liquidity after every change, from its window-start liquidity."""
    base = accounts.select(
        "position_id", ts_us=pl.lit(start.ts_us), liquidity=pl.col("liquidity_start")
    )
    changes = (
        flows_window.sort("block_number", "log_index")
        .join(accounts.select("position_id", "liquidity_start"), on="position_id")
        .with_columns(
            liquidity=pl.col("liquidity_start")
            + pl.col("liquidity_delta").cum_sum().over("position_id")
        )
        .select("position_id", "ts_us", "liquidity")
    )
    return pl.concat([base, changes]).with_columns(pl.col("ts_us").cast(pl.Int64))
```
