```python
L6_REL_TOL = 1e-9


def check_l6(
    steps: pl.DataFrame,
    accounts: pl.DataFrame,
    aligned: pl.DataFrame,
    swap_fees: pl.DataFrame,
    n_window_flows: int,
) -> CheckResult:
    """Accounting identities (spec L6, plan Ruling 13)."""
    accrued = steps.filter(pl.col("accrued"))
    pool0 = int(accrued.filter(pl.col("zero_for_one"))["lp_fee"].sum())
    pool1 = int(accrued.filter(~pl.col("zero_for_one"))["lp_fee"].sum())
    pos0, pos1 = int(accounts["fees_raw0"].sum()), int(accounts["fees_raw1"].sum())
    tol = accrued.height + n_window_flows + 2 * accounts.height
    fees_ok = 0 <= pool0 - pos0 <= tol and 0 <= pool1 - pos1 <= tol
    pool_lvr = float(accrued["lvr_0"].sum())
    pos_lvr = float(accounts["lvr_0"].sum())
    lvr_ok = abs(pool_lvr - pos_lvr) <= L6_REL_TOL * max(1.0, abs(pool_lvr))
    fee_usd = (
        aligned.join(swap_fees, on=["block_number", "log_index"], how="left")
        .select(
            pl.when(pl.col("fee_token") == "USDC")
            .then(pl.col("fee_raw").cast(pl.Float64) / 1e6)
            .when(pl.col("fee_token") == "WETH")
            .then(pl.col("fee_raw").cast(pl.Float64) / 1e18 * pl.col("p_cex_0"))
            .otherwise(0.0)
            .alias("fee_usd"),
            "markout_usd_0",
        )
    )
    identity = float((fee_usd["fee_usd"] + fee_usd["markout_usd_0"]).sum())
    all_lvr = float(steps["lvr_0"].sum())
    identity_ok = abs(all_lvr - identity) <= L6_REL_TOL * max(1.0, abs(all_lvr))
    return CheckResult(
        "L6 accounting identities",
        fees_ok and lvr_ok and identity_ok and steps.height > 0,
        {
            "pool_lp_fees_raw": [pool0, pool1],
            "position_fees_raw": [pos0, pos1],
            "fee_shortfall_tolerance": tol,
            "pool_lvr_0_usd": pool_lvr,
            "position_lvr_0_usd": pos_lvr,
            "markout_identity_lvr_usd": identity,
            "all_steps_lvr_0_usd": all_lvr,
        },
    )
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/figures.py`:

```python
"""Charts 7-11 (spec section 10). Every chart renders even from empty input."""

from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402
import polars as pl  # noqa: E402

from uniswap_lp_economics.figures import floats, no_data, save_figure  # noqa: E402


def fees_vs_lvr_daily(daily: pl.DataFrame, path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 7: daily LP fees vs measured LVR."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
    if daily.height:
        x = daily["day"].to_list()
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["fees_usd"])], label="LP fees")
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["lvr_0"])], label="LVR (0 s)")
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["lvr_12"])], ls="--", label="LVR (12 s)")
        ax.legend()
        fig.autofmt_xdate()
    else:
        no_data(ax)
    ax.axhline(0.0, color="grey", lw=0.8)
    ax.set_ylabel("USD thousands per day")
    ax.set_title("Do fees cover LVR? Daily LP fees vs measured LVR")
    save_figure(fig, path)


def measured_vs_theory(daily: pl.DataFrame, path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 8: measured arbitrage LVR vs theory, with and without the P_trade adjustment."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
    if daily.height:
        x = daily["day"].to_list()
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["lvr_0_searcher"])], label="measured (searchers)")
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["theory_1s"])], label="theory: sigma^2/8")
        ax.plot(x, [v / 1e3 for v in floats(daily["theory_arb"])], label="theory x P_trade")
        ax.legend()
        fig.autofmt_xdate()
    else:
        no_data(ax)
    ax.set_ylabel("USD thousands per day")
    ax.set_title("Measured vs theoretical LVR")
    save_figure(fig, path)


def regime_chart(summary: pl.DataFrame, path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 9: fees - LVR by volatility tercile."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
    if summary.height and "fees_minus_lvr_usd" in summary.columns:
        ax.bar(summary["regime"].to_list(), [v / 1e3 for v in floats(summary["fees_minus_lvr_usd"])],
               color="tab:blue")
    else:
        no_data(ax)
    ax.axhline(0.0, color="grey", lw=0.8)
    ax.set_ylabel("fees - LVR, USD thousands")
    ax.set_title("LP result by volatility regime (daily realised volatility terciles)")
    save_figure(fig, path)


def group_chart(groups: pl.DataFrame, path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 10: bp of position value per day by LP type and by range width, with 95% intervals."""
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(13, 5))
    for ax, grouping in zip(axes, ("lp_type", "width_bucket"), strict=True):
        g = groups.filter(pl.col("grouping") == grouping) if groups.height else groups
        if g.height:
            y = np.array(floats(g["bp_per_day"]))
            lo = y - np.array(floats(g["ci_lo_bp"]))
            hi = np.array(floats(g["ci_hi_bp"])) - y
            ax.bar(g["group"].to_list(), y, yerr=[lo, hi], color="tab:green", capsize=3)
            ax.tick_params(axis="x", rotation=30)
        else:
            no_data(ax)
        ax.axhline(0.0, color="grey", lw=0.8)
        ax.set_title(f"P&L vs rebalancing by {grouping.replace('_', ' ')}")
        ax.set_ylabel("bp of position value per day")
    save_figure(fig, path)


def lifetime_chart(by_width: pl.DataFrame, by_life: pl.DataFrame, path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 11: share of window-active positions that beat holding over their lifetime."""
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12, 5))
    for ax, df, col in ((axes[0], by_width, "width_bucket"), (axes[1], by_life, "life_bucket")):
        if df.height:
            ax.bar(df[col].to_list(), floats(df["share_beat_hold"]), color="tab:purple")
            ax.set_ylim(0, 1)
            ax.tick_params(axis="x", rotation=30)
        else:
            no_data(ax)
        ax.axhline(0.5, color="grey", lw=0.8, ls="--")
        ax.set_title(f"Beat holding, by {col.replace('_', ' ')}")
        ax.set_ylabel("share of positions")
    save_figure(fig, path)
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/analyze.py`:

```python
"""`ulpe lp`: validated data -> LP results (checks L1-L7, tables, charts, summary.json["lp"])."""

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics import align, config, report, validate
from uniswap_lp_economics.build import load_snapshot_meta
from uniswap_lp_economics.data import binance, chain_state, history
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import read_exact, write_exact, write_text_atomic
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp import figures, lifetime, pnl, theory
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.checks import (
    check_l1, check_l2, check_l3, check_l4, check_l5, check_l6, check_l7,
)
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.engine import contexts_from_aligned, replay_window
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.ledger import Ledger
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.positions import build_positions, liquidity_at
from uniswap_lp_economics.lp.state import FeeAccumulators, PoolState
from uniswap_lp_economics.tickbook import TickBook
from uniswap_lp_economics.v3math import Q96

LP_FIGURES = {
    "daily": "7_lp_fees_vs_lvr_daily.png",
    "theory": "8_lvr_measured_vs_theory.png",
    "regimes": "9_lvr_by_volatility_regime.png",
    "groups": "10_lp_pnl_by_group.png",
    "lifetime": "11_lifetime_beat_holding.png",
}
GROUPINGS = (
    ("lp_type", pnl.LP_TYPES),
    ("width_bucket", pnl.WIDTH_BUCKETS),
    ("holding", pnl.HOLD_BUCKETS),
    ("cohort", pnl.COHORTS),
)
PNL_NOTE = (
    "P&L against rebalancing = fees - LVR (0 s) - LP gas; against holding = everything the LP got "
    "out minus everything put in, at the exit price. bp_per_day = P&L / (hourly time-weighted "
    "position value x days). Intervals: 95% bootstrap over positions (1,000 resamples, seed 42)."
)


@dataclass
class LpInputs:
    events: pl.DataFrame
    ticks: pl.DataFrame
    meta: dict[str, int]
    aligned: pl.DataFrame
    blocks_state: pl.DataFrame
    swap_fees: pl.DataFrame
    trades: pl.DataFrame
    pool_history: pl.DataFrame
    npm_history: pl.DataFrame
    snapshot_fees: pl.DataFrame
    sample_fees: pl.DataFrame
    start_positions: pl.DataFrame
    end_positions: pl.DataFrame
    owner_code: pl.DataFrame
    minute_klines: pl.DataFrame
    lp_labels: pl.DataFrame
    window_end_us: int = 0


def _need(path: Path, command: str) -> Path:
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{path} missing; run `ulpe {command}` first")
    return path


def load_lp_inputs(raw: Path, interim: Path, processed: Path, lp_labels_csv: Path) -> LpInputs:
    cs = raw / chain_state.CHAIN_DIR
    pool_history, npm_history = history.load_history(raw)
    return LpInputs(
        events=read_exact(_need(interim / "events.parquet", "build")),
        ticks=read_exact(_need(raw / "tickbook_snapshot.parquet", "fetch-snapshot")),
        meta=load_snapshot_meta(raw),
        aligned=read_exact(_need(processed / "aligned.parquet", "analyze")),
        blocks_state=read_exact(_need(processed / "blocks_state.parquet", "analyze")),
        swap_fees=read_exact(_need(processed / "swap_fees.parquet", "analyze")),
        trades=pl.read_parquet(_need(interim / "cex_ethusdc.parquet", "build")),
        pool_history=pool_history,
        npm_history=npm_history,
        snapshot_fees=read_exact(_need(cs / chain_state.SNAPSHOT_FEES, "fetch-chain-state")),
        sample_fees=read_exact(_need(cs / chain_state.SAMPLE_FEES, "fetch-chain-state")),
        start_positions=read_exact(_need(cs / chain_state.START_POSITIONS, "fetch-chain-state")),
        end_positions=read_exact(_need(cs / chain_state.END_POSITIONS, "fetch-chain-state")),
        owner_code=pl.read_parquet(_need(cs / chain_state.OWNER_CODE, "fetch-chain-state")),
        minute_klines=binance.load_minute_klines(raw),
        lp_labels=pnl.load_lp_labels(lp_labels_csv),
        window_end_us=align.window_end_us(),
    )


def lp_analysis(
    inp: LpInputs,
    results_dir: Path,
    processed_dir: Path,
    provenance: dict[str, Any],
    end_us: int | None = None,
) -> str:
    end_us = align.window_end_us() if end_us is None else end_us
    snap = int(inp.meta["block"])
    data = build_positions(inp.pool_history, inp.npm_history)
    state = PoolState(
        TickBook.from_snapshot(inp.ticks, inp.meta),
        FeeAccumulators.from_rows(inp.snapshot_fees, inp.ticks["tick"].to_list()),
        int(inp.meta["fee_protocol"]),
    )
    ledger = Ledger(data, inp.start_positions, snap)
    capture = {
        int(b): g.filter(pl.col("kind") == "tick")["tick"].to_list()
        for (b,), g in inp.sample_fees.partition_by("block", as_dict=True).items()
    }
    window_npm = inp.npm_history.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snap)
    run = replay_window(
        inp.events, state, contexts_from_aligned(inp.aligned), capture, ledger, window_npm
    )
    accounts = ledger.accounts_frame()
    flows_window = data.flows.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snap)
    results = [
        check_l1(inp.swap_fees),
        check_l2(run.captures, inp.sample_fees),
        check_l3(ledger, inp.end_positions),
        check_l4(ledger, inp.end_positions, snap),
        check_l5(liquidity_at(data, snap), inp.ticks, inp.meta),
        check_l6(run.steps, accounts, inp.aligned, inp.swap_fees, flows_window.height),
        check_l7(inp.events, inp.pool_history.filter(pl.col("block_number") > snap), data),
    ]
    status = validate.write_report(
        results, results_dir, provenance, stem="lp_checks", title="LP analysis checks"
    )
    for r in results:
        print(f"{'PASS' if r.passed else 'FAIL'}  {r.name}: {r.summary}")
    if status != "PASS":
        return status

    bs = inp.blocks_state.sort("block_number")
    prices = {
        b: (t, p) for b, t, p in bs.select("block_number", "ts_us", "p_cex").iter_rows()
    }
    first, last = bs.row(0, named=True), bs.row(-1, named=True)
    start = pnl.Edge(first["block_number"], first["ts_us"], int(inp.meta["sqrt_price_x96"]),
                     first["p_cex"])
    end = pnl.Edge(last["block_number"], last["ts_us"], int(last["end_sqrt_price_x96"]),
                   last["p_cex"])
    gas = pnl.gas_by_position(flows_window, inp.events, prices)
    hours = list(range(start.ts_us, end.ts_us, pnl.HOUR_US))
    grid = (
        pl.DataFrame({"ts_us": hours}, schema={"ts_us": pl.Int64})
        .join_asof(bs.select("ts_us", "sqrt_price_x96", "p_cex"), on="ts_us", strategy="backward")
        .select("ts_us", sqrt=pl.col("sqrt_price_x96").cast(pl.Float64) / Q96, p_cex="p_cex")
    )
    spans = pnl.position_spans(data.positions, accounts, flows_window, start, end)
    twv = dict(
        pnl.time_weighted_value(pnl.liquidity_path(accounts, flows_window, start), spans, grid)
        .iter_rows()
    )
    has_code = dict(inp.owner_code.select("address", "has_code").iter_rows())
    positions = pnl.window_pnl(
        data.positions, accounts, flows_window, prices, gas, twv,
        set(inp.lp_labels["address"].to_list()), has_code, start, end,
    )
    groups = pl.concat(
        [
            pnl.group_table(positions, by, order)
            .rename({by: "group"})
            .with_columns(grouping=pl.lit(by))
            for by, order in GROUPINGS
        ],
        how="diagonal",
    ).select("grouping", "group", pl.exclude("grouping", "group"))

    ref = align.reference_table(inp.trades)
    grid_1s = theory.price_grid(ref, start.ts_us, end.ts_us, 1, end_us)
    grid_1m = theory.price_grid(ref, start.ts_us, end.ts_us, 60, end_us)
    int_1s = theory.interval_theory(grid_1s, bs)
    int_1m = theory.interval_theory(grid_1m, bs)
    daily = theory.measured_daily(run.steps, inp.aligned).join(
        theory.daily_theory(int_1s, int_1m), on="day", how="left"
    )
    daily, regime_summary = theory.regimes(daily)
    days = ledger.days_frame()
    regime_groups = theory.regime_by_group(days, positions.select("position_id", "lp_type"), daily)

    life = lifetime.lifetime_table(
        data.positions, data.flows, accounts, positions["position_id"].to_list(), prices,
        align.kline_reference_table(inp.minute_klines, bar_s=60), start.ts_us, end,
    )
    life_overall, life_width, life_bucket = lifetime.lifetime_summary(life)

    steps = run.steps
    searcher = steps.filter(pl.col("flow_label") == "searcher")
    totals = {
        "lp_fees_usd": float(steps["fee_usd"].sum()),
        "protocol_fees_usd": float(inp.aligned["protocol_fee_usd"].sum()),
        "lvr_0_usd": float(steps["lvr_0"].sum()),
        "lvr_12_usd": float(steps["lvr_12"].sum()),
        "lvr_0_searcher_usd": float(searcher["lvr_0"].sum()),
        "lp_fees_searcher_usd": float(searcher["fee_usd"].sum()),
        "theory_1s_usd": float(daily["theory_1s"].sum()),
        "theory_1m_usd": float(daily["theory_1m"].sum()),
        "theory_arb_usd": float(daily["theory_arb"].sum()),
        "theory_arb_fees_usd": float(daily["theory_arb_fees"].sum()),
        "positions_window_active": positions.height,
        "pnl_rebal_total_usd": float(positions["pnl_rebal_usd"].sum()),
        "pnl_hold_total_usd": float(positions["pnl_hold_usd"].sum()),
        "share_positions_beat_hold_window": float((positions["pnl_hold_usd"] > 0).mean())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        if positions.height
        else None,
        "zero_liquidity_steps": run.zero_liquidity_steps,
        "steps_without_12s_reference": run.null_12s_steps,
    }
    totals["fees_minus_lvr_usd"] = totals["lp_fees_usd"] - totals["lvr_0_usd"]

    processed_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    write_exact(steps, processed_dir / "swap_steps.parquet")
    write_exact(positions, processed_dir / "positions.parquet")
    write_exact(data.flows, processed_dir / "position_flows.parquet")
    write_exact(days, processed_dir / "position_days.parquet")
    write_exact(
        int_1s.group_by("block_number").agg(pl.col("r2").sum(), pl.col("lvr_theory").sum()),
        processed_dir / "lp_blocks.parquet",
    )

    out = results_dir
    report.write_markdown(
        out, "lp_pnl", "LP P&L: fees vs LVR",
        [("Window totals", report.metrics_table(totals)),
         ("By LP type", groups.filter(pl.col("grouping") == "lp_type"))],
        notes=[PNL_NOTE],
    )
    report.write_table(daily, out, "lvr_daily", "Daily fees, LVR and theory")
    report.write_markdown(
        out, "vol_regimes", "Fees vs LVR by volatility regime",
        [("By regime", regime_summary), ("By regime and LP type", regime_groups)],
    )
    report.write_table(groups, out, "lp_groups", "LP P&L by group", [PNL_NOTE])
    write_text_atomic(out / "positions.csv", positions.write_csv())
    report.write_markdown(
        out, "lifetime", "Lifetime P&L against holding (window-active positions)",
        [("Overall", report.metrics_table(life_overall)), ("By range width", life_width),
         ("By lifetime", life_bucket)],
    )
    figs = out / "figures"
    figures.fees_vs_lvr_daily(daily, figs / LP_FIGURES["daily"])
    figures.measured_vs_theory(daily, figs / LP_FIGURES["theory"])
    figures.regime_chart(regime_summary, figs / LP_FIGURES["regimes"])
    figures.group_chart(groups, figs / LP_FIGURES["groups"])
    figures.lifetime_chart(life_width, life_bucket, figs / LP_FIGURES["lifetime"])

    summary_path = out / "summary.json"
    summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if summary_path.exists() else {}
    summary["lp"] = {
        **provenance,
        **totals,
        "regimes": regime_summary.to_dicts(),
        "groups": groups.to_dicts(),
        "lifetime": life_overall,
        "checks": {r.name: r.summary for r in results},
    }
    report.write_summary(summary, out)
    return status


def run_lp() -> str:
    validate.require_validated()
    inp = load_lp_inputs(config.RAW_DIR, config.INTERIM_DIR, config.PROCESSED_DIR,
                         config.LP_LABELS_CSV)
    provenance = {
        "generated_utc": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "git_commit": validate.git_commit(),
    }
    return lp_analysis(inp, config.RESULTS_DIR, config.PROCESSED_DIR, provenance)
```

`cli.py`: `from uniswap_lp_economics.lp import analyze as lp_analysis_module`, register `sub.add_parser("lp", help="LP P&L and LVR: checks L1-L7, results/ (sub-project 3)")`
and dispatch like `analyze`:

```python
    elif args.command == "lp":
        try:
            status = lp_analysis_module.run_lp()
        except (FileNotFoundError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as e:
            raise SystemExit(str(e)) from e
        raise SystemExit(0 if status == "PASS" else 1)
```

## Milestone M6 — where LVR goes

### Task 23: Builder revenue and payment data (D5, aggregated) and relay sample (D6)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/builders.py`
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/relays.py`
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/config.py` (`RELAYS`, `N_RELAY_SAMPLES`)
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/cli.py` (`fetch-builders [--dry-run]`, `fetch-relays`)
- Test: `tests/test_builders.py`, `tests/test_relays.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `config.RELAYS` (tuple of relay base URLs), `config.N_RELAY_SAMPLES = 1000`
  - `builders.BUILDERS_DIR = "builders"`, `BLOCK_REVENUE = "block_revenue.parquet"`,
    `TX_DIRECT = "pool_tx_direct_payments.parquet"`, `TX_OTHER_LOGS = "pool_tx_other_logs.parquet"`,
    `MAX_GB_BILLED = 700`
  - `build_block_revenue_query(start, end) -> str`, `build_tx_direct_query(start, end) -> str`,
    `build_tx_other_logs_query(start, end) -> str`
  - `wei_columns(df, cols: list[str]) -> pl.DataFrame` (decimal strings → Int128, nulls → 0)
  - `fetch_builders(raw_dir, start, end, dry_run=False) -> None` (skips files that exist)
  - `load_builders(raw_dir) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]` (FileNotFoundError
    naming `ulpe fetch-builders`)
  - Schemas: block revenue `block_number Int64, miner String, priority_wei Int128, direct_wei Int128,
    last_from String, last_value_wei Int128`; direct payments `tx_hash String, direct_wei Int128`;

Create `tests/test_relays.py`:

```python
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.data import relays

R1, R2 = "https://relay-one.example", "https://relay-two.example"


def transport(answers: dict[str, list[httpx.Response]]) -> httpx.MockTransport:
    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        queue = answers[f"{request.url.scheme}://{request.url.host}"]
        return queue.pop(0) if len(queue) > 1 else queue[0]

    return httpx.MockTransport(handler)


def test_query_block_tries_relays_in_order_and_retries() -> None:
    payload = [{"value": "12345", "proposer_fee_recipient": "0x" + "ab" * 20,
                "builder_pubkey": "0xbeef"}]
    client = httpx.Client(transport=transport({
        R1: [httpx.Response(200, json=[])],
        R2: [httpx.Response(429), httpx.Response(200, json=payload)],
    }))
    row = relays.query_block(client, 7, (R1, R2), backoff_s=0)
    assert row["found"] and row["relay"] == R2 and row["value_wei"] == 12345


def test_query_block_not_found() -> None:
    client = httpx.Client(transport=transport({R1: [httpx.Response(200, json=[])]}))
    row = relays.query_block(client, 7, (R1,), backoff_s=0)
    assert row == {"block_number": 7, "relay": None, "found": False, "value_wei": None,
                   "proposer_fee_recipient": None, "builder_pubkey": None}


def test_fetch_relay_sample_resumes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        calls.append(int(request.url.params["block_number"]))
        return httpx.Response(200, json=[])

    client = httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    path = relays.fetch_relay_sample(tmp_path, 1, 100, n=5, client=client, relay_urls=(R1,))
    assert pl.read_parquet(path).height == 5 and len(calls) == 5
    relays.fetch_relay_sample(tmp_path, 1, 100, n=5, client=client, relay_urls=(R1,))
    assert len(calls) == 5  # nothing re-queried
    assert relays.sample_blocks(1, 100, 5) == relays.sample_blocks(1, 100, 5)
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/builders.py`:

```python
"""Builder revenue and payments in the window (spec D5), aggregated inside BigQuery (plan
Ruling 4): the window has ~110 M transactions, too many to download."""

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import polars as pl
from google.cloud import bigquery

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import GCP_PROJECT, POOL_ADDRESS, USDC_ADDRESS, WETH_ADDRESS
from uniswap_lp_economics.data.bigquery import DATASET
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import write_exact

BUILDERS_DIR = "builders"
BLOCK_REVENUE = "block_revenue.parquet"
TX_DIRECT = "pool_tx_direct_payments.parquet"
TX_OTHER_LOGS = "pool_tx_other_logs.parquet"
MAX_GB_BILLED = 700


def _window(column: str, start: date, end: date) -> str:
    return (
        f"{column} >= TIMESTAMP('{start.isoformat()}') "
        f"AND {column} < TIMESTAMP('{end.isoformat()}')"
    )


def build_block_revenue_query(start: date, end: date) -> str:
    """Per block: priority fees (fee recipient's own transactions excluded), successful trace
    payments to the fee recipient, and the block's last transaction (the proposer payment)."""
    return f"""
WITH b AS (
  SELECT number, miner, base_fee_per_gas FROM `{DATASET}.blocks`
  WHERE {_window("timestamp", start, end)}
),
t AS (
  SELECT block_number, transaction_index, from_address, value, receipt_gas_used,
         receipt_effective_gas_price
  FROM `{DATASET}.transactions` WHERE {_window("block_timestamp", start, end)}
),
pr AS (
  SELECT t.block_number,
         SUM(IF(t.from_address != b.miner,
                CAST(t.receipt_gas_used AS BIGNUMERIC)
                * (t.receipt_effective_gas_price - b.base_fee_per_gas), 0)) AS priority_wei,
         MAX(t.transaction_index) AS last_index
  FROM t JOIN b ON b.number = t.block_number
  GROUP BY t.block_number
),
tr AS (
  SELECT x.block_number, SUM(x.value) AS direct_wei
  FROM `{DATASET}.traces` x JOIN b ON b.number = x.block_number
  WHERE {_window("x.block_timestamp", start, end)} AND x.status = 1 AND x.value > 0
    AND x.to_address = b.miner AND x.from_address != b.miner
  GROUP BY x.block_number
),
last AS (
  SELECT t.block_number, t.from_address AS last_from, t.value AS last_value
  FROM t JOIN pr ON pr.block_number = t.block_number AND pr.last_index = t.transaction_index
)
SELECT b.number AS block_number, b.miner,
       CAST(pr.priority_wei AS STRING) AS priority_wei,
       CAST(tr.direct_wei AS STRING) AS direct_wei,
       last.last_from, CAST(last.last_value AS STRING) AS last_value_wei
FROM b
LEFT JOIN pr ON pr.block_number = b.number
LEFT JOIN tr ON tr.block_number = b.number
LEFT JOIN last ON last.block_number = b.number
ORDER BY block_number
"""


def _pool_txs(start: date, end: date) -> str:
    return f"""pool_txs AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT transaction_hash FROM `{DATASET}.logs`
  WHERE {_window("block_timestamp", start, end)} AND address = '{POOL_ADDRESS}'
)"""


def build_tx_direct_query(start: date, end: date) -> str:
    """Per transaction touching our pool: successful trace payments to the block's fee recipient."""
    return f"""
WITH {_pool_txs(start, end)},
b AS (SELECT number, miner FROM `{DATASET}.blocks` WHERE {_window("timestamp", start, end)})
SELECT x.transaction_hash AS tx_hash, CAST(SUM(x.value) AS STRING) AS direct_wei
FROM `{DATASET}.traces` x
JOIN pool_txs p ON p.transaction_hash = x.transaction_hash
JOIN b ON b.number = x.block_number
WHERE {_window("x.block_timestamp", start, end)} AND x.status = 1 AND x.value > 0
  AND x.to_address = b.miner
GROUP BY x.transaction_hash
"""


def build_tx_other_logs_query(start: date, end: date) -> str:
    """Per transaction touching our pool: logs from anything other than our pool, USDC, WETH
    or the transaction's target (non-zero: the transaction also traded elsewhere)."""
    allowed = ", ".join(f"'{a}'" for a in (POOL_ADDRESS, USDC_ADDRESS, WETH_ADDRESS))
    return f"""
WITH {_pool_txs(start, end)},
t AS (SELECT `hash`, to_address FROM `{DATASET}.transactions`
      WHERE {_window("block_timestamp", start, end)})
SELECT l.transaction_hash AS tx_hash,
       COUNTIF(l.address NOT IN ({allowed}) AND l.address != t.to_address) AS other_logs,
       COUNT(*) AS logs
FROM `{DATASET}.logs` l
JOIN pool_txs p ON p.transaction_hash = l.transaction_hash
JOIN t ON t.`hash` = l.transaction_hash
WHERE {_window("l.block_timestamp", start, end)}
GROUP BY l.transaction_hash
"""


def wei_columns(df: pl.DataFrame, cols: list[str]) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Decimal strings from BigQuery (NUMERIC/BIGNUMERIC) to exact Int128; nulls become 0."""
    return df.with_columns(
        pl.Series(c, [int(Decimal(v)) if v is not None else 0 for v in df[c].to_list()],
                  dtype=pl.Int128)
        for c in cols
    )


def _run(client: bigquery.Client, query: str, dry_run: bool) -> pl.DataFrame | None:
    dry = client.query(query, job_config=bigquery.QueryJobConfig(dry_run=True))
    print(f"  scans {(dry.total_bytes_processed or 0) / 1e9:.1f} GB")
    if dry_run:
        return None
    cfg = bigquery.QueryJobConfig(maximum_bytes_billed=int(MAX_GB_BILLED * 1e9))
    df = pl.from_arrow(client.query(query, job_config=cfg).result().to_arrow())
    assert isinstance(df, pl.DataFrame)
    return df


def fetch_builders(raw_dir: Path, start: date, end: date, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:
    out = raw_dir / BUILDERS_DIR
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    client = bigquery.Client(project=GCP_PROJECT)
    jobs: list[tuple[str, str, list[str], dict[str, Any]]] = [
        (BLOCK_REVENUE, build_block_revenue_query(start, end),
         ["priority_wei", "direct_wei", "last_value_wei"], {"block_number": pl.Int64}),
        (TX_DIRECT, build_tx_direct_query(start, end), ["direct_wei"], {}),
        (TX_OTHER_LOGS, build_tx_other_logs_query(start, end), [],
         {"other_logs": pl.Int64, "logs": pl.Int64}),
    ]
    for name, query, wei, casts in jobs:
        if not dry_run and (out / name).exists():
            print(f"{name} already downloaded, skipping")
            continue
        print(f"{name}:")
        df = _run(client, query, dry_run)
        if df is None:
            continue
        df = wei_columns(df, wei).with_columns(pl.col(c).cast(t) for c, t in casts.items())
        write_exact(df, out / name)
        print(f"  wrote {df.height:,} rows")


def load_builders(raw_dir: Path) -> tuple[pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame, pl.DataFrame]:
    folder = raw_dir / BUILDERS_DIR
    frames = []
    for name in (BLOCK_REVENUE, TX_DIRECT, TX_OTHER_LOGS):
        if not (folder / name).exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{folder / name} missing; run `ulpe fetch-builders` first")
        from uniswap_lp_economics.io import read_exact

        frames.append(read_exact(folder / name))
    return frames[0], frames[1], frames[2]
```

(Move the `read_exact` import to the module top.)

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/data/relays.py`:

```python
"""Relay-reported proposer payments for a random sample of window blocks (spec D6).

Free public endpoints (`proposer_payload_delivered`); used only to cross-check the on-chain
proposer payment (a reported check). Resumable: blocks already in the file are not re-queried.
"""

import random
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx
import polars as pl

from uniswap_lp_economics.config import N_RELAY_SAMPLES, RELAYS
from uniswap_lp_economics.io import write_exact

RELAY_FILE = "relays.parquet"
PATH = "/relay/v1/data/bidtraces/proposer_payload_delivered"
RELAY_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {
    "block_number": pl.Int64,
    "relay": pl.String,
    "found": pl.Boolean,
    "value_wei": pl.Int128,
    "proposer_fee_recipient": pl.String,
    "builder_pubkey": pl.String,
}
_RETRIES = 3


def sample_blocks(first: int, last: int, n: int, seed: int = 42) -> list[int]:
    return sorted(random.Random(seed).sample(range(first, last + 1), n))


def _get(client: httpx.Client, url: str, block: int, backoff_s: float) -> list[Any]:
    for attempt in range(_RETRIES):
        try:
            r = client.get(url, params={"block_number": block}, timeout=30)
        except httpx.RequestError:
            r = None
        if r is not None and r.status_code == 200:
            body = r.json()
            return body if isinstance(body, list) else []
        if r is not None and r.status_code not in (429,) and r.status_code < 500:
            return []
        time.sleep(backoff_s * 2**attempt)
    return []


def query_block(
    client: httpx.Client, block: int, relays: tuple[str, ...] = RELAYS, backoff_s: float = 1.0
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for relay in relays:
        payloads = _get(client, relay + PATH, block, backoff_s)
        if payloads:
            p = payloads[0]
            return {
                "block_number": block,
                "relay": relay,
                "found": True,
                "value_wei": int(p["value"]),
                "proposer_fee_recipient": str(p.get("proposer_fee_recipient", "")).lower(),
                "builder_pubkey": p.get("builder_pubkey"),
            }
    return {"block_number": block, "relay": None, "found": False, "value_wei": None,
            "proposer_fee_recipient": None, "builder_pubkey": None}


def fetch_relay_sample(
    raw_dir: Path,
    first: int,
    last: int,
    n: int = N_RELAY_SAMPLES,
    client: httpx.Client | None = None,
    relay_urls: tuple[str, ...] = RELAYS,
) -> Path:
    out = raw_dir / RELAY_FILE
    raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    done = pl.read_parquet(out) if out.exists() else pl.DataFrame(schema=RELAY_SCHEMA)
    seen = set(done["block_number"].to_list())
    todo = [b for b in sample_blocks(first, last, n) if b not in seen]
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    own = client is None
    http = client or httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True)
    try:
        for i, b in enumerate(todo):
            rows.append(query_block(http, b, relay_urls))
            if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:
                write_exact(pl.concat([done, pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=RELAY_SCHEMA)]), out)
                print(f"  {i + 1}/{len(todo)} blocks")
    finally:
        if own:
            http.close()
    write_exact(pl.concat([done, pl.DataFrame(rows, schema=RELAY_SCHEMA)]), out)
    return out
```

### Task 24: Where LVR goes (`lp/destination.py`), reported checks, figure 12

**Files:**
- Create: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/destination.py`
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/checks.py` (reported checks R1–R5)
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/figures.py` (`destination_waterfall`)
- Modify: `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/analyze.py` (inputs, results, summary)
- Modify: `tests/lpworld.py` (the new inputs)
- Test: `tests/test_destination.py`, `tests/test_lp_analyze.py`, `tests/test_lp_figures.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D5 frames (Task 23), relays (Task 23), sub-project 2 `blocks.parquet`
  (`block_number, miner, base_fee_per_gas`), `transfers.load_bot_transfers` and `flows.top_bots`.
- Produces:
  - `destination.LIMITATION_TEXT` (verbatim spec section 7)
  - `block_split(block_revenue) -> pl.DataFrame` (`block_number, miner, revenue_wei, payment_wei,
    proposer_share, self_built`)
  - `searcher_costs(aligned, events, blocks, tx_direct, tx_other_logs, split) -> pl.DataFrame`
    (one row per searcher swap: `gross_usd, burned_usd, builder_paid_usd, proposer_usd,
    builder_margin_usd, scope, kept_usd`)
  - `destination(steps, aligned, costs) -> dict[str, float]`
  - `lp.checks.check_r1_relays(relays, split)`, `check_r2_traces_vs_alchemy(tx_direct, bot_transfers, blocks)`,
    `check_r3_lp_types(positions)`, `check_r4_transferred(positions_table, active_ids)`,
    `check_r5_null_12s(null_12s_steps, steps)` — all `CheckResult` with `passed=True` (reported)
  - `lp.figures.destination_waterfall(d: dict[str, float], path)`
  - `results/lvr_destination.md`, `figures/12_lvr_destination.png`, `summary.json["lp"]["destination"]`

Chain (USD, 0 s): LVR on searcher steps = LP fees on those steps + protocol fees on those swaps +
searcher gross (their 0 s markout). Gross splits into multi-pool gross (no breakdown) and
single-pool gross = gas burned + builder margin + proposer payment + searcher kept.

```python
)

         "last_from": [MINER, "0x" + "33" * 20], "last_value_wei": [8 * 10**17, 5]},
        schema={"block_number": pl.Int64, "miner": pl.String, "priority_wei": pl.Int128,

    )


def test_block_split_payment_and_self_built() -> None:

    one, two = s.row(0, named=True), s.row(1, named=True)
    assert one["revenue_wei"] == 10**18 and one["payment_wei"] == 8 * 10**17
    assert one["proposer_share"] == pytest.approx(0.8) and not one["self_built"]
    assert two["self_built"] and two["proposer_share"] == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_searcher_costs_and_destination_chain() -> None:
    aligned = pl.DataFrame({
        "block_number": [1, 1], "log_index": [0, 1], "tx_hash": ["0xa", "0xb"],
        "flow_label": ["searcher", "searcher"], "notional_usd": [1000.0, 1000.0],
        "markout_usd_0": [10.0, 5.0], "p_cex_0": [2000.0, 2000.0],
        "protocol_fee_usd": [0.1, 0.1],
    })

                           "receipt_gas_used": [100_000, 100_000],

    blocks = pl.DataFrame({"block_number": [1], "miner": [MINER], "base_fee_per_gas": [10**9]})
    direct = pl.DataFrame({"tx_hash": ["0xa"], "direct_wei": [10**15]},
                          schema={"tx_hash": pl.String, "direct_wei": pl.Int128})
    other = pl.DataFrame({"tx_hash": ["0xa", "0xb"], "other_logs": [0, 2], "logs": [3, 9]})
    costs = searcher_costs(aligned, events, blocks, direct, other, block_split(revenue()))

    assert a["proposer_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.8 * a["builder_paid_usd"])
```

Create `src/uniswap_lp_economics/lp/destination.py`:

```python
"""Where LVR goes (spec section 7): LVR on searcher steps -> LP fees, protocol fees and the
searchers' gross; single-pool gross -> gas burned, builder margin, proposer payment, kept."""

from typing import Any

import polars as pl

LIMITATION_TEXT = (
    "The split of builder payments between builder and proposer uses each block's ratio of proposer "
    "payment to builder revenue; how a builder prices an individual transaction is not observed. "
    "Proposer payments are identified as the block's last transaction from the fee recipient and "
    "cross-checked against relay data on a sample of blocks only."
)
SINGLE, MULTI = "single_pool", "multi_pool"
WEI = 1e18


def block_split(block_revenue: pl.DataFrame) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Builder revenue R_b and proposer payment Q_b per block. Without a payment transaction the
    fee recipient is the proposer (a self-built block) and Q_b = R_b."""
    revenue = pl.col("priority_wei") + pl.col("direct_wei")
    paid = pl.col("last_from") == pl.col("miner")
    return block_revenue.select(
        "block_number",
        "miner",
        revenue_wei=revenue,
        payment_wei=pl.when(paid).then(pl.col("last_value_wei")).otherwise(revenue),
        self_built=~paid.fill_null(False),
    ).with_columns(
        proposer_share=pl.when(pl.col("revenue_wei") > 0)
        .then(pl.col("payment_wei").cast(pl.Float64) / pl.col("revenue_wei").cast(pl.Float64))
        .otherwise(0.0)
    )


def searcher_costs(
    aligned: pl.DataFrame,
    events: pl.DataFrame,
    blocks: pl.DataFrame,
    tx_direct: pl.DataFrame,
    tx_other_logs: pl.DataFrame,
    split: pl.DataFrame,
) -> pl.DataFrame:
    """Per searcher swap: its share (by notional within the transaction) of gas burned and of
    the transaction's payment to the fee recipient, split into proposer and builder margin."""
    tx = events.group_by("tx_hash").agg(
        pl.col("receipt_gas_used").first(), pl.col("receipt_effective_gas_price").first()
    )
    s = (
        aligned.filter(pl.col("flow_label") == "searcher")
        .join(tx, on="tx_hash", how="left")
        .join(blocks.select("block_number", "base_fee_per_gas"), on="block_number", how="left")
        .join(tx_direct, on="tx_hash", how="left")
        .join(tx_other_logs.select("tx_hash", "other_logs"), on="tx_hash", how="left")
        .join(split.select("block_number", "proposer_share"), on="block_number", how="left")
    )
    tx_notional = pl.col("notional_usd").sum().over("tx_hash")
    share = pl.when(tx_notional > 0).then(pl.col("notional_usd") / tx_notional).otherwise(
        1.0 / pl.len().over("tx_hash")
    )
    usd = pl.col("p_cex_0") / WEI * share
    gas = pl.col("receipt_gas_used").cast(pl.Float64)
    base = pl.col("base_fee_per_gas").cast(pl.Float64)
    eff = pl.col("receipt_effective_gas_price").cast(pl.Float64)
    paid = gas * (eff - base) + pl.col("direct_wei").cast(pl.Float64).fill_null(0.0)
    out = s.with_columns(
        gross_usd=pl.col("markout_usd_0"),
        burned_usd=gas * base * usd,
        builder_paid_usd=paid * usd,
        scope=pl.when(pl.col("other_logs") == 0).then(pl.lit(SINGLE)).otherwise(pl.lit(MULTI)),
    ).with_columns(
        proposer_usd=pl.col("builder_paid_usd") * pl.col("proposer_share").fill_null(0.0),
    )
    return out.with_columns(
        builder_margin_usd=pl.col("builder_paid_usd") - pl.col("proposer_usd"),
        kept_usd=pl.col("gross_usd") - pl.col("burned_usd") - pl.col("builder_paid_usd"),
    )


def destination(steps: pl.DataFrame, aligned: pl.DataFrame, costs: pl.DataFrame) -> dict[str, Any]:
    searcher = pl.col("flow_label") == "searcher"
    single = costs.filter(pl.col("scope") == SINGLE)

    def total(df: pl.DataFrame, col: str) -> float:
        return float(df[col].sum()) if df.height else 0.0

    return {
        "lvr_searcher_usd": total(steps.filter(searcher), "lvr_0"),
        "lvr_other_flows_usd": total(steps.filter(~searcher), "lvr_0"),
        "lp_fees_searcher_usd": total(steps.filter(searcher), "fee_usd"),
        "protocol_fees_searcher_usd": total(aligned.filter(searcher), "protocol_fee_usd"),
        "searcher_gross_usd": total(costs, "gross_usd"),
        "multi_pool_gross_usd": total(costs.filter(pl.col("scope") == MULTI), "gross_usd"),
        "single_pool_gross_usd": total(single, "gross_usd"),
        "gas_burned_usd": total(single, "burned_usd"),
        "builder_margin_usd": total(single, "builder_margin_usd"),
        "proposer_usd": total(single, "proposer_usd"),
        "searcher_kept_usd": total(single, "kept_usd"),
    }
```

Append the reported checks to `lp/checks.py`:

```python
def check_r1_relays(relays: pl.DataFrame, split: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    found = relays.filter(pl.col("found")).join(
        split.select("block_number", "payment_wei"), on="block_number", how="inner"
    )
    diff = (found["value_wei"] - found["payment_wei"]).abs()
    exact = int((diff <= 1).sum()) if found.height else 0
    within = (
        int((diff.cast(pl.Float64) <= 0.01 * found["value_wei"].cast(pl.Float64)).sum())
        if found.height
        else 0
    )
    return CheckResult(
        "R1 relay vs on-chain proposer payment (reported)",
        True,
        {"sampled": relays.height, "found_on_a_relay": found.height,
         "match_within_1_wei": exact, "match_within_1pct": within},
    )


def check_r2_traces_vs_alchemy(
    tx_direct: pl.DataFrame, bot_transfers: pl.DataFrame, blocks: pl.DataFrame
) -> CheckResult:
    from uniswap_lp_economics.arbprofit import coinbase_payments

    alchemy = coinbase_payments(bot_transfers, blocks)
    both = alchemy.join(tx_direct, on="tx_hash", how="left")
    same = int((both["coinbase_wei"] == both["direct_wei"].fill_null(0)).sum()) if both.height else 0
    return CheckResult(
        "R2 traces vs Alchemy builder payments, top bots (reported)",
        True,
        {"alchemy_txs_with_payment": alchemy.height, "same_amount_in_traces": same},
    )


def check_r3_lp_types(positions: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    by = positions.group_by("lp_type").agg(positions=pl.len(), fee_usd=pl.col("fee_usd").sum())
    return CheckResult("R3 LP-type coverage (reported)", True, {"by_lp_type": by.to_dicts()})


def check_r4_transferred(positions_table: pl.DataFrame, active_ids: list[str]) -> CheckResult:
    active = positions_table.filter(pl.col("position_id").is_in(active_ids))
    return CheckResult(
        "R4 NFT positions transferred while open (reported)",
        True,
        {"window_active": active.height, "transferred": int(active["transferred"].sum())},
    )


def check_r5_null_12s(null_12s_steps: int, steps: pl.DataFrame) -> CheckResult:
    return CheckResult(
        "R5 steps without a 12 s reference (reported)",
        True,
        {"steps": steps.height, "without_12s_reference": null_12s_steps},
    )
```

Add to `lp/figures.py`:

```python
DESTINATION_STEPS = [
    ("lvr_searcher_usd", "LVR on\nsearcher swaps", "total"),
    ("lp_fees_searcher_usd", "- LP fees", "down"),
    ("protocol_fees_searcher_usd", "- protocol\nfees", "down"),
    ("multi_pool_gross_usd", "- multi-pool\n(no split)", "down"),
    ("gas_burned_usd", "- gas\nburned", "down"),
    ("builder_margin_usd", "- builder\nmargin", "down"),
    ("proposer_usd", "- proposer", "down"),
    ("searcher_kept_usd", "searcher kept\n(upper bound)", "total"),
]


def destination_waterfall(d: dict[str, float], path: Path) -> None:
    """Chart 12: where LVR goes, as a floating waterfall."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 5))
    if not d:
        no_data(ax)
    else:
        level = 0.0
        for i, (key, label, kind) in enumerate(DESTINATION_STEPS):
            v = d.get(key, 0.0) / 1e3
            if kind == "total":
                ax.bar(i, v, color="tab:blue")
                level = v
            else:
                ax.bar(i, -v, bottom=level, color="tab:red")
                level -= v
            ax.annotate(f"{v:,.1f}", (i, max(level, v if kind == "total" else level)),
                        ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=8)
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(DESTINATION_STEPS)), [s[1] for s in DESTINATION_STEPS])
    ax.axhline(0.0, color="grey", lw=0.8)
    ax.set_ylabel("USD thousands")
    ax.set_title("Where LVR goes: fees, gas, builders, proposers, searchers")
    save_figure(fig, path)
```

```python
import sys

import pytest

from uniswap_lp_economics import cli


def test_all_runs_every_step_in_order(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    ran: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_step", lambda name, confirm_cost: ran.append(name) or "PASS")
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", ["ulpe", "all", "--confirm-cost"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as e:
        cli.main()
    assert e.value.code == 0
    assert ran == list(cli.ALL_STEPS)
    assert ran.index("validate") < ran.index("analyze") < ran.index("fetch-history") < ran.index("lp")


def test_all_without_the_flag_stops_at_the_paid_query(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    ran: list[str] = []

    def step(name: str, confirm_cost: bool) -> str:
        ran.append(name)
        if name == "fetch-history" and not confirm_cost:
            raise SystemExit("stage 1 is a paid query; run with --confirm-cost")
        return "PASS"

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_step", step)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", ["ulpe", "all"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="paid query"):
        cli.main()
    assert ran[-1] == "fetch-history"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `cli.py`, first move the bodies of the `fetch-price-history` and `fetch-relays` dispatch
branches (Tasks 20 and 23) into functions `fetch_price_history() -> None` and
`fetch_relays() -> None`, and make those branches call them. Then add the ordered steps and one
dispatcher:

```python
ALL_STEPS = (
    "fetch-logs", "fetch-snapshot", "fetch-cex", "fetch-samples", "build", "validate",
    "fetch-blocks", "fetch-transfers", "analyze", "fetch-chain-state", "fetch-history",
    "fetch-chain-state", "fetch-price-history", "fetch-builders", "fetch-relays", "lp",
)


def run_step(name: str, confirm_cost: bool) -> str:
    """Run one pipeline step; "PASS"/"FAIL" for the gated ones (validate, analyze, lp), else "OK".

    fetch-chain-state appears twice in ALL_STEPS: its position part needs the history."""
    if name == "fetch-logs":
        for start, end in bigquery.month_windows(config.WINDOW_START, config.WINDOW_END):
            if not bigquery.raw_logs_path(start, end).exists():
                bigquery.fetch_pool_logs(start, end)
    elif name == "fetch-snapshot":
        fetch_snapshot()
    elif name == "fetch-cex":
        fetch_cex()
    elif name == "fetch-samples":
        fetch_samples()
    elif name == "build":
        build.build_all(config.RAW_DIR, config.INTERIM_DIR)
    elif name == "validate":
        return validate.run_validation()
    elif name == "fetch-blocks":
        fetch_blocks(dry_run=False)
    elif name == "fetch-transfers":
        fetch_transfers()
    elif name == "analyze":
        return analysis.run_analysis()
    elif name == "fetch-chain-state":
        fetch_chain_state()
    elif name == "fetch-history":
        history.fetch_history(
            config.RAW_DIR, config.POOL_CREATED, config.WINDOW_END, confirm_cost
        )
    elif name == "fetch-price-history":
        fetch_price_history()
    elif name == "fetch-builders":
        builders.fetch_builders(config.RAW_DIR, config.WINDOW_START, config.WINDOW_END)
    elif name == "fetch-relays":
        fetch_relays()
    elif name == "lp":
        return lp_analysis_module.run_lp()
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown pipeline step {name!r}")
    return "OK"
```
